# 101: Chapter 99: The World Is So Big

Dragonite didn't know how to advise Cyclizar, as it had never encountered such a situation before.

All the Dragonites on Dragonite Island were good dragons. Even though some had rough and fiery temperaments, they never showed hostility towards it.

After leaving Dragonite Island, it came to live on a ranch, where it met only good people and good Pokemon.

For a moment, Cyclizar also didn't know what to do.

So the two Dragon-type Pokemon sat together on the grass, gazing at the round moon.

On the other side of the grass, three Gogoats lay sideways, leaning against each other, chewing grass while watching the two dragons in the distance.

"Baa?" (What are they doing?) one of the Gogoats asked.

Another Gogoat expressed confusion, "Baa!"

And so, the three Gogoats continued eating their Grassy Seed while watching Dragonite and Cyclizar.

Meanwhile, inside the wooden house.

A Teddiursa that had been sleeping soundly suddenly felt something and opened its eyes wide.

It lifted its head and saw an "Abra" levitating above, watching it.

"Mmm?" Teddiursa looked a bit confused.

The "Abra" disappeared immediately.

Teddiursa sat up and rubbed its eyes.

It glanced at the sleeping D beside it, then quietly climbed out of bed, stepping into the moonlight outside.

Under the eaves, there came the soft snores of a Butterfree.

The "Abra" was levitating there.

Teddiursa didn't wake Butterfree nor did it disturb Cyclizar and Dragonite, who were watching the moon on the grass.

It quietly followed the "Abra" to a secluded corner.

A white light flashed over the "Abra's" body.

In the light, its body began to change rapidly.

Moments later, a cute-looking Pokemon with a pair of beautiful blue eyes appeared before Teddiursa, a Pokemon it had never seen before.

"Mmm?!"

Teddiursa was a bit surprised.

It cautiously gazed at the mysterious Pokemon with a thin layer of soft pink fur in front of it, asking, "Mmm?"

Is it the hero?

"Miu!"

An ethereal voice came from the mysterious Pokemon.

Upon hearing this voice, Teddiursa's eyes widened.

It really is the hero! The hero is so cute!

The self-proclaimed hero Pokemon looked at the Teddiursa it had saved and said, "Miu." (I'm leaving.)

Teddiursa felt its heart tighten, followed by an overwhelming sense of reluctance.

"Mmm?" Where are you going? It asked.

The hero replied, "Miu!" (To see other places. This world is big and vast, I want to explore more, see humans, other Pokemon, and maybe meet Pokemon like you. When that happens, I'll save them too!)

Just like in the movie about heroes it once watched in a place called Pokemon Hollywood.

There was a dashing Roserade in it, traveling the world and saving Pokemon in danger.

The Pokemon in the movie called that Roserade a hero.

"Mmm..." Hearing this, Teddiursa's face showed longing.

The world is so big, and there are so many things one can do.

It also wanted to travel the world like the hero, to see the broader world.

Teddiursa thought about the courageous scene when the hero appeared in a dark alley to save it from being surrounded.

It quickly asked, "Mmm, mmm?"

Upon hearing this, the hero hesitated.

"Miu?" (You want to travel with me?)

"Mmm!" Teddiursa nodded vigorously.

D's ranch didn't need its help, and instead of staying here, it preferred to travel outside and maybe even find more treasures to bring back to D.

Doesn't D really like the stones it finds?

This afternoon, Teddiursa saw D grinning foolishly at the stones it had collected.

The hero recalled past memories of lonely travels and hesitated a bit.

This was the first time it met a Pokemon who wanted to travel together and shared the same ambitions.

The hero envisioned traveling the world together with Teddiursa.

Just like human Trainers, traveling the world with companions, witnessing beautiful landscapes, and meeting different people and experiences.

At this moment, the hero was moved.

After a brief thought, it said to Teddiursa, "Miu?" (But will that human let you leave with me?)

Teddiursa didn't know, perhaps other humans wouldn't allow it, but D...

"Mmm!"

After much deliberation, Teddiursa decided to give it a try.

So the next morning, as soon as D woke up, he saw Teddiursa running up to him, chirping away.

"What's going on?"

D reached out and picked up Teddiursa.

"Mmm, mmm!"

Teddiursa tried to express its desire to travel, but soon realized D couldn't understand.

This made Teddiursa feel a bit deflated.

"Mmm..."

Seeing his little bear like this, D raised an eyebrow and asked, "Do you have something you want to tell me?"

Teddiursa nodded.

D: "Let me guess, do you want to eat a Honey Bun?"

Teddiursa shook its head.

D: "Then do you want some honey pudding?"

Teddiursa continued shaking its head.

D: "Could it be you want to just drink honey directly?"

"Mmm..."

Teddiursa continued shaking its head.

Seeing this, D was a bit surprised.

Huh? What's going on, that even honey isn't interesting to Teddiursa anymore?

Just then, nearby, Koraidon seemed to sense something.

With a sudden pounce, it made a "thump" sound, as if something had been hit.

Immediately, an Abra was knocked to the ground by Koraidon.

The form of the Abra rapidly transformed the moment it hit the ground, finally becoming a Pokémon resembling a small cat with soft, pink fur and large blue eyes.

Seeing its silhouette, D's eyes widened, showing a rather astonished expression.

Mew, Mew, Mew, Mew, Mew?!

What's happening? Why is there a Mew in his house?

Compared to D, Koraidon was much calmer.

It tilted its head, looking at the Mew hovering in mid-air with a somewhat speechless expression.

"Ahh..."

Seeing itself exposed in front of D and many Pokemon, Mew wasn't flustered; instead, it turned its gaze towards D.

An azure glow flickered in its beautiful blue eyes.

The next second, images appeared in D's mind—images he had never seen before.

In those images, he saw Teddiursa in a dark alleyway.

A few Meowths opening a Treasure Chest next to a trash can mistook it for a territory snatcher and gathered other Meowths from the street to gang up on Teddiursa.

At the critical moment, Mew appeared, driving away the Meowths and saving Teddiursa.

Having witnessed the wider world beyond the mountain and being influenced by Mew, Teddiursa was full of aspirations.

The world is so vast, it wants to see it!

---

---

---

---

# 102: Chapter 100: Preparations Before Departure

Moments later, those images vanished.

D's consciousness returned to reality, and he looked at the Teddiursa and Mew in front of him with a peculiar expression.

After pondering for a long time, D asked, "So, you want to travel outside with Mew?"

"Yippee!"

Seeing D finally understand, Teddiursa couldn't help but exclaim joyfully.

What a hero! He made D understand its thoughts instantly, it thought.

D looked at Teddiursa's happy face and didn't know what to say for a moment.

Teddiursa ate the Lucky Pudding, and thanks to its effects, it luckily encountered the traveling Legendary Pokemon Mew in the city.

The two Pokemon became good friends as a result.

At least for now, it seems that way.

Thinking this, D gazed at the Mew levitating in mid-air.

In reality, Mew was adorably cute, with soft light pink fur all over its body, clean blue eyes, big and moist. A slender tail gently swayed behind it, with two small paws naturally hanging down.

Sensing D's gaze, Mew tilted its head: "miu?"

Whoa!

For an instant, D felt something hit him in his heart.

So cute!

Can he go and touch it?

But what if Mew thinks he's a creep?

After all, it's their first meeting, and it wouldn't be good to scare Mew away.

Thinking about sustainable development, D coughed twice, restrained his itch to move, shifted his focus away from Mew, and resumed talking about serious matters.

"Have you decided where you want to travel?"

"Yippee..."

Hearing these words, Teddiursa hesitated.

About this matter, it hadn't thought it through.

It didn't know what the world outside was like; it only knew it was a vast place, with all kinds of people and things.

Seeing this, D silently thought to himself, as expected.

Compared to humans, it's very dangerous for Pokemon to travel alone.

Especially for weak Pokemon, unlike Mew, Teddiursa is just a regular Pokemon, easily encountering various dangers outside.

For example, being attacked by wild Pokemon, getting lost and never finding the way back, or being sick without anyone to take care of it while unconscious.

With Teddiursa's departure, he may never have the chance to see it again.

D was inclined to persuade.

Why leave? Isn't life here good enough? With endless honey to eat, there are no dangerous Pokemon.

But then he thought, is this really the life Teddiursa wants?

Just like himself, once giving up on chasing dreams to become a corporate drone.

Going outside to see the world is Teddiursa's dream.

D fell silent for a moment, then looked at Teddiursa's apprehensive appearance, sighed, and asked Mew beside him:

"Mew, if Teddiursa travels with you, can you take good care of it?"

"miu!"

Mew lightly nodded.

Of course! Taking care of like-minded companions is a skill a qualified hero must master!

"Alright." D nodded: "I'll leave Teddiursa in your care."

Hearing this, Teddiursa waited excitedly, showing a delighted expression.

"Yippee!"

Mew also showed a childlike side, happily giving high fives with Teddiursa: "miu!"

From today onwards, it has a companion!

Seeing their appearance, D helplessly shook his head, thought for a while, and told them: "But I hope you can leave after a few days."

Before Teddiursa departs, he needs to prepare some food for it so it has strength when it encounters challenges outside.

Additionally, he must find a way for Teddiursa to locate the way back.

Once it has traveled enough, it can return here.

Mew and Teddiursa agreed.

After D finished his daily ranch work, he went to the town.

He found a shop that makes accessories and spent a hefty sum to rush a custom-made wristband for Teddiursa that is waterproof, electric-proof, and heat-resistant.

After it was made, D went to the Porto Marinada Market to buy an abundance of ingredients.

When he rode Cyclizar back home, Teddiursa was sitting on the roof with Mew playing together.

D called Teddiursa down and put the wristband on its arm.

This wristband was specially designed with a hidden space inside.

D placed a note and several large denomination League coins there.

The note indicates Teddiursa's family address, which is the ranch's location.

D told Teddiursa that if it couldn't find its way back, it should go to a human city and find Miss Jenny or Miss Joy, show them the note, and let them help buy a ticket or boat ticket to send Teddiursa back.

Anyway, Miss Joy and Miss Jenny look identical worldwide.

After thinking a bit, D also took Teddiursa to the town to recognize Miss Joy and Miss Jenny.

Looking at the wristband on its arm, Teddiursa was very touched.

D smiled, patting its head, and once everything was prepared, he took it back to the ranch.

Sending a group of Pokemon outside to play, D stood in the kitchen, quite touched as he gazed at the large amount of ingredients laid out before him.

"Hope today I can make super powerful food!" D silently wished in his heart.

He recalled the Honey Pudding and Honey Bun he had made before; the ingredients enhancing Teddiursa seemed inseparable from honey.

Thus, in the upcoming days of cooking experiments, D planned to start with honey.

With a glance at Teddiursa waiting obediently beside him, D turned around, planning to take out a few Lucky Puddings from the fridge for it to eat.

But when he opened the fridge, he found the Lucky Pudding was already eaten up.

D: "..."

Oh well, since he has no idea now, he might as well make some Lucky Pudding for Teddiursa to eat.

After all, it won't be able to eat any for a long time afterward.

And just then, D suddenly thought there were still some Black Truffles at home.

Now here's the question: if he adds high-grade enhanced ingredient Black Truffle into Lucky Pudding, could he make a permanently luck-enhancing dish?

Without hesitation, D began attempting immediately.

He took out the last small piece of Black Truffle, ground it into powder, and mixed it into the prepared pudding liquid, then poured it into a container and put it in the fridge to chill.

After patiently waiting for a while, D couldn't wait to take out the batch of pudding that had Black Truffle added.

Sure enough, the information about the dish changed.

[Black Truffle Caramel Honey Pudding (A+): A uniquely flavored dessert made with Black Truffle, Light Cream, MooMoo Fresh Milk, and Honey.

Cooking Effect: Extreme Luck. After consumption, humans experience a significant temporary boost to luck; for Pokemon, the chance of picking up rare items during the journey increases.

Evaluation: A delicious dessert that makes people feel happy after eating it, more likely to bring good fortune.]

"Extreme Luck?" Looking at the expected cooking effect, D slightly frowned: "It just further enhances luck, but the duration doesn't seem to change, what a pity."

He divided the batch of pudding between Teddiursa and Mew, then looked at the remaining Gelatin Powder and sticky honey, suddenly having a flash of inspiration in his mind, thinking of a new dessert!

---

---

---

---

# 103: Chapter 101: Slick as Oil! The Invincible Skill!

That's honey gummies!

Gelatin powder has the function of providing a stable structure, so it is often used in the culinary industry to make foods like jelly, mousse, and cheese.

And gummies happen to be one of them.

D recalled the method of making honey gummies and then started a new round of production.

First, mix the gelatin powder with water, then combine the honey and Nomel Berry juice, and heat it in a pot over medium-low heat until slightly warm.

Finally, pour the prepared gelatin powder into the pot, continuously stirring until completely melted, becoming clear and golden without a trace of impurity.

Seeing it was about ready, D turned off the heat, let it cool slightly, and then filled it into molds provided as a gift with the refrigerator and started chilling.

While waiting, D began making other honey treats.

However, the results were not very ideal.

[Honey Grilled Fish: Cooking Effect, Stamina Recovery (Large)]

[Honey Lemon Water: Cooking Effect, Revitalization, consuming it will relieve fatigue and restore energy.]

...

"None of these are quite right!"

Looking at these foods, D couldn't help but feel a bit frustrated.

At that moment, the scheduled alarm clock rang.

He turned around, opened the refrigerator, took the solidified honey gummies out of the molds, and as he looked down, the information about these square gummies surfaced in his mind.

[Honey Gummies (A): Made with honey and Nomel Berry, these gummies are tangy, sweet, and chewy, quite favored by some Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Run Away, allows Pokemon to gain the skill: guaranteed escape, ignoring the opponent's strength.

Evaluation: Don't underestimate this small piece of gummy!]

D: "..."

Run Away? What a bizarre effect...

D looked odd. He glanced at the Teddiursa enjoying the pudding nearby and thought, maybe this skill wouldn't be of much use to him, but for Teddiursa, it might be different.

If it encounters a dangerous Pokemon outside and can't win, it can choose to run away directly and be assured of escaping from danger.

Thinking this way, the effect of this honey gummy seems even more exaggerated than the previous honey bun.

If you delve deeper.

Pickup ability + Lucky Pudding + Honey Gummies, combined, might even allow Teddiursa to pluck some wool from Legendary Pokemon.

For example, sneaking a feather from Zapdos, then running away, leaving Zapdos furiously discharging there.

Thinking about it carefully, this is quite thrilling…

D immediately fed a big piece to Teddiursa.

Then earnestly advised: "I have one more thing to tell you, if you encounter danger outside, like a powerful Pokemon, don't hesitate at that time, just turn around and run, remember?"

"Eem!"

In response, Teddiursa felt quite in agreement.

Looking at its expression, D suddenly felt there was no need to remind this.

Because Teddiursa wasn't the type of Pokemon to stubbornly lock horns with an opponent.

After all, when it came to the ranch to steal honey, it chose to flee immediately upon realizing how scary the place was.

Teddiursa's nature was still that of a cunning little bear.

"..."

Alright, just pretend he said nothing.

D turned around and continued researching new recipes.

Yet after researching for three consecutive days, he couldn't come up with another recipe with magical effects.

The concocted dishes either restored stamina or energy, or even attracted attention, making Pokemon immersed in happiness and ignoring the outside world.

Time flew by, and soon it was the day for Teddiursa to leave.

That day, D, along with Koraidon and other Pokemon, saw it off at the ranch's entrance.

Teddiursa was full of reluctance.

"Eem…"

D encouraged with a smile: "Go ahead! If one day you don't want to travel anymore, just come back to the ranch, I'll make a sumptuous honey banquet waiting for you."

"Eem…" Teddiursa wiped a tear and then nodded heavily.

Before leaving, Teddiursa expressed a wish to have another piece of pudding.

Seeing this, D turned back to the kitchen to make it for him.

The previous batch had long been devoured by Teddiursa and Mew.

For a short while, he wouldn't be able to see Teddiursa.

D wished for Teddiursa to have a safe and smooth journey, to fulfill its dreams.

Life's road keeps extending, and Teddiursa, along with Mew, would embark on a new life.

With this sincere wish, D made the last piece of pudding for Teddiursa.

Forty minutes later, this pudding was freshly made.

D took it out from the refrigerator, intending to hand it directly to Teddiursa, but upon seeing the pudding, he noticed a different effect from before.

[Lucky Pudding (S): Made with emotions of sincere wishing, using light cream, MooMoo Fresh Milk, and Honey.

Cooking Effect: Fortune Accompanies, when consumed by humans and Pokemon, luck permanently increases, and when given to Pokemon, the probability of picking rare items during their travels permanently increases.

Evaluation: A delicious dessert, consuming it can make one feel happy and tends to bring good luck.]

?!!

D's eyes widened, he couldn't believe as he looked at this pudding.

Its effect changed from temporary to permanent!

What's going on?! The making process and ingredients were exactly the same, why was there such a big difference in effect?

Could it be the mood during making that made the difference?

D looked up and saw Teddiursa in front of him, eyes bright and expectant, waiting to eat the last piece of pudding before embarking on the journey.

He felt a slight hesitation within.

Because humans could also consume this pudding, and its effect would be permanent too.

But…

Looking at Teddiursa in front of him, D took a deep breath and still chose to give the pudding to it.

"Eat it!"

Teddiursa happily took it, devoured the pudding in one bite, then reluctantly rushed into D's arms for a hug.

D caught Teddiursa with outstretched arms, gently patting its back.

After a while, Teddiursa jumped down.

It went outside, waving to D, and then boarded the Mew, transformed into a Fearow.

The "Fearow" flapped its wings and flew high into the sky.

They kept flying and flying, filled with the desire to see this vast world, flying towards dreams and the distance.

Not sure how much time passed, a vast blue sea appeared ahead.

Beside the sea was a beautiful coastal city, with numerous cruise ships docked at the port.

Mew brought Teddiursa down onto one of the luxury cruise ships about to set sail.

A flash of light as the Fearow vanished, and in its place was a Mew.

Mew and Teddiursa floated above a secluded deck, joyfully overlooking the view ahead.

"Honk——!"

The melodic sound of a whistle echoed, the cruise ship below slowly started, heading towards the vast blue sea.

A human voice came from the ship's loudspeakers:

"Attention all passengers, the Saint Ann is officially setting sail, the next stop will be Vermilion City in the Kanto Region!"

---

---

---

---

# 104: Chapter 102: Flower Field

The "Fearow" carrying Teddiursa finally disappeared into the vast blue sky.

D stood in the farm, gazing in the direction they left, feeling a sense of melancholy in his heart.

Who would have thought, after crossing into the Pokemon World, he didn't go on a journey himself, instead sending his Pokemon off on their travels.

This feeling... it's strange.

Teddiursa has left, but their life must go on.

D took a deep breath and went home to start cleaning the cooking utensils he had just used.

Unknowingly, time had marched into late February.

The crops in the fields were almost ready for harvest again.

Time to get busy!

Early the next morning, D planned to harvest this batch of ripe crops.

When he arrived at the farmland, he was pleasantly surprised to find the flowers he had planted were in bloom.

A variety of flowers mixed together, colorful and vibrant, filled the entire farm with a delicate floral fragrance.

The flowers had finally bloomed, and the Pokemon living on the farm were all very happy.

Alcremie, Smoliv, Nacli, and Pawmi were all attracted over.

The small Alcremie played hide and seek with the little Nacli among the tall flowers.

Smoliv found a comfortable shady spot to linger.

Pawmi excitedly ran to a flower, gently sniffing its fragrance.

Perhaps accidentally inhaling some pollen into its nostrils, it sneezed several times consecutively.

As for which Pokemon were the happiest, it had to be the Combee and Butterfree.

Early in the morning, the diligent Combee happily gathered here, flying back and forth over the blooming flower fields, collecting nectar.

Butterfree gently flapped its wings, pausing among the flowers.

D, wearing a straw hat, watched this scene, a smile involuntarily spreading across his face.

Images of these two types of Pokemon's ecological habits flashed in his mind.

In the wild, when a wild Butterfree encounters wild Combee, they would often fight over nectar.

But on this farm, Butterfree and Combee did not do so; instead, they displayed harmony and mutual understanding.

"You guys have fun here; I need to go to work now."

D said to the Pokemon, then went to the farmland area to start the harvest of this round of crops.

Koraidon and Cyclizar also came to help, placing the harvested tomatoes, cucumbers, and turnips into the large baskets D had prepared in advance.

Worried that he couldn't handle it alone, D never significantly expanded the scale of his planting, because plowing, planting, fertilizing, pest control, and weeding all needed his personal attention.

He also didn't raise too many Pokemon because he was only one person.

If he raised too many, he would be overwhelmed, neglecting some Pokemon, making them unhappy, and resulting in a decrease in the quality of their products, having the opposite effect.

But even so, the work in the field kept them busy for several hours.

Approaching noon, the large baskets were all filled, and D was sweating profusely from exhaustion.

He wiped the sweat from his forehead, looking at those fruits and vegetables.

Truth be told, the profits from agriculture were far less than those from animal husbandry.

But leaving such a large piece of land unplanted would be too wasteful, and the grown crops could be kept for himself and his Pokemon to eat aside from selling.

The only drawback is that these seasonal vegetables can't be stored for long periods.

"It would be great to have an ice cellar," D thought.

Crops like potatoes and turnips can be stored for a long, long time if placed in a low-temperature environment.

But building an ice cellar requires money and also needs specialized cooling facilities.

These large cooling facilities consume a lot of electricity, and if installed, he would probably have to pay quite a bit of electricity bill every month.

So, is there another way?

The first thing that came to D's mind was Ice Type Pokemon.

Ordinary Ice Type Pokemon wouldn't do; it had to be the kind that liked to live in ice caves.

Like Cryogonal, Avalugg, or Glaceon.

An ice cellar could serve as a home for them, or be used to store food, leveraging the power of Pokemon, saving effort and money.

Among these, Glaceon was the one D most wanted to capture.

Because he had a dream: that one day in the future, he would collect all Eevee evolutions.

Glaceon living in the ice cellar, Leafeon taking care of crops, Flareon providing warmth in winter, Jolteon supplying electricity, and Vaporeon occasionally watering with Dragonite.

Just thinking about it, the Eevee family is simply versatile!

In addition to these, it would be necessary to capture a Pokemon capable of cultivating the land.

Looking at the farmland and crops in front of him, D was lost in thought.

Regarding farming, he suddenly thought of a Pokemon.

That was Calyrex.

Calyrex, the legendary king who once ruled ancient Galar, is said to possess great wisdom, able to view all things past, present, and future.

The reason he thought of it was because this Legendary Pokemon is also known as The King of Bountiful Harvests.

It has the ability to heal spirits and make plants sprout. It is said that when it waves its right hand, flowers bloom, and green grass thrives; when it waves its left hand, fields become fertile, and crops bountiful.

That's practically the god of agriculture!

If he could entice it to the farm, in a certain sense, it would be a perfect match for the job.

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but feel he was going too far.

Not long ago, he was just contemplating stealing its horse, and now he wanted to bring the king itself here.

"Sinful, sinful, Calyrex-sama, please bless my farm with bountiful harvests every year!"

D silently prayed in his heart, even pondering if he should build a shrine for Calyrex.

Because according to the settings, Calyrex indeed gets things done.

The only pity is, it's unclear whether Calyrex in the Galar Region can sense the faith coming from the Paldea Region.

————

Meanwhile, at the southernmost tip of the distant Galar region, on a snowfield perpetually blanketed by snowflakes.

A small and slender Pokemon, yet always elegant and dignified in demeanor, weakly opened its eyes.

Its gaze penetrated through distant time and space, seeing a human praying to it.

Feeling that slight trace of faith emanating from the other person, this Pokemon slightly regained some of its spirit.

If D were here, he would immediately recognize this Pokemon as the legendary King of Bountiful Harvests, Calyrex.

It had the special ability to gain strength by absorbing faith.

But in recent years, the faith provided by the people of the Galar Region had dwindled.

The people of the New Era no longer believed in the King of Bountiful Harvests, causing its power to wane, and its body to weaken.

Feeling the faint trace of faith, Calyrex murmured softly:

"Who would have thought, in this era there would still be humans who trust in me..."

---

---

---

---

# 105: Chapter 103: Does Dragonite Want to Return to Dragonite Island?

League Calendar Year 198, Spring, March 14th.

This is the last month of spring, followed closely by the scorching summer.

D really dislikes the feeling of being drenched in sweat, so on this morning, he took the money he'd saved up these days and headed to Porto Marinada Town, intending to buy a few air conditioners.

He rode on Cyclizar all the way to the department store in town, and under the guidance of a sales assistant, returned to the floor selling appliances.

There, he selected four air conditioners, planning to install one in the Salt House where Nacli lives, one in the Pokemon House where Miltank lives, one in the Chicken House, and one in his own cabin.

A hundred and twenty thousand League Coins were spent in an instant, all money saved up over the past half month from selling crops, rock salt, milk, and goat's milk.

Even though he was mentally prepared, looking at his nearly empty wallet, D couldn't help but feel a pang of pain.

But there's no choice, these are necessities of life that must be installed before summer arrives.

Seeing this customer buy so many air conditioners at once, the sales assistant was surprised but still arranged for staff to come and install them.

As soon as the four air conditioners were all installed, D instantly felt relieved.

This way, by the time summer comes, he wouldn't be too hot to sleep!

Thanks to joining by Miltank and the rest, the ranch's funding problem was solved, and the ranch officially entered the next phase—a revival phase.

In this phase, D mainly prepares to do two things.

First, expand his residence, turning it into a real home, a fully functional home with a bedroom, living room, cellar, study, and bathroom.

The bedroom is for resting, the living room can entertain guests occasionally visiting the ranch, and the study can be used to keep account books, read, and record recipes.

As for the cellar…

In another two weeks, summer will arrive, and the hop planting can officially start; without a cellar, it means he can't start a brewing business.

Besides that, he also needs to prepare some money to purchase barrels for brewing.

As for the second thing, it's to plant more summer crops and use them to develop even more new dishes.

Then see if any of the dishes meet the criteria, and use them to strengthen the Pokemon on the ranch, achieving a cycle.

At noon, D began preparing lunch for all the Pokemon.

He opened the refrigerator, looking at the abundant ingredients inside, and his gaze paused on a jar of golden sticky liquid.

Soon after, the information about this jar of liquid popped into his mind.

[Tulip Honey: Honey made from the pollen and nectar of tulips, carefully tasting can bring out a faint floral aroma from it, making it an excellent ingredient for desserts.]

This is the honey that the Combee has gathered from the flower field recently.

The first time he saw them gathering, D got a fright.

Because he remembered tulips contain mannose and galactose, which are toxic to bees in that world, so bees gathering tulip nectar would die on the flowers.

But it turns out that the constitution of Pokemon is beyond what ordinary bees can compare to.

Although they're both "bees," tulip nectar had no effect on the Combee.

Seeing this, D felt relieved and let them gather.

It's a pity that Teddiursa left too early, if it had stayed a few more days, it could have tasted this new flavor of honey. D thought.

All this honey has been used by him to make Lucky Pudding, but unfortunately, despite making so much of it, he hasn't managed to make a batch with a permanent luck effect again.

However, D is not discouraged, because to him, whether it's permanent or not doesn't matter.

While he's in the ranch, he can have a pudding for the lucky effect at any time. If he keeps eating them, the effect they bring is virtually permanent when averaged out.

"I wonder where Teddiursa and Mew are right now."

Pressing down the concerns in his heart, D started preparing lunch for Koraidon and the others.

Today's lunch is the same as usual: grilled steak and sandwiches, plus a serving of smashed cucumbers and a cup of optional Berry Juice and MooMoo Milk.

During the meal, D looked at Dragonite and realized he hadn't asked about its move training progress in a long time, so he casually asked:

"Did you master the Electric Type move Thunder I taught you earlier?"

Talking about moves, Dragonite immediately became excited.

"Rawr!" It nodded its big head.

Not only that, but it has also managed to successfully execute the combo moveset of Rain Dance + Hurricane + Thunder that D taught it last time!

"Oh?" Seeing this, D was a bit surprised: "You've progressed quite quickly!"

"Rawr~"

So after the meal, Dragonite dragged D outside with its big claws to show the progress it has made over this period.

D stood under the eaves, with Koraidon and Cyclizar standing on either side of him.

They saw Dragonite flap its wings and fly into the air.

It concentrated, and the once clear sky was instantly covered with a dense layer of dark clouds, with tendrils of Electric Type Energy swirling within.

It started to rain.

At first, it was a light drizzle, but gradually, the rain grew heavier, forming strings that poured down with a patter.

Dragonite was bathed in this heavy rain, its body drenched glossy by the raindrops, its eyes seeming to blaze with flames.

The next second, Dragonite flapped its wings, and a gust of Hurricane formed, merging with the pouring rain to create a terrifyingly large storm.

D watched the rather spectacular scene.

If an ordinary Pokemon faced this move without special environmental training, it would probably end up in a flustered mess.

At this moment—

There was a loud rumble.

A giant bolt of lightning flickered directly from the thundercloud down, striking the grass below into a deep pit.

Sss!

D took a sharp breath.

What terrifying power!

If a Pokemon got hit by that move…he simply couldn't imagine.

At this moment, the storm began to subside, the dark clouds above gradually dispersed, revealing the clear blue sky.

"Clap clap clap!"

D immediately started applauding, praising: "That's amazing!"

"Rawr…"

Hearing this, Dragonite sheepishly landed beside D, using its big claw to scratch its head.

No way, it's still got a long way to go!

In Dragonite's mind flashed the image of the mysterious Pokemon it saw above the typhoon's eye, entirely pitch black, resembling a roaming dragon.

Looking at the transformed Dragonite before him, D couldn't help but feel moved.

Extreme Speed, Dragon Tail, Thunder Punch, Hurricane, Agility, Dragon Dance, Rain Dance, Thunderbolt, Thunder.

Before he knew it, Dragonite had learned so many moves.

D felt like there was nothing left for him to teach it.

Thinking of this, D asked: "You've already learned so many moves, what are your plans next?"

Hearing this, Dragonite immediately panicked.

Because hearing D's words, it seemed like he was saying I have nothing left to teach you, you can leave now!

Is he trying to send it away?

Dragonite didn't want to leave, and immediately looked forlorn as it furrowed its brow: "Rawr…"

Though humans and Pokemon can't speak the same language, looking at Dragonite's expression, D somehow magically understood what it meant at this moment.

He felt a mixture of amusement and helplessness: "I'm not trying to send you away, I just want to ask about your next plans, I mean I'll soon have nothing left to teach you, by then will you want to leave the ranch and return to Dragonite Island?"

D hadn't forgotten, Dragonite initially followed him wanting to learn moves.

Hearing this, Dragonite fell into contemplation.

At first, it was indeed because it wanted to learn moves that it followed D.

But later…

D gave it a Berry Platter to help it learn Extreme Speed.

D got Koraidon to teach it to use Dragon Tail.

D accompanied it under the rain to practice Thunder Punch and Rain Dance, and even got sick because of it.

Worried about D, it then realized Agility while delivering milk.

At this moment, Dragonite wasn't staying with D just because it wanted to get stronger anymore.

——It liked this human, and wanted to stay with him forever.

---

---

---

---

# 106: Chapter 104: The Day of the Dragon King's Return!

Thinking of this, Dragonite resolutely shook its head.

It wanted to stay! Live together with D!

But before that, Dragonite hoped D could accompany it to Dragonite Island.

It wanted to show off the moves it had learned in front of the other Dragonite and tell them it had become the most powerful Dragonite in the group, making them envious!

Dragonite looked serious, tugging at D's arm with its chubby paw.

"Rawr!"

"What's wrong?"

D didn't react immediately. It took him a while to realize that Dragonite seemed to want to take him somewhere.

But unfortunately, Dragonite couldn't speak, and the two of them couldn't communicate normally.

After a long time of rawr-ing, seeing that D still didn't understand, Dragonite drooped its head, looking disheartened and aggrieved.

It looked very pitiful in this state.

D's heart was moved. He reached out to pat Dragonite's head, recalling Dragonite's recent actions, and guessed in his mind:

"You want me to go somewhere with you?"

Hearing this, Dragonite's eyes instantly lit up.

"Rawr!"

The place Dragonite wanted to take him...

Intuition told D it couldn't be Casseroya Lake, nor the cliff where they first met.

Plus, from what he saw in Yousuke's picture book about Dragonite's ecological habits—flying from the sea to Casseroya Lake every evening, staying the night, and returning to the sea at dawn.

This meant the place Dragonite wanted to take him to was likely the Dragonite Island in the sea, home to many Dragonite.

While thinking about it, D asked, "Is it far from the ranch?"

There were things to do at the ranch every day, and he couldn't leave for too long.

"Rawr!"

Dragonite shook its head; at its speed, they'd get there quickly.

Seeing this, D agreed: "Alright."

Upon seeing D agree, Dragonite immediately cheered and indicated they could set off now.

D looked up at the sky.

Seeing it was still early, D reckoned he could be back before dark.

But to avoid any mid-way accidents, he prepared food for the Pokemon at the ranch in advance.

He told them he would be going out with Dragonite, and if he returned too late, they shouldn't wait up for him to rest first.

None of the Pokemon had any objections, except for Koraidon, who wanted to come along.

With no choice, D took it with him.

After handling affairs at the ranch, D rode on Koraidon, leaving the ranch under Dragonite's lead, setting off towards the island of Dragonite.

The two Pokemon gradually ascended, the ranch below becoming a small black dot, eventually disappearing into the vast Paldea Region meadow.

The wind blew past their ears, the sky above was a clear blue, and in the distance, Paldea Sea stretched endlessly.

The speed of the two Pokemon was not slow. After about a dozen minutes, they left Paldea's borders, reaching above the ocean.

Looking at this familiar place, Dragonite happily said to D: "Rawr~"

It mimicked the powerful and magnificent mysterious Pokemon with intense dance movements.

Seeing its actions, D suddenly realized, "Did you meet that black Pokemon here?"

Dragonite nodded repeatedly: "Rawr!"

It looked up towards the higher dome, indicating that the Pokemon flew higher and disappeared above the clouds.

"Rayquaza normally lives in the atmosphere." D looked up at the sky, and higher, to the mysterious cosmos, and said to Dragonite with emotion, "It's rare for it to come down here, so you were lucky to encounter it."

He regretfully added, "I wish I had come with you back then..."

After all, it was a shiny Rayquaza!

Seeing it even once would make his journey to this world worthwhile.

Most people rarely, if ever, see Legendary Pokemon in their lifetime.

Seeing D constantly obsessed, Dragonite couldn't help but look upwards: "Rawr?" Should we try to find it there?

D understood what Dragonite meant and shook his head: "Better not."

For Rayquaza, the whole planet's atmosphere was its domain. It was in Paldea before, and there's no telling where it has flown to now; it might even be on the other side of the planet.

Gradually, the Paldea region behind them also disappeared. Looking around, all that met the eye was endless azure ocean.

The sea occasionally raised white waves, with Wattrel skimming over, preying on the schools of fish beneath.

Occasionally, a small island of white sand appeared, with a few coconut trees growing wildly, some Wigletts poking their heads from the beach, enjoying the afternoon sun.

Now and then, Finizen would leap high out of the water, chasing and playing with their companions.

In mid-air, D looked down at the vibrant scene, unable to help but exclaim:

"These are Pokemon rarely seen on land!"

But this also proved they were already far from the Paldea region.

As time ticked away, just when D no longer remembered how long he'd left the ranch, a small island suddenly appeared on the horizon.

---

It was a vibrant island, covered in lush vegetation as seen from afar.

The vegetation formed a circular belt, encircling the center of the island. Under the sunlight, the entire island appeared lively and lush.

Seeing the island, Dragonite's eyes lit up with excitement. It immediately turned to D and exclaimed, "Aowoo!"

D raised an eyebrow and asked, "Is that where you used to live?"

"Aowoo!"

Dragonite nodded.

D didn't ask further. Seeing they were about to reach their destination, Dragonite and Koraidon lowered their flying altitude, flying close to the sea surface before landing on a steep cliff.

Below, the waves constantly battered the cliff. D jumped off Koraidon.

"Aowoo~"

Having returned to its former home, Dragonite was very happy and eagerly led the way.

D and Koraidon followed behind, curiously observing the unique surroundings.

Unlike other places, the plants on this island were exceptionally lush, with deep grass, robust trees, and yellow and white wildflowers growing in the forest, with branches laden with plump berries.

"Interesting."

Seeing this scene, D couldn't help but think of the Dragon's Holy Sanctuary in Blackthorn City of the Johto Region.

It was a very special place.

The land was covered with beautiful flowers and trees, and the flowers bloomed all year round.

It was said that all of this was influenced by the power of Dragon Type Pokemon.

If this was Dragonite's hometown, then there must be more than just one Dragonite here.

So, were these lush plants also influenced by the Dragonites on the island?

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but stop and moved under a large, lush tree.

He wanted to pick a large leaf to examine, but in the next second, a small figure suddenly peeked out from behind the leaf.

"Ah!"

D was startled and let out a cry, losing his balance and falling onto the grass behind.

Koraidon and Dragonite turned back at the sound, only to see a petite, pale blue-scaled, bright white-bellied, serpentine Pokemon peeking curiously at this stranger from behind the leaf.

The Pokemon had blue eyes and a white bump on its forehead, which was the developing horn.

It had white fins on the sides of its head, a large round white nose, and although it was snake-like, it lacked the sinister coldness of snakes and instead appeared very cute.

Koraidon helped D up, and D looked at the small Pokemon, breathing a sigh of relief:

"It's a Dratini!"

He thought it was a poisonous snake hiding behind the tree to ambush him.

Seeing the Dratini, Dragonite rushed over and chattered at it twice.

The Dratini immediately looked like it was about to cry.

D: "...Why are you scolding it?"

Dragonite looked innocent: "Aowoo..."

It wasn't scolding Dratini, it was just worried about Dratini's safety.

Dratini was at its weakest stage, during which it would be protected by the Dragonites and Dragonairs in the tribe until it evolved.

On this island, all Dratinis lived in the central lake of the island and couldn't sneak out without an adult.

Seeing Dragonite's expression, it really didn't seem to be scolding the Dratini on purpose. D stood by quietly observing.

Dragonite seemed to say something to Dratini, and Dratini followed behind like a guilty child.

They continued forward, guided by Dragonite, and after passing through the dense circular forest, they reached the center of the island.

A huge lake appeared here.

Seeing the situation clearly, D was completely stunned.

Wow, so many Dragonites!

In the lake, many Dratinis and Dragonairs swam around.

On the grassy lakeside, quite a few Dragonites stood.

They grouped in twos and threes, sitting or standing, with some lying on the grass snoring.

Some looked rough and fierce, very cold, much like Iris's Dragonite from the anime.

Others appeared gentle, happily watching the Dratinis in the lake while sitting comfortably on the grass.

D even saw a pink-bodied Dratini in the lake.

That's a Shining Dratini!

Pink and adorable!

D was very charmed.

At this moment, their arrival caught the attention of the Dragonites.

They stopped what they were doing and turned to look over.

Then, D saw his Dragonite flap its wings, fly over to the group, chattering with the Dragonites, and then, under the watchful eyes of the Dragonites, Dratinis, and Dragonairs, soar into the sky.

Dragonite concentrated, and a few clouds floated over the previously clear sky.

It began to rain from above.

Dragonite flapped its wings, merging the wind and rain, forming a massive hurricane, ravaging the entire island.

Dragonite circled within, surrounded by streaks of lightning, with a loud crash as a thick bolt of lightning struck down directly.

The wild Dragonites by the lakeside watched this scene, dumbfounded.

Some Dragonites had their eyes wide open, with shocked expressions remarkably human-like.

---

The rain gradually eased until the sky cleared again.

Only then did Dragonite proudly land on the ground.

At this moment, D finally understood the purpose of Dragonite's return.

It was showing off to its companions the powerful moves it had mastered!

After Dragonite's display, the young Dratini in the great lake looked on with admiration in their eyes.

Just then, a defiant-looking Dragonite, after recovering from its shock, challenged Dragonite to a duel.

Dragonite blinked and happily accepted the challenge.

The two Dragonite flew into the air and officially began the sparring, watched by a crowd of Dragonite.

The Wild Dragonite opened its mouth wide, starting to gather a purple energy inside.

D immediately recognized the name of that move—Dragon Rage.

So, how would his Dragonite respond?

D quickly turned his gaze toward his Dragonite.

He saw that it wasn't flustered at all; instead, it flapped its wings and charged swiftly forward, launching a surprise attack at lightning speed.

Just as the Wild Dragonite's Dragon Rage was being gathered, it was hit by Dragonite.

This move Extreme Speed knocked it off balance, and the purple shockwave it had gathered was spit out toward the sky.

"Nice, it even knows to use Extreme Speed for a preemptive strike," D praised in his heart.

The battle was not yet over.

That Wild Dragonite, stunned by Dragonite's speed being faster than its own, launched a new wave of attacks once more.

It flapped its wings, conjuring a fierce Hurricane that swept forward.

But this move hardly affected Dragonite.

After all, Dragonite had abundant experience facing Hurricanes; back then, it had endured all pains to cross typhoons and reach the eye of the storm.

So now, Dragonite withstood the fierce wind, its mind flashing back to the image of Rayquaza dancing fiercely amidst the storm.

Then, facing the wind, it charged forward.

A layer of Spark appeared on its fist, and it struck the opponent with a Thunder Punch.

The Wild Dragonite staggered but quickly regained its posture.

The dragon wings on its back spread to the widest, a white light flashed across, transforming into massive white light wings, charging straight at Dragonite.

Facing this Wing Attack, Dragonite didn't retreat; instead, it swung its tail to meet the blow.

Wing Attack vs Dragon Tail!

The two Dragonite collided in mid-air; then, one of them fell to the ground like a kite with a broken string.

The battle had ended.

Gazing at Dragonite floating in the air, at this moment, the entire audience was stunned!

Almost all the Dragonite had an incredulous expression on their faces.

Clearly, the last time they met, it was a dummy Dragonite that couldn't learn moves. Why has it become so powerful now?

Under the watchful eyes of many Dragonite, Dragonite somewhat bashfully landed in front of the injured Dragonite, looking at it with some concern.

At this moment, D ran up with Koraidon, pulling out a bottle of Berry Milk with the effect of restoring Pokemon's stamina and fed it to the Dragonite injured by Dragonite.

Instantly, its wounds disappeared at a speed visible to the naked eye.

The Dragonite looked astonished.

It didn't blame Dragonite since it's not uncommon for Dragonite to occasionally spar with each other and get injured.

It looked at this human and then at this suddenly powerful companion and couldn't help but ask from the bottom of its heart:

"Awooo?" (How did you become so powerful?)

Dragonite, looking delighted, happily replied, "Awooo!" (That's because I found my Trainer!)

Wild Dragonite looked puzzled: "Awooo?" (Trainer?)

Thinking of Koraidon's description of a Trainer, Dragonite narrated, "Awooo!" (A human who can help Pokemon become stronger and learn moves!)

At that very moment, hearing these words, all the Dragonite seeking to grow stronger turned their gaze to the only human present.

D immediately felt a chill down his spine.

But before he could speak, Dragonite put on a wary expression, standing in front of him: "Awooo!" (This is my Trainer!)

---

---

---

---

# 107: Chapter 105: Dragonite's Love

Dragonite Island, central lake, 3:00pm.

D and Koraidon sat by the lake, gazing thoughtfully at the sky, dotted with white clouds.

Not far away, a Dragonite was surrounded by everyone, recounting events that had occurred recently.

D and Koraidon chose not to disturb them, instead appreciating the island's unique landscape from their spot by the lake.

Although the island was situated in the depths of the sea, perhaps influenced by the power of the Dragonite, the atmosphere here felt no different from that on land.

The circular lake, encircled by surrounding trees, resembled a full moon, lying quietly in the embrace of the sea.

If night came, D could imagine the scene here.

——The lake surface reflects the night sky, a full moon in the sky, and also a full moon in the lake water. When Dratini swim through it, the moon shatters along the ripples.

But there's no need to worry; just wait patiently for a moment, and the shattered moon in the lake will return to wholeness.

The Dratini rest on this "full moon," bathing in the moonlight, entering their dreams.

D's mind conjured up these images—the grass by the lake was soft, the sunlight above painted patches through the tree shadows, and the wind was gentle.

His heart was at peace; he couldn't help but lie down beside Koraidon, his gaze passing through the circular opening to the blue sky above.

Suddenly, a shadow swept over his head. When D looked closely, he saw several petite Dratini hovering over his head, their big eyes curiously observing him lying on the ground.

Their shadows blocked the light above, their expressions of wonder as if puzzled why this human was suddenly lying on the ground.

D was slightly startled, then reached out to poke the cheek of one of the quasi-god hatchlings.

Sensing the touch, a smile appeared on D's face.

The little Dratini felt smooth and soft to the touch.

Surrounded by so many adorable quasi-god hatchlings, D felt like he had reached the peak of his life.

Dratini are so cute!

But compared to Salamence, Hydreigon, and those dragon-type Pokemon, Dragonite truly is very tame and friendly, both to humans and hatchlings.

"Woo~"

The touched Dratini let out a melodious and innocent cry.

D then sat up, turning his head to start searching the lake's surface for that Shining Dratini.

However, after searching for a long time, he couldn't find any trace of it.

"Strange, could I have seen it wrong?"

He asked a Dragonair nearby, "Have you seen a pink Dratini around here?"

"Woo?"

The Dragonair blinked, seemingly not quite understanding his question.

Seeing this, D explained, "It's a different color, not the same as you Dratini."

This time the Dragonair understood; it knew which Dratini this human was talking about, then happily dived into the lake, soon returning with a pink Dratini.

Gazing at this uniquely colored Dratini, D's eyes lit up immediately.

Indeed, it was the Shining Dratini!

Ordinary Dratini are light blue, but this Dratini was light pink, looking very girlish and extremely cute.

This Dratini, brought by its companion, looked at him shyly.

Seeing this scene, D felt that this Dratini might have a timid personality.

When he attempted to reach out and touch it, the Dratini immediately showed a frightened expression.

Noticing this, D instinctively wanted to pull out candy from his pocket to coax it like a child, but soon realized he had brought nothing except two bottles of emergency Berry Milk on this trip.

"What a pity."

D looked regretful.

If only he knew Dragonite was bringing him to Dragon Island, he would have brought all the snacks from his refrigerator.

So, D could only try to convey friendliness to it: "Don't be afraid; we won't harm you, um...we just want to be friends with you."

Yet this Dratini still felt very scared.

Following its gaze, D then understood what the Dratini was afraid of.

"Ah, grrr…"

Upon realizing D's gaze, Koraidon next to him immediately showed an innocent expression.

D: "..."

He wanted to say more when suddenly all conversations among the gathered Dragonite ceased.

A Wild Dragonite flew over from the sky, slowly landing on the island.

Then, D noticed his own Dragonite looking at the other, appearing quite tense.

Something's not right!

D narrowed his eyes, picking up on an unusual vibe.

As everyone knows, Pokemon can also experience love like humans, then reproduce and flourish.

Just like Meowth in the anime, which loved the female Meowth Madonna, but Madonna eventually chose to be with Persian and rejected it.

Some Pokemon even fall in love with other species; the anime even featured phrases like "Tropius fell in love with Miss Joy's Meganium."

And now, seeing Dragonite muster courage to step forward, this scene couldn't help but remind D of a shy young boy confessing to his crush.

Dragonite looked very nervous and shy, stepping forward without knowing what to say to the other.

Immediately after, D saw that Wild Dragonite assume a fighting stance.

D was taken aback, involuntarily asking, "What are they doing?"

"Ah, grrr…" Koraidon didn't understand such matters but recognized that Wild Dragonite.

Nearby, Dragonair also watched that scene, softly speaking as if answering D's question:

"Woo…" (That Dragonite is confessing to the other…)

But all the Dragonite on the island know that the female Dragonite is quite special.

It is extremely strong, and every Dragonite that confesses to it gets beaten badly.

According to it, only a Dragonite that can defeat it head-on can become its future mate and together raise even stronger Dratini.

However, unfortunately, D couldn't understand Pokemon language and naturally couldn't grasp Dragonair's words.

He only saw his Dragonite and that female Dragonite start battling.

The battle began. The female Dragonite unleashed her full aura, exuding terrifying killing intent, then dove towards Dragonite.

Faced with this seemingly dreadful attack, Dragonite was initially quite panicked, but quickly pulled itself together.

It increased its speed to dodge the Dragon Rush, then used Rain Dance.

Vast clouds rolled in, and the sky began to pour down rain.

Dragonite flapped its wings, creating a Hurricane, the storm mixed with rain, roaring as it swept forth.

In an instant, the female Dragonite was enveloped by this storm and rain.

I was very shocked.

Though puzzled why this Dragonite, which once couldn't learn any powerful moves, had become so strong, it still clenched its teeth, focusing completely on the battle.

It wouldn't be defeated so easily!

---

---

---

---

# 108: Chapter 106: Returning to the Nest

The storm almost covered the entire sky above the island.

To avoid affecting everyone on the island, the two Dragonites came to the sea outside.

Seeing this, D quickly got up and rode on Koraidon to follow.

The other Dragonites and Dragonairs on the island also followed.

They all came to the edge of the island, standing on the cliff, gazing at the magnificent scene not far away.

The sky underwent dramatic changes, the storm swept over the sea, and the two Dragonites continued their battle in the storm.

The female Dragonite's attacks were extremely fierce, and in the face of the storm, it also flapped its wings, unleashing an even more powerful Hurricane.

The situation, originally favorable for Dragonite, immediately turned around.

The fierce storm streaked across its body, but fortunately, the scales on its surface protected it from too much harm.

Dragonite gritted its teeth, imagining D's teachings, as a strong Electric Type Energy gathered in its body.

"Boom!"

A thunderclap exploded in the clouds above, terrifying lightning surging within them.

This sound gave the female Dragonite a shock, and it frowned, its body standing in the storm suddenly accelerated.

Before the lightning could strike, it charged towards Dragonite at lightning speed.

That was Extreme Speed!

The Thunder was interrupted, and Dragonite staggered from the collision, feeling momentarily dazed.

At that moment, the female Dragonite charged again.

With a move of Dragon Tail, it sent Dragonite flying onto the island.

The battle ended, and the dark clouds in the sky dispersed completely.

The female Dragonite slowly descended in front of Dragonite, looking at this suddenly formidable yet foolish Dragonite, it said:

"Roar!" (Though I don't know what you've encountered recently, it's still not enough!)

Dragonite did not feel discouraged for losing the battle, or rather, its original purpose for requesting the battle was to see its own growth.

But for now, as the opponent said, it's still far from enough!

"Roar..." (When we get back, I must increase the practice of moves…)

Dragonite had already begun to plan its life after returning to the ranch.

Covered in wounds, it stood up from the ground, not feeling down at all, but full of fighting spirit.

The female Dragonite was drawn to Dragonite's demeanor, giving a few more glances to this formerly clumsy Dragonite.

It seems its changes are quite significant?

No longer having that constantly gloomy and sorrowful look sitting blankly on the island.

At this moment, Dragonite had returned to D's side, seriously saying: "Roar!" (Let's go back!)

D understood what Dragonite meant, looking at the female Dragonite behind them, finding that it was also looking this way.

D raised an eyebrow slightly, not saying much, but turned around to bid farewell to the delicate and lovely Dratinis and Dragonairs by his side.

"I have to go, I'll come play with you next time!"

If there is a next time, he'll bring a bunch of Pokemon snacks to feed these cute little fellows.

Dragonite also waved its chubby paws to bid farewell to its companions on the island.

A few Dragonites watched this scene with envy, deeply moved by the human Trainer who could help them grow.

So, they exchanged glances and then proactively ran over.

"Roar!"

"Roar~"

"..."

Seeing their actions, Dragonite was instantly on high alert, pushing them back with its big paw.

"Roar!" (This is mine!)

One Dragonite resisted strongly: "Roar!" (It could also be ours!)

"Roar!" (No way!)

But having just experienced two battles, Dragonite was now exhausted, ultimately unable to block those Dragonites, allowing them to charge in front of D.

Looking at the three young Dragonites surrounding him with eager eyes, D was quite surprised.

What does this mean?

Soon, D thought of a possibility.

His heart moved, looking at the Dragonites in front of him, pondering, "Do you want to come with us?"

"Awoo~"

The three Dragonite nodded repeatedly.

But just at that moment, Dragonite regained a bit of energy and ran over to hug D tightly with its big claws.

D: "..."

Koraidon stood beside them, utterly speechless. It let out a loud roar, scaring off the three Dragonite, and then leisurely walked over to D, signaling him to ride on its back.

Dragonite exchanged a glance with Koraidon, and in this matter, the two Pokemon rarely reached a consensus.

Regarding the three Dragonite that wanted to join, although D was tempted, he still refused them.

Because he could guess their intentions for wanting to follow him.

That is, they wanted to become stronger like Dragonite.

However, the reason Dragonite has reached this point is entirely due to its own efforts; D only provided a bit of guidance and advice.

His Dragonite is naive and easily convinced, can be arbitrarily pua'd, but other Dragonite might not be the same.

Moreover, suddenly having so many Dragonite that are big eaters joining, he really might not be able to afford them; just cooking meals every day would exhaust him half to death.

Of course, this doesn't mean he'll completely abandon this adorable group of Dragonite.

At least before creating meals that can help Dragonite grow...

Once the farm expands further, needing more delivery staff to help with shipments, then he can lure these Dragonite into working with those meals, establishing a Dragonite farm and Dragonite delivery company of his own.

Anyway, Dragon Island is right here; the Dragonite won't run away.

Thinking of this, D said to the three Dragonite, "I understand your intentions, but I'm sorry, I can't take you back with me now."

"But don't worry. Maybe by the next meeting, I'll bring a way to help you become stronger. Until that day comes, please wait patiently here."

The three Dragonite blinked their eyes and then happily nodded, agreeing.

At least this human hasn't completely rejected them!

Dragonite have very long lifespans; they have ample time to wait for that day to come.

Looking at the group of Dragonite, Dragonair, and Dratini in front of him, D wanted to say more, but was forcefully pulled away by Dragonite and Koraidon.

"Awoo!" (Okay, no more talking, it's time to go home!)

D sat on Koraidon's back, waving goodbye to the dragons: "Goodbye, Dragonair, Dratini!"

Dragon Island gradually disappeared from view, feeling the sea breeze blowing towards him, D asked:

"Did you really like that Dragonite today?"

"Awoo..."

Under the setting sun, Dragonite gently nodded and then shook its head.

Although it likes the others, after experiencing life on the farm and witnessing that powerful and mysterious Pokemon with its own eyes, it no longer feels sad or hurt because of their rejection.

Just as Teddiursa said, this world is vast, with many things it can do.

For example, covering the entire world with its rain!

Dragonite faced the setting sun, letting out a passionate dragon roar, using all its strength to perform Rain Dance.

In mid-air, rolling clouds swept over, covering the vast sky.

A torrential downpour fell from them, raindrops stringing together, pattering onto the sea surface, merging into the ocean.

Dragonite flapped its wings, stirring currents, and soared freely amidst the rain.

D and Koraidon got drenched, looking at Dragonite's spirited figure, feeling a bit speechless.

"Seriously..."

Koraidon was very dissatisfied with Dragonite's behavior of using Rain Dance anywhere and anytime.

It let out a loud roar, driving away the clouds in the sky, and the sunlight became increasingly intense.

Under this strong sunlight, Koraidon emanated intense crimson energy, continuously pulsating around it.

Well, performing Sunny Day even triggered Koraidon's ability, causing crimson pulsations.

D speechlessly raised his hand to call Dragonite back, stopping them from messing with the weather:

"Alright, restore the weather to its original state, otherwise, you'll mess it up again."

The strong sunlight dispersed, and the sky returned to the color of dusk.

The beautiful sunset dyed the entire sky red, reflecting in the sea and coloring it with a sunset hue.

The Kilowattrel and Pelipper that had been flying outside all day began to return home.

Gazing at that scene, D couldn't help but exclaim, "How beautiful!"

The evening is approaching, it's time to go home!

---

---

---

---

# 109: Chapter 107: Days Without a Ponyta (Third Update)

Another brand-new day.

This morning, after finishing his farm work, D leisurely took a watering can and started watering the flowers in the garden.

Early in the morning, the diligent Combees began their labor.

They flew back and forth among the flowers, collecting nectar from the blossoms.

D warmly greeted them, "Good morning, Combee!"

"Buzz~"

The Combees let out a melodious growl in response to D's greeting, then continued with their work.

After watering, D started patrolling his ranch, the home he and the many Pokémon had painstakingly built.

In the fields, Dragonite was, as usual, practicing Rain Dance.

In front of the Chicken House, Pawmi was herding the hens.

Over a month had passed, and the little yellow chicks that hatched from their eggs had grown quite a bit and started mimicking their mother, pecking at food in the grass.

At this stage, the chicks already knew they shouldn't eat beyond their fill, so there was no worry about them overeating. D changed the feeding system in the Chicken House to free feeding.

This was much more convenient.

Upon seeing him approaching, the leisurely Pawmi on the grass merrily raised its paws, greeting him.

Over time, this lost Pawmi had completely adapted to life on the ranch.

Imitating his schedule, it began working from early morning till late evening. Every morning after breakfast, it would automatically head to the Chicken House to herd, and in the evening, it would herd the hens back into the house, then obediently return to the cabin to sleep with him.

D smiled and nodded to Pawmi, then continued onward.

Ahead was the Pokemon House, where three Miltanks lived.

The pasture seeds he sowed on the land in front of the house over a fortnight ago had sprouted, and he believed it wouldn't be long before they grew into lush, delicious pasture.

The three Miltanks had finished producing milk and were lying on the grass, basking in the sun.

They simply loved life here.

Because the food here was much tastier than at their previous ranch.

To thank the rancher here, they put effort into producing milk every day.

—After all, leaving it is a waste; it's better to milk it all out, making them feel lighter.

Moving further ahead, they reached Nacli's Salt House.

However, recently, the weather had been improving, and the Naclis, led by the Alcremie, were frolicking outside every day.

This resulted in a significant decrease in rock salt production.

D felt helpless about this but chose not to shut the Naclis inside, letting them play freely instead.

Finding no one in the Salt House, D closed the door and headed to the Gogoats' territory.

The three Gogoats had finished their dancing for today and were lazily lying on the grass, munching on it.

Beside them, there was a figure that surprised D.

Is that... Smoliv?

Curious, he walked over to the Smoliv by the Gogoats and asked, "What are you doing here?"

Smoliv swayed gently, happily responding, "Oliv~"

Because it's really cozy here!

At that moment, D noticed a green glow emanating from beneath the Gogoats.

He instantly understood.

Thanks to the Gogoats, the Grass Type Energy on this patch of grass was particularly dense and abundant.

This environment was a sanctuary for plants and Grass-Type Pokémon, so they naturally gravitated toward it and stayed there.

"Gogoat, I'll leave Smoliv in your care for now; I need to get to work," D said.

The Gogoats gladly agreed to look after Smoliv.

Meanwhile, D went to the shed behind the house, took out an axe and a saw, and used the leftover wood and a simple workbench inside to start crafting a wooden fence.

He planned to handcraft a wooden fence, enclosing the flower garden and separating it from the fields, dedicating it solely for viewing pleasure.

Once summer came and those flowers wilted, D planned to plant a batch of sunflower seeds there, establishing a beautiful Sunflora Field.

Such precise handiwork was beyond Koraidon's help; its strength was too excessive, likely snapping wood accidentally.

Thus, D had to do it himself, and he toiled until the afternoon to barely finish the task.

Setting up the wooden fence around the garden and hanging a sign painted with flowers at the entrance, a complete garden was finally ready.

D clapped his hands, realizing several calluses had formed unknowingly during his work.

He didn't mind it, though.

Perhaps for a farmer, these represented badges of honor for hard work?

Dusting off his hands, D patted the heads of Koraidon and Cyclizar beside him, asking, "What do you guys want to eat tonight?"

"Ah guh!~"

"Gao!~"

The two Pokémon responded simultaneously.

Yet D didn't understand a single word, laughing it off, "How about sandwiches then?"

Koraidon, Cyclizar: "..."

Saying that, D took out their favorite steaks from the fridge, intending to make fried steaks.

Just then, the video phone behind him rang.

D raised his eyebrow in surprise.

Who could be calling him at this time?

He washed his hands and went over to answer it, hearing Mayor Thomas's voice through the call.

"Good evening, D!"

"Good evening, Mayor Thomas! What can I do for you today?"

"Haha, it's about the horse racing event at the end of March. Since you have a phone now, I'm calling instead of mailing you a letter."

"The town will be hosting a Spring Horse Race on the 25th of this month. Anyone with a Horse-type Pokémon can participate, along with numerous passing Trainers entering the race!"

"However, it seems you don't have any Horse-type Pokémon yet, so you might not be able to participate."

"But you can still come and watch the race. You can buy betting coupons at the event for the contestants you fancy. If they become the Champion, you can exchange the betting coupons for various items!"

Betting coupons? Isn't that just horse betting?

Hearing about exchanging for various items, D's interest was piqued, and he inquired, "Items?"

Mayor Thomas answered, "Since most of the contestants are passing Trainers, we've prepared many items useful to Trainers, like various Evolution Stones, equipment for training Pokémon, and other wondrous items for Pokémon to hold."

Evolution Stones...

D quickly thought of a goal in his life—to collect all Eevee evolutions.

Most Eevee evolutions require Evolution Stones, with the exception of Sylveon, Espeon, and Umbreon. To gather the other Eevee types, he'd need five Evolution Stones.

These are Fire, Water, Thunder, Leaf, and Ice.

Currently, he only had one Water Stone, which Teddiursa found and gifted him before.

Although he didn't have an Eevee yet, it was always good to be prepared.

Ending the call, D began contemplating his current situation.

There were still 10 days left until the horse racing event.

According to Mayor Thomas, everyone could buy a limited amount of betting coupons.

These betting coupons could be used to bet on the contestants they supported, earning more coupons, and then exchanged for items.

Apart from that, one could also participate in the race themselves. Besides the Champion Trophy, the winner could select a free item as a reward.

If confident enough, they could even bet the coupons on themselves.

The question was, in those ten days, would he come across a Horse-type Pokémon?

Even if he did, could he nurture it to a level capable of winning the championship?

Hmm, that was a question indeed.

---

---

---

---

# 110: Chapter 108: Preparations

Speaking of Horse-type Pokemon, D's favorite, and the one he currently has the strength to capture, is Rapidash.

There are two kinds of Rapidash: one is the regular fiery type, and the other is the Galar Region form, the Galarian Rapidash.

This Pokemon has blazing flames on its back and mane, looking both cool and stylish as it runs.

If a Trainer wears a helmet and rides it, they could start cosplaying as an Undead Knight right away.

However, these horses inhabit vast plains, and their numbers aren't many, so capturing them poses a certain challenge.

Hanging up the phone, D turned to the kitchen to continue preparing dinner for the Pokemon.

There are still many things waiting for him at the ranch, like setting up supports in the fields for the growth of hops as the weather gets hotter and summer approaches.

The paths in the ranch that were damaged while the Pokemon were playing also await his repair.

Besides that, he also has to take care of the Pokemon on the ranch.

In such circumstances, he simply can't leave for long.

"Maybe I should just forget it this time," D thought.

After all, it's only his first year in this world, and thinking it over, there's no rush to participate in the horse race contest.

There's plenty of time in the future, and ten days is just too short. Even if he captures a Horse-type Pokemon, there wouldn't be enough time to train it properly.

Moreover, he hasn't even developed food that can make Pokemon run faster.

Life moves slowly, and if he misses this year, there's always next year.

Besides participating in the horse race, there was something else D wanted to do.

That is to go to Glaseado Mountain to see if he could encounter some Ice Type Pokemon that suits his fancy and bring it back to the ranch.

At this moment, the oil in the pot was heated, and D used tongs to place two fresh steaks onto it.

The frying pan immediately emitted a sizzling sound.

Once the steaks turned golden, D skillfully flipped them and sprinkled some black pepper and other seasonings on top.

Soon, two sizzling, juicy steaks were ready.

The aroma of the cooked steaks wafted through the room, and D served them onto a plate, handing it to Koraidon and Cyclizar, asking them to eat on the side.

Next, he had to prepare meals for Dragonite, Pawmi, and Beautifly.

The sky outside had already darkened, and after a day of playing, the many Pokemon said goodbye to their companions and returned to their little nests.

After dinner, D, as usual, took a flashlight to patrol the ranch grounds.

Seeing that Miltank, Nacli, Gogoat, and the hens were all fine, he went back inside.

He took off his boots, prepared to wash up and then go to bed.

Just then, D suddenly noticed something was missing from the windowsill.

He looked around and turned to Pawmi, asking, "Have you seen where Smoliv went?"

"Pawmi..."

Pawmi shook its head.

Noticing this, D paused what he was doing and went to the Gogoat's pen.

Sure enough, he saw Smoliv lying on the grass there.

It was cuddled up on a patch of grass, taking a nap. Hearing the approaching footsteps, Smoliv was startled awake, instinctively opening its eyes.

Seeing that it was D, it relaxed again.

"Not going back to sleep?" D asked.

"Smoliv~"

Smoliv gently shook its head, indicating that it liked this place and preferred staying here.

"Alright then."

Seeing this, D was a bit helpless but said nothing further.

He always chose to respect the Pokemon's wishes.

So from that day forward, Smoliv officially moved into the pen to live with Gogoat.

————

The next morning, after finishing the tasks on the ranch, D rode Cyclizar into town.

The temperature at Glaseado Mountain is very low, covered in snow all year round. Without adequate preparation, it's not advisable to head there.

Therefore, upon arriving in town, D went to the store and bought a set of thermal gear.

The set consisted of four parts: a thermal coat, gloves, windproof goggles, and snow boots. These are essentials for going to Glaseado Mountain.
