In addition, he also needed to purchase some portable kerosene stoves for lighting fires.

Once everything was ready, D didn't set off immediately but went to Keri's cottage to consult her about the ice cellar.

After listening, Keri seemed a bit surprised and slowly said:

"Hmm, usually when you're expanding a house, you can choose to build a cellar underground, but turning a cellar into an ice cellar might affect the house above, you know, cold air can seep through the ground and spread into the house."

D thought about it and realized she was right, so he continued to ask, "What if I dug an ice cellar in another part of the ranch?"

"I wouldn't recommend you do that."

After thinking for a moment, Keri offered her suggestion:

"Rather than digging a new hole on the ranch to make an ice cellar, I suggest you remodel the cave behind your house into an ice cave."

"If I remember correctly, you're currently using it to grow mushrooms, right?" Keri asked again.

D nodded. The cave contained several mushroom cultivation trays, and every few days, some mushrooms would grow.

The types of mushrooms were common and ordinary. Initially, D was very intrigued and would pick some mushrooms for making soup or cooking.

Later, when the novelty wore off, he ended up throwing all the mushrooms into the shipping box for Zack to sell in town.

Now that he thought about it, there really was no better place to convert into an ice cellar.

And it wouldn't require much work; with an Ice Type Pokemon moving in, a natural ice cellar would gradually form.

"Got it," D thanked Keri.

Keri smiled and seized the opportunity to promote her furniture:

"I just got a new batch of professional cooling equipment. Are you interested? They're not expensive, only..."

D replied helplessly, "I'm planning to capture an Ice Type Pokemon to help with the remodel."

"What about power generation facilities?" Keri continued her pitch: "They can absorb and store the electricity released by Pokemon, and if the Pokemon's energy is sufficient, it can power the whole ranch!"

Hmm?

Hearing this, D was instantly intrigued.

Seeing his expression, Keri's smile grew even more radiant: "It's not expensive, only thirty thousand League coins for one unit."

Thirty thousand...

The price was indeed reasonable.

If he had this power generation device, it would save him a huge amount on electricity bills in the future.

The ranch has Pawmi, an Electric Type Pokemon.

Dragonite can also use Electric-Type moves, so borrowing some electricity from them shouldn't be a problem.

With this thought, D immediately planned to buy one and have it installed.

This way, if there's another power outage due to a typhoon or other severe weather, he could generate his own electricity.

---

---

---

---

# 111: Chapter 109: Glaseado Mountain

D straightforwardly paid the money.

Keri then instructed a Machamp to move the power generator out and onto a truck.

Keri got into the car, poked her head out from the driver's seat window, and shouted, "You go back first, I'll follow right behind you!"

"Got it!"

D responded and then rode the Cyclizar back to the ranch.

Not long after, Keri also arrived there.

Machamp unloaded the power generator from the truck, and Keri looked around before asking, "Where should we install it? It's best if it can directly connect to the main power supply."

D lifted his foot and led the way forward, "Follow me!"

He planned to place the power generator in the back storage room because the house's power box was there.

It turned out, Keri was not only a skilled architect but also an excellent electrician.

Watching her skillfully install the power generator and connect the electric wires, D showed a surprised expression on his face.

"I never thought you had this skill too."

Keri explained, "That's because I've been living alone since I was a child and gradually learned it."

D was taken aback, "What about your parents?"

Keri shrugged, "They passed away twenty years ago. They used to be the town's carpenters, so I naturally inherited their work when I grew up."

D opened his mouth, apologizing to her, "Sorry."

Keri seemed very indifferent, "Birth, old age, sickness, and death are natural; there's no need to apologize for such things."

"I'm doing well now, running a shop with my own hands, with Machamp and the others helping me. I think if they were still around, they would be proud of me for being this capable!"

D was slightly stunned, then admired, "Yes, you are amazing!"

Keri smiled, and after finishing the installation of the power generator, she stood up and walked outside, into the cave used for mushroom cultivation.

Looking at everything inside, Keri turned back and asked, "Do you still need these mushroom culturing trays? If not, you can leave them for me to reclaim!"

"Reclaim?"

Keri nodded, "Although it's cheaper than buying them, it can also help you clear out these useless things!"

Mushrooms have extremely demanding growth requirements, and since the cave was transformed into an ice cave, there was no suitable place for them to grow anymore at the ranch, leaving them as trash to be thrown away in the end.

D thought of this, nodding, "Then, they're yours!"

Keri smiled slightly, then began to move the mushroom culturing trays from the cave to the truck with Machamp.

After handling everything, Keri pulled out her wallet from the driver's seat, took out some League money, and handed it to D:

"See you later!"

After saying that, she drove the truck away.

Meanwhile, D started to pack up, preparing to go to Glaseado Mountain with Koraidon.

He put the lunch he'd prepared in advance into the fridge, instructing the Cyclizar to distribute it to everyone at noon.

The Cyclizar nodded obediently in agreement.

Seeing this, D put cold-resistant clothing and some Berry Milk into his backpack to regain strength.

The geographical environment of Glaseado Mountain is special, and the temperature there is likely to be very low.

He didn't know if Koraidon could withstand it.

At this moment, D suddenly thought of the first dish he cooked after arriving in this world.

—Hot noodle soup.

If he remembered correctly, the dish's effect was just cold resistance, which would be very helpful for the upcoming destination.

Thinking of this, D put down his backpack again and, under Koraidon's puzzled gaze, went to the fridge, took out the necessary ingredients, and began cooking.

He chopped scallions into sections, prepared two poached eggs in advance, then started a fire to boil water before placing the noodles in the boiling water.

Then he sprinkled a layer of scallion flowers on top, laid down two poached eggs, and finally dripped a few drops of sesame oil.

Soon, a complete bowl of hot noodle soup was ready.

D looked down and the effect of the hot noodle soup emerged.

[Hot Noodle Soup (B+): A simple dish, but a warm choice for the spleen and stomach in a cold winter.

Cooking Effect: Cold Resistance lv3, capable of enduring severe cold, warming the body after consumption for a duration of 6 hours.

Rating: Slight]

"Indeed, with full ingredients and seasonings, the dish's effect has been enhanced," D thought.

B+, a whole one and a half grade improvement compared to the original C-, with cold resistance duration increasing from lv1 to lv3, extending from 30 minutes to 6 hours.

"Want to try it?"

D placed the bowl of noodles in front of Koraidon.

Sniffing the bowl of somewhat plain noodle soup, Koraidon's nose twitched, the rich aroma was overwhelming, making it swallow hard.

"Ah gas?"

This bowl of noodles seemed different from last time?

Koraidon was a bit perplexed, and under D's gaze, it lifted the bowl and gulped down the soup and noodles.

After finishing, Koraidon immediately felt its entire body begin to warm up.

It knew this was the magic effect of the noodles.

Watching Koraidon devour it, D felt a bit speechless.

He quickly finished his own bowl of hot noodle soup, then took out two Lucky Puddings from the fridge, one for himself and one for Koraidon.

Hoping their luck would be good enough to encounter desired Ice Type Pokemon today.

Once everything was ready, D rode Koraidon heading toward Glaseado Mountain.

This path was familiar to Koraidon because two months earlier, it had accompanied D to fish at the end of this road.

That place was Casseroya Lake, and Glaseado Mountain was located on the east side of Casseroya Lake.

Glaseado Mountain is the highest peak in the Paldea Region; due to its high altitude, the temperature there is extremely low, causing snowflakes to constantly drift down from Glaseado Mountain's summit, covering the vast mountains with white snow year-round.

This weather affected the living habits of the humans residing nearby.

They even named the town Montenevera Town, signifying a town of snow and soul defense.

Such a unique geographical environment also created a very special Pokemon ecology on Glaseado Mountain.

The vast majority residing there are Ice Type Pokemon.

Koraidon's speed was fast, its crimson figure streaking across the sky, quickly reaching Casseroya Lake.

D raised his head, gazing at the distant high mountain shrouded in mist, then asked Koraidon, "Are you scared?"

Koraidon shook its head, "Ah gas!"

"Great, then let's keep going!"

Koraidon picked up speed, charging into Glaseado Mountain.

Its crimson figure stood out conspicuously in the vast white snowy world.

---
