# 112: Chapter 110: The Strange Trainer on the Snowy Mountain

When D entered Glaseado Mountain, thirty minutes had passed, and snow began to fall from the sky.

White snowflakes swirled down from the peak.

This snowfall covered the entire Glaseado Mountain, and as far as the eye could see, it was a world dressed in a silver-white blanket.

The temperature here was extremely low, and the breath turned into white mist the moment it hit the air, dissipating quickly.

Thanks to the effect of the hot soup, the cold-resistant suit had not yet been needed.

Since the effect of "Cold Resistance" lasts for 6 hours, D only wore a thin jacket into the mountain.

Koraidon slowed its pace, gliding in mid-air while D looked down to observe the situation below.

The entire mountain was covered with a thick layer of white snow, and occasionally pine trees could be seen standing amidst the white.

These are very hardy trees that can grow normally even in environments as cold as minus fifty degrees.

The trunk was upright, and the pine needles were dark green, adding a splash of green to this place.

Perhaps due to the weather, very few Pokemon were wandering in the wild.

At this time, the wild Pokemon inhabiting this mountain range were likely all hiding in their cozy nests.

Either sleeping, gazing at the snowy scenery outside, or snuggling with companions for warmth, licking each other's fur.

If it were him, in such extreme weather, D thought he would choose to stay home and not go out.

Looking at the vast expanse of white below, D pondered as he patted Koraidon's back and said to it, "Let's land first!"

The body colors of Ice Type Pokemon are mostly white or light blue, making them easily blend into the snowy landscape when viewed from above, and carelessness might let them slip by unnoticed.

Therefore, D planned to explore from the ground.

"Ah ga hiss!"

Koraidon received the command and immediately descended onto the snow below.

D jumped down from Koraidon's back.

The pair walked on the snow, D's black leather boots making a crunching sound, and with every step Koraidon took, it left several footprints.

D turned back and found Koraidon's footprints quite cute, a bit like a dog's.

Just then, Koraidon seemed to notice something and alerted D, "Ah ga hiss!"

D looked up and saw a creature wriggling on the snow not far away.

It was a Pokemon resembling a moth larva, snow-white all over, covered in ice crystals, moving while eating the snow on the ground.

"A Snom?"

D recognized the Pokemon's name.

Snom, an Ice and Bug Type Pokemon.

He remembered that this type of Pokemon loves to eat freshly fallen, soft white snow, and the more they eat, the sharper the ice spikes on their back become.

In reality, Snorunt looked very cute, its white round body like a dumpling.

"Snom won't do." D shook his head silently.

He came out this time to find a Pokemon suitable for making an ice cellar, and Snom was obviously unsuitable because there wasn't enough snow on the ranch for it to eat.

"Let's go and have a look ahead!" D said.

Avoiding Snom, they began to delve deeper into Glaseado Mountain.

About five minutes later, D and Koraidon climbed over a low hill and finally saw the silhouette of a second Ice Type Pokemon under a snow-covered pine tree.

—Snorunt.

Under the pine tree, there were five Snorunts in total, lively and jumping around under the tree, seemingly playing games.

Just then, one of the Snorunts sensed the presence of strangers.

It turned its head and saw a human accompanied by a fearsome Pokemon.

The red Pokemon emitted an imposing pressure, and the Snorunt jumped up in fright, hastily turning to flee.

The other Snorunts followed suit and quickly vanished in the snow.

D: "..."

Koraidon: "Ah ga hiss..."

With the Snorunts fled, they had no choice but to look for other Pokemon.

After walking for about ten minutes, a chilling Ice River appeared before D's eyes.

By the river, D finally saw an Ice Type Pokemon suitable for the ranch.

It was an Avalugg.

Its appearance was like a large turtle, its whole body hard and pale like an iceberg, with a pair of amber eyes.

Avalugg could release icy breaths to freeze the ground into an ice surface, a perfect ability for making an ice cellar.

"This is it!"

D felt a surge in his heart and took an empty Poke Ball from his pocket.

However, before he could step forward, the Avalugg moved.

It moved its heavy ice feet into the Ice River, then the entire massive body entered the river.

Like an iceberg collapsing, it sent a wave surging on the river's surface.

When the river's surface returned to calmness, Avalugg was floating steadily on top.

It turned to look at the riverbank, and only then did D notice a few other Pokemon on the snowy ground by the river.

They were a group of small Bergmites.

Seeing Avalugg successfully enter the water, the group of Bergmite cheered and leapt onto its back.

Once all the Bergmites were aboard, Avalugg began to swim along the river.

Its body, floating on the river surface, was like a colossal ice aircraft carrier.

---

Staring at this scene, D was filled with questions.

What are they doing?

"Koraidon, follow them and take a look!"

Riding on Koraidon, D tracked the group of Bergmite and Avalugg from above.

The Avalugg was already floating downstream, moving very quickly, and in about thirty minutes, they finally reached the end of the river.

There was the Paldea North Sea, where the river flowed down from Glaseado Mountain and eventually merged into the Paldea Sea.

Carrying the Bergmite on its back, the Avalugg entered the Paldea Sea and slowly drifted away.

Koraidon landed on a mountain peak, watching that scene.

Watching the figure of Avalugg disappear into the vast ocean, Koraidon tilted its head, a little confused: "Ahka-sii?"

D was also puzzled: "I don't know what they're doing either."

Although he could clearly remember the names of every Pokemon, when it came to some not-so-popular Pokemon, that was about it.

He wasn't very familiar with their specific ecology.

At that moment, a voice came from behind, explaining the scene to him.

"That is Avalugg migrating."

D turned around to see a man in a down jacket walking over with an Incineroar.

The man curiously stared at Koraidon, circled around it, and asked full of novelty: "What kind of Pokemon is this? I've never seen it before!"

"Uh..." D introduced, "It's called Koraidon."

"Wow, look at those solid muscles, that strong body, it's so powerful, so powerful!" The man said enviously with admiration, "Please tell me, where did you capture this kind of Pokemon?!"

D: "..." Could he have run into a weirdo?

Noticing the strange look from the other side, the man then realized he was being inappropriate. He quickly withdrew his gaze, coughed twice, and introduced himself:

"I'm Everett from the Alola Region, currently exploring this snowy mountain!"

D's gaze turned to the Incineroar beside him, "D, a local of Paldea, you just mentioned Avalugg was migrating, what do you mean by that?"

Everett explained: "This is the habit of the Avalugg kind of Pokemon, they often carry Bergmite over the ocean to change residences."

Is that so?

D fell into contemplation.

The distances between each region are very far, crossing the sea to change residence, won't they encounter any dangers on the way?

Like sea storms, tsunamis, or such.

At this time, Everett's gaze shifted from Koraidon to him.

"I've wanted to ask since the beginning, aren't you cold dressed like this?"

Hearing this, D came back to his senses and looked down at his own clothing.

A thin tracksuit, a pair of jeans, and leather boots.

He paused for a moment and replied, "It's okay."

"Young people really have energy to burn!" The man looked back at Koraidon, enviously asking: "Are you here to explore Glaseado Mountain as well?"

"Sort of!" D said, "I plan to capture an Ice Type Pokemon here, but I haven't found a suitable one yet, the only Avalugg already moved away."

Upon hearing this, the man immediately beamed, enthusiastically pounding his chest: "I can help you; I'm very familiar with the kinds of Pokemon that inhabit around here!"

D sensed the little motives of the other and squinted his eyes: "What do you want?"

The man showed an embarrassed expression: "Let me admire your Koraidon a bit more; its muscles are really strong!"

D: "You really like muscular Pokemon?"

"Exactly!" The man nodded repeatedly, flexing his biceps, his eyes bright, saying: "Exercising muscles with Pokemon is the most wonderful thing in the world! It feels like I've found a like-minded friend!"

D: "..." He didn't quite understand this behavior.

But just looking, exchanging muscle tips, seemed harmless.

So D nodded in agreement, then asked: "Have you seen Alolan Vulpix or Glaceon in Glaseado Mountain?"

Everett: "Haven't seen Alolan Vulpix, but I have seen Glaceon! Follow me; let's head back to my campsite first!"

D nodded and followed him back.

Everett, as he walked, continued to introduce the Pokemon that inhabited nearby.

Like Snorunt, Glalie, Delibird...

Passing by a cave, he pointed inside and said: "These kinds of caves generally have Pokemon inhabiting them."

He carefully observed the cave, then exclaimed joyfully: "This seems to be where that Glaceon lives!"

"Really?" D instantly perked up.

"Exactly! It's right here!" Everett nodded, exuding an air of recklessness, and dove headfirst into the cave.

D also wanted to go in and take a look, but soon, an angry roar came from inside the cave.

Immediately after, Everett fled out in a panic, running and shouting: "Oh, sorry Beartic! I didn't mean to disturb your rest!"

Chasing after him was a fierce-looking Beartic.

D: "..."

Why does this guy feel somewhat unreliable?

At the critical moment, Incineroar stood in front of Everett.

Noticing the Pokemon emitting hot air, Beartic's expression turned serious.

---

Everett hid behind Incineroar, apologizing shyly to it, "I'm really sorry!"

Beartic said nothing, exhaled a breath of cold air, and turned back into the cave.

Everett let out a long sigh of relief, "I was scared to death; the Pokemon here are too fierce."

D watched him silently, pondering whether he should leave.

At this moment, Everett noticed his expression and quickly explained, "I made a mistake, but I promise, there's definitely a Glaceon nearby! My campsite is just ahead, let's go there first!"

Forget it, let's follow and see.

D nodded.

The two continued onward and soon arrived at a campsite nestled under a cliff, with a campfire burning, looking very safe.

Returning to this spot, Everett let out a sigh of relief and then took out several Poke Balls to throw them out, "Everyone, come out!"

With flashes of white light, a Machamp, a Grimmsnarl, and an Obstagoon appeared in the camp.

D scanned them at a glance; all the Pokemon were muscular types.

In his heart, he exclaimed, good heavens, then said, "You're just missing an Urshifu and a Buzzwole."

Hearing this, Everett's attention was immediately caught, "Urshifu? Buzzwole?"

"Huh?" D was a bit surprised. He looked at the Obstagoon and Grimmsnarl and asked, "You've never been to the Galar Region? It's said that Champion Mustard has an Urshifu, and it's also a muscular type of Pokemon. As for Buzzwole..."

Saying this, D suddenly realized it was still early, and he wasn't sure if people in the Alola Region were aware of the Ultra Beast.

After pondering for a moment, he didn't reveal more information, just said, "Both of these are well-built, not inferior to Incineroar."

Everett nodded, then shook his head, "I've heard of Mr. Mustard, but I'm not very familiar with his Pokemon."

He memorized the names of these two Pokemon and decided to inquire about them later.

Everett took out a map of Glaseado Mountain from his backpack, compared the positions on it, then said:

"Found it, Glaceon is right here! I have a habit of marking rare Pokemon on my map! But that Glaceon is very wary, and quite aloof toward humans. I think capturing it will be rather challenging."

"No worries, I'd like to give it a try," D said, "You know, Eevee is such a cute Pokemon, and like you, I also have a goal."

"Oh? What is it?"

"To collect all types of Eevee!"

To this, Everett seemed not to understand, "Are those small, weak Pokemon really that good? Something like Incineroar, tall and powerful, is much better!"

D argued, "It's precisely because they're small that they're cute! They're fluffy, and it's so comfortable to sleep with them in winter. Plus, they're so adorable, just seeing them is satisfying."

Having said that, D closed his mouth.

Because he suddenly realized that for Everett, he could also sleep hugging Incineroar and Obstagoon.

It seemed no different from his liking for Eevee...

"Forget it, let's not talk about this."

Confused, D simply gave up the discussion and changed the topic, "Tell me Glaceon's habitat!"

Everett set the map aside, looked eagerly at Koraidon, and said:

"I suppose I could, but before that, I'd like to have a battle with that Pokemon, to see whose muscles are stronger!"

Hearing this, D was a bit taken aback. He glanced at Koraidon beside him, then at the Pokemon beside Everett, and asked:

"Are you sure?"

---

---

---

---

# 113: Chapter 111: How Could You Possibly Be a Country Farmer?!

"Of course!"

Everett found this statement a bit strange, but he didn't think much of it and instead said enthusiastically:

"Pokemon battles have to be bone-crunching to be exciting! The scars left on muscles will also become badges of honor! Come on, D, show me those powerful muscles of yours!"

As he spoke, he struck a pose like a contestant in a bodybuilding competition, showing off his muscles.

Perhaps due to spending so much time together, the Incineroar and others were somewhat influenced by their trainer.

Upon hearing Everett's words, they all uniformly began to flex their muscles, emitting low growls from their mouths.

"Roar! Ha! Hey!"

D: "..."

At that moment, it was as if a prompt saying "Muscleman Everett has come to challenge you!" popped into his mind.

However...

He didn't even know what moves Koraidon could use, let alone how to command it in battle.

After a moment of silence, D glanced at Koraidon.

Koraidon didn't have any problems.

Without needing D to speak, it stood up from its sprinting posture on all fours, taking a few steps forward to stand in front of D, gazing at the Incineroar opposite.

"Aaaahhh!"

D's gaze followed, meeting Everett's in mid-air.

The moment the trainers' eyes met, the battle was about to begin!

Everett, full of fighting spirit, pointed forward: "Incineroar, use Roar!"

The command was given, and Incineroar lifted its head and chest, letting out an intimidating roar towards Koraidon.

"Awoooo!"

Koraidon: ?

It stood there, somewhat puzzled, looking at the opponent.

As the roar ended, Incineroar moved.

Its sharp claws suddenly unsheathed from its pads, and its strong leg muscles powered it forward, lunging at Koraidon.

Watching the charging Incineroar, Koraidon slightly sidestepped, easily dodging its claws that swiped past.

"What? That speed?!" Everett's eyes widened in shock.

Then, Koraidon moved.

It leaped up from the snowy ground, its body glowing with a crimson light, the tendrils on its head transforming into wing-like shapes, changing form as it descended ferociously.

"Danger!" Alarms blared in Everett's mind as he quickly warned, "Dodge quickly!"

But it was too late.

Noticing the movement behind, Incineroar turned around immediately.

The next second, its body was enveloped by an ancient and fierce aura.

With a thunderous boom, a violent explosion erupted in the snowy area.

The noise was tremendous, causing the snow on the nearby pine trees to fall in a flurry.

Moments later, the disturbed snow gently settled back to the ground.

When everything calmed down, the first thing in view was Koraidon's towering crimson figure.

Incineroar, on the other hand, lay motionless on the ground, clearly having lost its ability to battle.

Everett stared at the scene, his face full of disbelief.

Defeated in one move?

Was that Pokemon's strength too exaggerated?!

At this moment, Everett suddenly understood the meaning behind D's earlier question, "Are you sure?"

He asked cautiously, "Hold on!"

D patted Koraidon's head next to him and, upon hearing Everett's words, was a bit puzzled: "What?"

Everett exclaimed, "You're not one of the Gym Leaders or an Elite from the Paldea Region, are you?"

"Uh..." D replied frankly, "Would you believe me if I said I'm just a countryside farmer?"

Everett shook his head repeatedly.

No way?

He thought to himself, how could a countryside farmer raise such a powerful Pokemon?

D offered no further explanation, simply saying, "If you head south of Glaseado Mountain, there's D Ranch west of Porto Marinada Town, selling fresh milk that helps Pokemon grow stronger. If you're interested, you can check it out."

This was a marketing technique he learned from Keri.

Seeing he wasn't joking, Everett was even more shocked.

"No way, are you serious?"

"Mhm." D nodded, then pointed at Koraidon's muscles, striking a muscle-flexing pose with its arm: "See these muscles? They're from drinking milk every day."

Koraidon seemed a bit reluctant but still cooperated with D's actions.

Everett looked utterly stunned.

If that's true, Paldea people are terrifying...

A mere countryside farmer with such strong power.

"Sigh, alright, I lost." Everett sighed, "It seems your trained muscles are far superior; we're still a long way off..."

He retrieved Incineroar into its Poke Ball to let it rest.

Having lost convincingly, Everett realized from the first glance that Koraidon was not an ordinary Pokemon.

Now it seemed, he wasn't wrong at all!

In its standing form, its muscles were even more robust, and upon closer look, one could see the fine red scales covering its muscles.

Taking a deep breath, Everett quickly regained his composure:

"Well then, let me tell you the location of Glaceon!"

He took out a map, unfolded it on a folding stool.

D leaned in to examine it.

Everett pointed to a marked spot on the map and said:

"That's where you'll find Glaceon. Head south from the camp, and you'll see Glaseado's Grasp, one of the top ten sights in Paldea. It's a Three-Point Pass, with a cave beneath the cliffs. I spotted Glaceon around there before."

Glaseado's Grasp...

D nodded, memorizing the location.

Then, he mounted Koraidon and bid farewell to Everett.

Watching him, Everett was a bit perplexed: "The terrain there is very steep; it's hard for Pokemon to run on."

D didn't say much but merely smiled. He gently patted Koraidon's neck.

The next second, the flashy feathers on Koraidon's head transformed into wings.

With a forceful push from its hind legs, its massive body leaped high into the air, the wings naturally spreading, beginning to soar southward.

Everett stared wide-eyed at this scene.

Is that even possible?

That Pokemon is just too versatile!

Not only is it powerful and muscular, but it can also run, jump, and even carry the trainer in flight.

Plus, judging by the air sac on its chest, swimming might not be a challenge for it either.

Envy washed over Everett.

How could there be such a perfect Pokemon in this world?!

He yearned for one too...

At this moment, D in mid-air waved down at Everett: "Goodbye, Everett!"

Hearing this voice, Everett snapped back to reality.

Recalling the events of the day and D's words, he couldn't help but murmur:

"D Ranch, huh? No, I must verify it for myself!"

He still didn't quite believe D was just a countryside farmer.

---

---

---

---

# 114: Chapter 112: The Hidden Cavern Beneath Glaseado's Grasp

Unbeknownst to them, the snow on Glaseado Mountain had stopped.

The surrounding temperature dropped a few more degrees, but fortunately, the Cold Resistance hadn't worn off yet.

It had been three hours since he left the ranch, which meant he had about two more hours to stay here.

That's enough! D thought.

Koraidon was flying through the sky at a leisurely pace.

Although the snow had stopped, the sky remained a vast expanse of white, with no trace of sunlight.

Occasionally, a few Delibird would flap their wings and fly past, their tails bulging like little packages.

D couldn't help but be drawn to those tails.

From what he knew, the tails of Delibird were hollow, and they would place food inside to transport.

To feed the waiting chicks in their nests atop cliffs, these Pokemon that dwelled in frigid regions were always moving food.

Compared to Pokemon like Froslass, which actively attacked humans, Delibird could be considered very kind-hearted.

They shared food with both humans and Pokemon and would voluntarily offer a portion of their food to people stranded in the snowy mountains to aid them.

It's said that every year, many people endangered in the snowy mountains are saved thanks to food from Delibird.

In real life, Delibird are quite endearing, and for some Trainers, they are great Pokemon partners.

Even in the Specials, the Delibird raised by the Masked Man Pryce once achieved the incredible feat of defeating Ho-oh.

Just then, D suddenly sensed a gaze upon himself.

He turned to see a Delibird flying behind him.

When it realized it had been noticed, the Delibird waved its red wings at him, then sped up to reach his side, using its wings to pull out two Berries from its tail and handing them to D.

"Hmm?"

Seeing this scene, D was slightly taken aback.

His mind blanked for a second before he realized that covered in snow and dressed so thinly, the Delibird had mistaken him for a human in distress.

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but find it both amusing and touching.

The Delibird chirped enthusiastically, without seeking any gratitude, and turned to fly away, continuing to gather food.

Seeing this, D quickly called out, "Wait a moment, Delibird!"

"Bibi?"

The Delibird stopped in confusion.

It turned to look at the human and the creature beneath him, then suddenly seemed to realize something.

Those two Berries weren't enough for them!

For such a big Pokemon, a single Berry wouldn't satisfy its hunger.

But today, it had only found five Berries in total...

Having already given away two, it was left with three.

Finding food in the snow was extremely difficult.

Thinking of this, the Delibird felt a bit troubled.

After hesitating for a moment, it took out another Berry from its tail.

Seeing this, D shook his head with a smile.

He returned the Berries to the Delibird, "Thank you very much for the food, but we don't need it. Keep it for yourself!"

"Bibi?"

The Delibird looked a bit puzzled.

D looked at its grasping tail, noticing only two Berries left inside.

What a kind-hearted Delibird it was to give away more than half of its Berries at once.

Since Berries didn't grow in the snow, it meant these Berries had been plucked from far away by the Delibird.

D pondered for a moment, then took out some of the food he had brought in his backpack.

This food was meant to lure the Glaceon back to the ranch.

It included honey buns, Berries, lucky puddings, and ham sandwiches.

Since there was plenty, D decided to give some to this kind-hearted Delibird, thoughtfully placing the food inside its tail.

"Bibi?!"

The Delibird seemed surprised by this abundance of food.

D smiled and said, "I have plenty of food. These are for you!"

"Bibi..."

Just then, Koraidon made a sound, reminding him that they were approaching their destination.

D waved goodbye to the Delibird, "Goodbye, Delibird!"

He gathered his thoughts and focused on the Three-Point Pass ahead.

It was a very unique cliff, shaped like a Pokemon's hand.

For this reason, it was named Glaseado's Grasp by the people of the Paldea Region, and it was one of the "Paldea Ten Sights," the top ten proudest landmarks of the Paldea Region.

Koraidon slowly descended onto the cliff, and D jumped down, standing atop it to observe the surroundings.

Here, he saw several Snorunt and a few Glalie.

But the presence of Glaceon was so much more captivating that these Pokemon didn't draw as much of his interest.

After scanning the area, D found no trace of Glaceon.

After pondering for a moment, he got back on Koraidon and said, "Let's check below!"

"Aga-ss!"

Koraidon called out, spreading its wings as it glided down towards Glaseado's Grasp.

Soon, they landed in a valley at the bottom of the cliff.

This valley, too, was covered in a blanket of white snow, but being shielded by tall cliffs on all sides, it wasn't as cold as above.

Tall pine trees and some hardy plants grew in abundance there.

Near these pine trees, D saw several winter-formed Deerling and Sawsbuck.

The Sawsbuck also noticed him, but given their gentle nature, they merely watched from afar without approaching.

D paid them no mind and looked up to ensure they were directly under Glaseado's Grasp before beginning his search around.

Soon, he found a hidden cave entrance behind an inconspicuous snowdrift.

D's eyes lit up, thinking, "This must be the cave Everett mentioned where he found Glaceon!"

He stepped towards the cave, intending to run straight in, but recalling Everett's encounter being chased by Beartic, he hesitated at the entrance, peering inside.

The cave seemed expansive; the end was not in sight, and there appeared to be forks inside.

"Koraidon, do you sense any strong Pokemon presence?" D asked.

Koraidon sniffed carefully, then shook its head, "Aga-ss."

Not here? Could it be that the Pokemon residing in this cave wasn't home?

It had been snowing until just recently, and the Pokemon might have been out searching for food.

"Let's go in and take a look!"

D began to venture deeper into the cave.

This cave was massive; it wasn't obvious from the outside, but once inside, a chilling sensation could be felt.

The cave was deep, and soon, a fork appeared ahead.

Standing at the fork, D pondered deeply.

Which way to go?

He randomly chose a path and continued to go deeper. The cave inside was eerily silent, with only his and Koraidon's breathing audible.

If he listened closely, he could even hear a few drops of water dripping.

It was then that Koraidon seemed to have discovered something and glanced towards the downward path at the fork.

"Aga-ss?"

D paused slightly and asked, "What's the matter, did you find something?"

Koraidon nodded its big head and led the way forward.

After about ten minutes of walking, they passed through a narrow, dark passage, and the space ahead suddenly widened.

This seemed to be a hidden Underground Cave.

Stalactites hung from the ceiling like icicles, and not far ahead lay a deep blue lake exuding white cold air.

In the center of the lake was a "Lake Island," upon which stood a crystal blue ice rock.

Koraidon's gaze settled on that ice rock.

It felt a powerful surge of Ice Type Energy emanating from it!

D also looked toward it.

Huh? What is that?

He mounted Koraidon, and with a leap, Koraidon landed on the Lake Island.

Just as D was about to take a closer look at the rock, a cold growl suddenly sounded from behind.

"Bui!"

---

---

---

---

# 115: Chapter 113: Glaceon and the Ice Stone

Hearing a sound, D swiftly turned his head.

Immediately, at the narrow entrance of the cave, he saw a Pokemon that looked somewhat like a fox.

Its entire body was covered in a layer of light blue fur, its head appearing as if adorned with a double-layered woven hat for warmth, blue fur growing on its forehead, extending like braids to the front.

It had four legs, with dark blue fur on its feet, looking as if it were wearing blue boots.

That was... Glaceon!

It had appeared there unnoticed, perhaps having followed closely behind him and Koraidon.

Everett was indeed telling the truth!

There truly was a Glaceon residing under Glaseado's Grasp!

D's eyes lit up instantly.

So next, it was time to consider how to capture this Glaceon.

With this in mind, D looked up at the Glaceon.

Its expression was vigilant, its gaze holding a hint of anger as it looked over.

It seemed that to this Glaceon, he and Koraidon were invaders trespassing into its territory.

Glaceon crouched slightly, taking a stance of attack, issuing a warning sound: "Bui!"

D: "..."

He was about to explain that it was a misunderstanding, but before he could react, Glaceon suddenly raised its head warily to look upward.

D also turned to look back, spotting a Froslass in a small ice cave on the rock wall behind him.

The Froslass observed the scene below with serene composure.

Its gaze shifted between the Glaceon and the suddenly appearing human, finally resting on a stone on the Lake Island that emitted a white chill.

The next moment, the Froslass moved, a blue light surfacing in its eyes.

Suddenly, D saw blue light manifest on the stone beside him.

"Bui!"

Witnessing this, Glaceon sprinted over rapidly.

Chunks of ice instantly formed around it, speeding towards the Froslass.

The ice shards hit the target successfully, having little effect but managing to disrupt the Froslass's movements.

Glaceon leaped with great agility, hopping onto the Lake Island, standing firmly on the stone, proclaiming its sovereignty to the Froslass fearlessly.

"Bui!"

This was its treasure, and it would absolutely not allow anyone to covet it!

Witnessing this scene, D instantly understood.

The Froslass was vying with the Glaceon for that stone containing Ice Type Energy!

So, was it a massive Ice Stone, or an Ice Stone?

Regardless of which it was, it contained Ice Type Energy, which had enormous appeal to Ice Type Pokemon formed naturally.

Though hit by the ice shards, the Froslass's gaze remained as calm as still water.

Its appearance initially gave the impression of a gentle and tender, kimono-clad graceful lady.

Yet its actions were far from gentle.

Froslass looked icily at the scene below, lightly raising its right hand to gather a pitch-black Shadow Ball, hurling it at Glaceon.

The Shadow Ball seemed powerful, and to prevent D from being caught in it, Koraidon unhesitatingly lifted him up by the waist, jumping away from the Lake Island.

They avoided the attack just in time, but Glaceon wasn't so lucky.

In a bid to protect its precious treasure, it failed to dodge, being directly hit by the Shadow Ball, its small body knocked to the ground.

Glaceon cried out in pain: "Bui..."

After setting D down, Koraidon turned back toward the Lake Island.

But the Froslass seemed unwilling to stop.

Almost tangible Telekinesis surged wildly from it, transforming into a powerful shockwave, sweeping towards Glaceon.

Watching this, D shouted: "Koraidon!"

Koraidon's body moved simultaneously.

It leaped forward, its tall frame landing in front of Glaceon, blocking the attack with its arms, roaring upwards: "Kashi!"

A powerful pressure instantly overwhelmed the Froslass.

Fear showed in its eyes.

It didn't want to provoke the human and the Pokemon, hence its attack target had always been solely Glaceon.

Now it seemed, the human decided to intervene, choosing to help Glaceon.

Yet... Froslass looked somewhat unwillingly at the stone that attracted it.

After weighing things over repeatedly, it finally turned and left the place.

Seeing the Froslass leave, Koraidon turned back to look at Glaceon.

A silly smile appeared on its face, asking: "Kashi?"

Glaceon stared blankly at the big guy that had protected it: "Bui…?"

D looked happily at Koraidon and Glaceon over there.

Just then, Koraidon's speed was so fast, giving him the illusion that even without his command, it would go over to protect Glaceon.

Anyway, as long as Glaceon was alright, that was good.

D stood on the shore, shouting toward the Lake Island: "Hey!"

Hearing his voice, Koraidon came over to take him to the Lake Island.

D looked at Glaceon, taking out a bottle of Berry Milk from his backpack and offered it to Glaceon.

"Drink it, once you do your injuries will heal!"

"Bui…" Glaceon hesitated for a moment, then turned its head aside, surprisingly refusing the Berry Milk: "Bui."

---

It agilely turned around and ran away, then came back with a small stone with snowflake-like patterns in its mouth and dropped it in front of D.

After doing this, Glaceon jumped onto that Lake Island emanating a white chill, lay down on it, and closed its eyes as if it was resting.

"It's just as Everett said, so cold..."

D looked down at the Ice Stone, and couldn't help but smile.

Although it had a cold attitude towards him on the surface, it was a Glaceon that knew how to express its gratitude.

D grew even more fond of this Glaceon in his heart.

Moreover, his original purpose in coming here was for this Glaceon.

But as it stands, capturing this Glaceon obviously can't be done through ordinary means.

Food doesn't work on it, and it doesn't seem to like battles very much either.

After some thought, D decided to first understand Glaceon's situation a bit better.

He looked at the Ice Stone beneath Glaceon and asked, "Is this stone your treasure?"

Upon hearing this, Glaceon, who was resting with its eyes closed, immediately opened its eyes and looked wary.

Seeing it seemed to misunderstand, D quickly said, "No need to worry, I'm not here to take the stone from you. I just accidentally arrived here."

Koraidon also nodded beside him, "Agaaash!"

Only then did Glaceon relax a bit.

It looked down at the Ice Stone beneath it, and memories of the past flashed through its mind.

At that time, it was still an Eevee.

During an accident, it got lost in the snowy mountains and accidentally stumbled into this place.

Here, it discovered this stone, and influenced by the stone, it evolved into a Glaceon and has lived here ever since.

Treasures...

This stone is indeed its treasure.

Thinking about this, Glaceon gently nodded, "Vee."

"Was that Froslass just now here to fight you for this stone?" D asked again.

Speaking of Froslass, Glaceon appeared a bit angry.

Initially, the Pokemon living nearby were all good, and they weren't attracted to this Ice Stone either.

But suddenly, one day, that Froslass came.

It wanted to take this Ice Stone and claim it for its own.

Before that, they had already fought many times without reaching a conclusion.

Because of this, Glaceon felt somewhat tired of it and really disliked that Froslass.

D observed Glaceon's expression and, seeing that it seemed to value the Ice Stone greatly, suddenly thought of a good idea!

"Since this stone is so important, that Froslass definitely won't give up. It'll come here again to take the stone," D said.

"Vee..." Glaceon's expression grew serious.

Seeing this, D first feigned pondering, then asked, "Have you ever thought of moving this stone to a safe place? Move it somewhere Froslass can't find, then it won't bother you anymore."

Upon hearing this, Glaceon's eyes lit up first.

Right! Just move the stone away, and let that Froslass never find it again!

But soon, it started pondering again.

Where is safe?

The snowy mountains are vast, and they're home to Ice Type Pokemon. If the stone is moved outside, it might attract other Ice Type Pokemon to snatch the stone.

Seeing Glaceon fall into the trap, D timely spoke up, "Ahem, um..."

Glaceon raised its eyes to look at him.

D: "I have a good place where you can hide this stone, it's very safe, and there are no other Ice Type Pokemon."

Glaceon stared at him with those deep blue eyes.

D's heart pounded, wondering if Glaceon had seen through him?

Just then, Koraidon nodded along with what D said, "Agaaash!"

Glaceon looked at Koraidon, at this Pokemon that had just protected it from Froslass's attack.

Glaceon was smart; it could sense that this Pokemon seemed to know it.

What a strange feeling, was it because they met somewhere before?

Glaceon began to think carefully.

But after thinking for a long time, it couldn't find an answer.

It couldn't help but ask, "Vee?"

"Agaaash!" Koraidon nodded its big head joyfully.

D watched this scene with great interest from the side.

It seemed that Glaceon was confirming whether there really was such a safe place with Koraidon.

After getting an answer, Glaceon was slightly stunned, then lowered its head to look at the stone beneath it.

It chose to trust this human and Pokemon who helped it.

"Vee."

D was delighted, "Made up your mind?"

Glaceon nodded.

Upon seeing this, D put the Ice Stone, which was about the same size as Glaceon, into his Space Backpack in front of it.

Perfect, the Ice Stone and Glaceon could be taken back to the cave together.

The Ice Stone, like the Ice Stone, is a tool that can help Eevee evolve into Glaceon.

The difference is, the Ice Stone disappears after use, while the Ice Stone does not. This stone, which is merged with nature, contains a large amount of Ice Type Energy.

Although Glaceon had already completed its evolution, due to the nature of Pokemon, they all like energy-rich places.

Because of this, Glaceon considered the Ice Stone a treasure.

Thinking about this, D clicked his tongue and looked at the Ice Stone in his hand, thinking this trip was indeed fruitful.

Glaceon, Ice Stone, Ice Stone, he wanted them all!

D took out an empty red-and-white ball from his pocket and said to Glaceon:

"The return trip is a bit far, come inside here first!"

Glaceon looked at the red-and-white ball, then reached out with a paw and lightly tapped the button on it.

The next second, its body transformed into a red light and was sucked into the Poke Ball.

When the Poke Ball stopped moving, D couldn't help but let out a long sigh of relief.

Captured Glaceon!

Though... it wasn't really an official capture.

After all, Glaceon was currently following him simply because he could take the Ice Stone to a safe place.

However, D wasn't worried; after all, there's plenty of time in the future!

Once back at the ranch, he would have plenty of time to get along with Glaceon until it acknowledged him.

Packing up his Space Backpack and making sure he left nothing behind, D and Koraidon left the cave.

As Koraidon slowly ascended, the entire Glaseado Mountain gradually shrank until it was completely left behind.

D looked back at the towering mountain range, recalling everything that happened today in his mind.

Once again, he felt the sense of security brought by Koraidon.

It could fly, run, and swim, was very strong, and had an incredibly gentle temperament, totally different from the descriptions of the Wing Lord except for occasionally being a bit silly; it almost had no flaws.

He thought he was very fortunate to encounter such a Pokemon.

Thanks to Koraidon, his ranch had developed to its current state in just a few months.

If it weren't for Koraidon, he might still be arduously fishing to make money.

Not to mention Miltank and Nacli, they probably wouldn't even have a place built for them to live yet.

At this moment, Koraidon seemed to notice D was spacing out on its back.

It turned its head slightly to look at him, "Agaaash?"

D snapped back to reality and happily said to it, "Koraidon, I absolutely love you!"

"Agaaash?!"

Koraidon's eyes widened, seemingly shocked by his words.

It knew that D liked everyone living on the ranch and cared for the many Pokemon living there every day.

But it was the first time it had seen D express his affection for them so straightforwardly.

The past self never received it, Dragonite never received it, Alcremie never received it, and Pawmi never received it either.

Does this mean it was the first Pokemon to receive such an honor?

Koraidon was overjoyed, and this excitement in its heart made it unconsciously fly faster.

Koraidon's agile figure transformed into a red light, like a red meteor streaking across the sky.

D was scared to death and quickly grabbed onto Koraidon, shouting, "Slow down, I'm going to fall off!"

---

---

---

---

# 116: Chapter 114: Teddiursa's Postcard (Third Update)

At three in the afternoon, D smoothly returned to the ranch.

He patted the head of the Cyclizar that approached him and took the Ice Stone and Glaceon to the cave behind the house.

"Come out, Glaceon!"

D took out the Poke Ball. Accompanied by a flash of white light, Glaceon's figure appeared in the somewhat dim and cool cave.

"How about we take a look around here?"

As D spoke, he took the Ice Stone out of his space backpack and placed it in the center of the cave.

"Glee…"

Glaceon curiously looked around.

Hmm, this cave is quite small, and there aren't any strange holes in the rock walls. It looks pretty safe.

Next, we just need to transform this place into an ice cave!

At that moment, a few curious little heads peeked in from outside the cave, looking inside.

These were Alcremie, five Naclis, Butterfree, and Cyclizar.

Sensing so many unfamiliar auras, Glaceon immediately became vigilant. Its body temperature dropped rapidly, and its blue fur froze, transforming into sharp ice needles, as if it were bristling.

D was slightly taken aback, then turned and saw the row of little heads at the cave entrance. He quickly waved his hand and introduced them to Glaceon:

"It's alright, they're also Pokemon living around here, different from Froslass!"

"Glee?" Glaceon looked over at them.

Noticing its gaze, the bubbly Alcremie cheerfully greeted it, "Maa~"

Under its lead, the Naclis all voiced, "Yen, Yen."

Butterfree happily flapped its wings, "Fwee~"

Cyclizar also signaled a welcome with a "Raaow!"

They were used to D occasionally bringing new companions from outside.

Curious about what the new companion was like this time, they all came over to have a look.

Glaceon blinked its eyes, seeing that they were indeed different from Froslass, it relaxed, and its fur returned to being soft.

Seeing this, D happily said, "Alcremie, I'll leave the task of showing our new companion around the ranch to you."

"Maa!"

Alcremie clenched its little hands, promising to accomplish the task!

"Get to know everyone first. I have some things to do, but don't worry, nobody will grab that stone from you," D said.

He planned to go to the town to find Keri to help install a door for the cave.

Without a door, the cold air would escape, and the icehouse couldn't be made.

Entrusting Glaceon to Alcremie, D rode Koraidon to Porto Marinada Town.

After he left, Alcremie enthusiastically started communicating with Glaceon.

"Maa!" (Hello there!)

The Naclis mimicked, "Yen, Yen."

"Glee…"

Glaceon found it all very unfamiliar and didn't know how to respond.

It lay on the Ice Stone, watching the chattering group of Pokemon with open eyes.

Initially, Alcremie was lively, but gradually its tone lowered, until no more words came out.

Seeing no expression on Glaceon's face and no response, Alcremie thought it didn't want to listen, and left the cave with the Naclis and Butterfree, heading to the grass outside.

Watching Alcremie turn away disheartened, Glaceon, who had been listening, opened its mouth as if trying to say something.

But the group of Pokemon had already left the cave.

When D returned from town, he saw Alcremie and the others sitting listlessly on the grass.

He couldn't help but ask, "What happened here?"

"Fwee…"

Butterfree hung its head low, looking towards the cave.

"Did Glaceon hurt you?" asked D.

Butterfree shook its head.

Thinking of Glaceon's cold demeanor from before, D asked, "Did it ignore you?"

If he hadn't found a common topic using the stone, Glaceon probably wouldn't have interacted with him either.

The Pokemon nodded their heads.

"Maa…"

D glanced toward the cave and reassured, "Don't be upset; maybe it's just not used to the environment on its first day here. Give it some time; it's actually a good Pokemon."

Upon hearing this, Alcremie perked right back up.

D chuckled, "That's the Alcremie I know!"

At this point, Keri, who came to install the door, also arrived at the ranch.

D let Alcremie and the others go play, and then took Keri to the cave.

Seeing Glaceon in the cave, Keri was very surprised, "You brought back an Ice Type Pokemon so quickly?"

D nodded, looking into the cave at Glaceon, which lay on the Ice Stone with its eyes closed, seemingly asleep.

Keri just asked casually, and then started installing the wooden door at the cave entrance.

The wooden door was 2.2 meters tall, with a square door hole at the bottom for Pokemon to come and go freely.

If Glaceon wants to bask in the sun, it can slip out through this door hole.

"All done!"

Soon, the door was installed.

Keri opened the door, only to see the inside of the cave pitch black.

D also noticed this. He turned, examining the cave wall, pointing to a weaker spot and asking, "Is it possible to install a window here?"

"Of course." Keri nodded.

Having already planned ahead after hearing D's plans, she brought all the supplies for doors and windows.

With Machamp's help, a hole was quickly opened in that spot.

Keri measured the distance, then began installing the window.

Light streamed through the windows into the room, brightening the icehouse.

"All done, the total is 3000 League Coins!" Keri handed her tools to Machamp, asking it to take them back to the car.

3000 League Coins was quite reasonable, and D paid without hesitation.

Keri smiled as she took the money, got into her car, and waved goodbye to D through the window:

"See you next time! If you have other house renovations, feel free to contact me!"

D: "Will do."

After seeing Keri off, D returned to the cave.

Glaceon seemed to have started influencing the place; in such a short while, the temperature dropped inside, like the air conditioning had been turned on.

No wonder it's an Ice Type Pokemon!

D thought again about Alcremie and the others' earlier state, and couldn't help but say:

"Alcremie and the others didn't mean any harm; they just wanted to be friends. Maybe you could try giving them a little response."

"Glee…" Glaceon thought of Alcremie and the others' earlier actions.

In the wild, it had no friends and was accustomed to being alone.

Respond?

Thinking of this, Glaceon gently nodded, "Glee."

It would give it a try.

"That's the spirit!"

D exhaled with relief, bade farewell to Glaceon, and turned to leave the cave, heading to the front.

Glaceon's situation couldn't be rushed, but D wasn't in a hurry. Knowing this Pokemon was inherently good was enough.

As for now…

D raised his head, gazing at the sunset clouds in the sky.

It's almost dark, and he should prepare dinner for everyone!

Just as D was about to head back to the house, a Pelipper suddenly landed above him.

Looking at this unfamiliar Pelipper, D was a bit surprised.

The Pelipper looked at him too, as if confirming this human's identity. It opened its mouth, dropping a postcard and a small pearl with a beautiful silver sheen.

After delivering the items, Pelipper flew away.

D bent down to pick up the pearl and postcard from the ground.

Though called a postcard, it was actually a piece of patterned stationery.

The stationery had no text, only two distinctly different paw prints.

One resembled a bear's paw, while the other seemed like the paw of a feline.

Seeing these items, two names instantly flashed in D's mind.

It was Teddiursa and Mew!

---

---

---

---

# 117: Chapter 115: Good Friends (Double-Length Chapter)

The small pearl in his hand emitted a beautiful silvery sheen.

Looking at it, D could almost imagine how Teddiursa and Mew managed to find it.

It might have been born or fallen from a pearl-producing Pokemon, finally drifting ashore after many years of being battered by the sea.

At that moment, Teddiursa and Mew, who were on an adventure, happened to pass by and discovered it.

With its Pickup ability, Teddiursa thought this shiny thing was quite pretty, so it picked it up.

To let him know it was a gift, Teddiursa and Mew, who couldn't speak human language, found some paper and left their paw prints on it with muddy hands.

Perhaps it was Teddiursa who came up with the idea, as it was the first to press its little paw print onto the paper.

Mew was attracted by this scene and thought it was fun, so it too left its paw print on the paper.

Afterwards, they entrusted the paper and the pearl to their good friend, Pelipper, whom they had met during their travels, asking it to deliver the items to the ranch in the Paldea region.

However, D was a bit puzzled.

How did Pelipper know the exact location of the ranch?

After pondering briefly, he arrived at a possible answer.

That is, just as the human world has its operating logic, the Pokemon World also has its own operating logic.

It's like the Meowth that wander through city alleys; they form a vast network sharing information that is unseen by humans.

Or like the Clefairy that gather and dance under the full moon, worshiping the moon as a deity, much like ancient human clans.

"What a pity..."

D couldn't help thinking, if only he had bought Teddiursa a camera when it left.

That way, Teddiursa could capture photos of what it encountered on its travels and send them back to the ranch, allowing him to know about their adventurous experiences.

However, since he has now received the pearl and postcard sent back by Teddiursa, it means their journey is going well.

D's worries were mostly eased, and he looked up at the sky, but Pelipper was already nowhere to be seen.

D looked again at the pearl in his hand, images of Mew and Teddiursa appeared in his mind.

Mew, which Team Rocket had spent decades searching for, had come into his life just like that.

They never even found it, only discovering a fossilized eyelash of Mew.

That eyelash fossil was later used by Team Rocket to create Mewtwo through genetic technology.

"I wonder what Boss Giovanni would feel if he knew about this incident."

Sometimes, humans can't help but have whimsical thoughts about things far removed from their lives.

Just like now, D, with a hint of mischievousness in his mind, imagined himself traveling to the Viridian Gym with Teddiursa, Mew, Koraidon, and Gogoat.

At that time, Boss Giovanni's expression would surely be priceless.

Unlike other regions that have some security problems, the Paldea region doesn't have a truly evil organization.

Team Star is just a group of former bullying victims forming a defense against bullying.

However, living a peaceful and stable life like this is also pretty nice.

Returning to his room, D carefully put the small pearl and postcard with paw prints away, storing them with the other possessions Teddiursa had previously accumulated in the drawer.

Cyclizar, with a growling stomach, was pitifully calling out from behind, just like a hungry child seeking their mother.

However, Cyclizar is indeed just a kid, as it's only a few months old, not yet a year.

He needed to start preparing dinner.

This evening, D planned to make pan-fried pork chops, paired with nutritious fruit juice and fresh milk.

He took the ingredients out of the refrigerator and began to busy himself.

When the sun had completely set, and darkness enveloped the ranch, dinner was finally ready.

D brought the steaming dishes to the table, letting Koraidon and the others eat first, while he took a Pokemon treat and a sandwich to the ice house at the back.

Pushing the door open, a burst of cold air hit him.

Glaceon was resting on the Ice Stone and immediately opened its eyes when it heard the sounds from outside.

D placed the food in front of it, smiling, "I wasn't sure what you like to eat, so I prepared these for you to try and see if they suit your taste."

"Bui...?"

Glaceon was quite surprised by this.

It looked down at the food it had never eaten before and couldn't help but wonder: Does this place actively provide food for Pokemon?

D crouched beside it, smiling, "Let me formally introduce myself, Glaceon. I'm the rancher of this ranch, and all the Pokemon you saw today came from different places to live here with me."

"Providing food for the Pokemon in the ranch is the responsibility of a good rancher, so please, eat without worry!"

"Bui..." Glaceon blinked, hesitating to touch the food.

In its mind, it was already grateful to have been given such a nice place to live.

And coupled with the fact that D had protected it from that Froslass previously.

Glaceon felt it owed him a lot of favors, and having done nothing in return, it couldn't bring itself to comfortably accept his food.

---

As a healthy Glaceon, it can solve its own food problems.

So, Glaceon gently shook its head: "Eevee."

Seeing this, D was a bit surprised.

"Not hungry?" he asked.

Glaceon curled back up on the Ice Stone.

D roughly guessed its thoughts and couldn't help but laugh: "All the berries growing on this ranch are mine, you know. If you want to pick some yourself at midnight, it's not much different from now, is it?"

"Eevee?!"

Glaceon had a surprised expression, what was going on?

D recalled the way Glaceon had refused his soda before, thinking that this Glaceon probably didn't like to owe anyone.

That's why it refused the food he offered.

How to put it? This kind of personality is quite cute.

Only this Glaceon was completely unaware of its current situation.

Glaceon felt full of doubt.

Suddenly, it got the feeling that it might have fallen into a trap.

But seeing the sincere look of the human in front, it thought maybe it was wrong.

D didn't explain to Glaceon because he had his own plans.

Glaceon must have a lot of questions now. If he didn't explain, Glaceon would be compelled to ask the Pokemon on the ranch.

At that time, it could enhance their relationship and bring them closer together.

Thinking of this, D stood up and bid farewell to Glaceon: "Well, good night, Glaceon!"

He walked out of the Ice Cave and back to the cabin, where he asked Alcremie, who was making cream in the kitchen:

"Alcremie, do you still want to be friends with Glaceon?"

Alcremie loved making friends, all the Pokemon on the ranch were its good friends.

Nacli, little Pawmi, the lazy Gogoat…

Hearing this, it nodded: "Gream..."

But then it dropped its head sadly because the Glaceon didn't respond to it, as if it didn't really want to be friends.

Seeing this, D called it over and whispered a few words into its ear.

After listening, Alcremie's eyes lit up: "Gream?" Could that really make it friends with Glaceon?

D nodded: "Of course, it's just that it appears very cold on the outside but is very kind inside. In human terms, it's called cold exterior, warm interior."

Alcremie looked at Koraidon, asking, "Gream?" And what about Koraidon?

D glanced at Koraidon and replied, "It's all warm."

"Gream!" Got it!

Alcremie nodded happily.

After giving instructions, D picked up little Pawmi to get ready for bed.

After more than a month of care, the once weak Pawmi had returned to a healthy shape, its fur becoming oily and slippery, fluffy.

Pawmi lay beside D, looking at Alcremie, it couldn't help but ask: "Paw?"

"Don't worry! They'll become good friends!" D patted its head: "Sleep now! I have a little task for you tomorrow."

He wanted to try the power facility Keri installed.

"Paw!" Hearing this, Pawmi began to look forward to it.

Compared to Dragonite, Koraidon, Gogoat, Pawmi felt like a small grass growing in the shadow of a big mountain.

It was just so tiny.

The weak Pawmi also hoped to do more for D like Dragonite, Gogoat, and Miltank.

Because D was very kind to it, not only bathing it but also preparing nutritious food, just like a mother.

But D was not its mother; they met by chance, yet he accepted it and forgave its stealing food.

Pawmi wanted to do more things, but it couldn't water nor produce milk.

So, when it heard D say he had something for it to do tomorrow, it was very happy.

"Paw?" What sort of task?

D teased: "You'll find out tomorrow, time to sleep now."

So Pawmi closed its eyes, started snoring, and looked forward to tomorrow's arrival.

Looking at this Pawmi, D couldn't help but admit he was charmed by its cuteness.

Such a well-behaved little Pawmi!

Like real family, wanting to do more for each other to share the load.

D smiled and patted Pawmi's cheek.

"Sleep now!"

————

At eight in the evening, most Pokemon on the ranch were already dreaming.

Alcremie arrived at the Ice Cave, holding a freshly picked Berry in its hand, as D suggested bringing a little gift.

Hearing footsteps outside, Glaceon in the Ice Cave immediately opened its eyes and cautiously looked at the door.

At this moment, a "Gream" call came from outside the door.

It was that Alcremie from the day. Glaceon thought.

The other was greeting it.

By nature, Glaceon wasn't planning to respond, but remembering what D had said earlier, it silently jumped off the Ice Stone.

Outside, Alcremie felt a bit nervous, wondering if the other would speak as D said?

And just then, the door was nudged open, and a Glaceon emerged from inside.

It came out!

Seeing Glaceon, Alcremie's eyes lit up: "Gream!"

Good evening! It greeted warmly.

---

"Bui..."

Glaceon looked at the Alcremie and finally responded.

Actually, it hadn't meant to ignore Alcremie before; it simply didn't know how to express itself or what to say.

Alcremie didn't seem to mind; it happily handed Glaceon a Berry and proactively said:

"Mama?" (Let me show you around this ranch! There are many Pokemon living here, and everyone is good friends.)

Good friends... Glaceon pondered on this word.

Combined with its inner confusion, it didn't refuse Alcremie. Instead, it lightly raised its forelimbs, followed behind Alcremie, and began familiarizing itself with this place.

Alcremie eagerly introduced the situation around them.

Glaceon moved forward, observing the surroundings; this was a habit it developed in the snowy mountains.

"Mama!" (Over there are Gogoats—they may look lazy, but they're actually quite impressive! With them is Smoliv, who came here with me.)

Upon hearing this, Glaceon glanced in that direction.

With just one look, it sensed a terrifying natural energy emanating from there.

The natural energy there was incredibly rich, almost as potent as its Ice Stone!

Intuition told it that if it hadn't gone to the snowy mountain initially but came here instead, it might have evolved into another form!

"Bui..."

Glaceon felt somewhat shocked inside.

Meanwhile, Alcremie seemed to be accustomed to it.

It continued to lead the way and said to Glaceon while passing by some houses:

"Mama... Mama!" (This is where Nacli, Miltank, and the hens live, but it's late; they're already resting... Tomorrow I'll introduce them to you!)

"Bui."

Then, they arrived at an orchard.

Alcremie said that D planted the fruit trees and crops here, and by autumn, these trees would bear delicious Berries.

Seeing this, Glaceon finally understood the words D spoke earlier.

It softly asked: "Bui?" (Have you always lived in this place?)

Alcremie nodded: "Mama~" (Yes!)

Glaceon: "Bui?" (Don't you need to find your own food every day?)

"Mama." Alcremie shook its head gently. Of course not! D cooks many delicious meals for them!

Glaceon was filled with confusion: "Bui?" (Why does D provide food for you?)

Upon hearing the question, Alcremie paused, seemingly not knowing the answer either.

It asked Glaceon to wait there for a moment, then ran back to the house and woke up D, who had just fallen asleep.

D yawned and rubbed his sleepy eyes, asking: "What's wrong?"

Alcremie: "Mama?" (Why do you cook so many delicious meals for us?)

Unfortunately, D didn't understand.

However, Koraidon, who was lying on the ground asleep, gave an answer.

Alcremie turned its head and saw Koraidon's mouth moving, although its eyes remained closed: "Ah-gah-s." (That's because D likes Pokemon.)

Having received the answer, Alcremie quickly ran outside, leaving D feeling confused.

He yawned, felt a wave of drowsiness, and fell back onto his pillow to continue sleeping.

D: zZZ

Watching Alcremie rush back, Glaceon found itself unsure of what to say.

Alcremie happily conveyed the answer to it.

Glaceon was slightly startled.

So, is it because he likes them that he prepares food for them?

Seeing it was still puzzled, Alcremie carefully recalled everyone in the ranch and then said to Glaceon:

"Mama!" (D prepares food for us, and we all help him manage the ranch!)

D works with Koraidon and Cyclizar to plant crops, Dragonite helps with watering, Combee collects honey, Gogoat produces goat milk...

It can also make delicious cream!

Upon hearing this, Glaceon felt a bit lost: "Bui?" (Why?)

Alcremie answered: "Mama!" (That's because we all like D!)

Mutual affection...

Glaceon didn't speak further.

A night breeze blew by.

It smelled a scent it had never experienced before.

Glaceon couldn't help but look up, and saw a beautiful flower field not far ahead—a flower field that couldn't exist in the snowy mountains.

Its gaze was irresistibly drawn to those flowers.

It walked forward, gently lowered its head, and inhaled the fragrance of the flowers.

At that moment, Glaceon felt like it understood something.

After everything settled, Alcremie escorted Glaceon to the entrance of the Ice Cave, nervously asking: "Mama?" (Can we be friends?)

Upon hearing this, Glaceon was slightly taken aback.

It thought about how Alcremie had given it the Berry, enthusiastically introduced the surroundings, and that moment in the afternoon when Alcremie received no response from it and left feeling disappointed.

After a moment of silence, it finally responded.

"Bui."

Alcremie was overjoyed, happily bidding farewell to Glaceon: "Mama!"

See you tomorrow!

---

---

---

---

# 118: Chapter 116: The Power Generation Problem on the Ranch

Early the next morning, D got up early, quickly solved breakfast, and then began to immerse himself in the day's work.

He fed the chickens and picked up the eggs they laid, poured the new batch of Grassy Seed into Gogoat's feed trough, stored the milk Miltank provided in barrels, collected the Rock Salt produced by Naclis, and packed the honey Combees gathered yesterday into glass jars.

Finally, he kept part of these for himself and placed the rest next to the shipping box, waiting for Zack to come and collect them in the afternoon.

After completing all these tasks, Dragonite by the field had also finished watering.

The leftover water droplets in the air, under the sunlight, formed a curved rainbow.

Dragonite was enjoying the rainbow alongside Butterfree, looking very happy.

"Phew..."

D took a deep breath, patted the dust off his hands, and headed towards the back, thinking about Glaceon's situation.

As he pushed open the wooden door, a chill immediately rushed towards him.

D couldn't help but shiver, and as he gazed inside, he saw that this cave had completely transformed, clearly remodeled by Glaceon.

The originally soft soil was covered by a smooth ice layer; some unmelted fine snow piled in the corners, and the cave walls were also encased in ice.

Walking in, D felt as if he'd entered an Eskimo ice house.

In the center of the ice cave, the Ice Stone was placed, and Glaceon was still lying on it, seemingly treating the cold stone as its bed.

"Looks like you've adapted already!" D praised, "This place is truly beautiful, you're amazing!"

Being praised for the first time, Glaceon felt a bit shy inside but did not express it outwardly, simply lowering its head slightly and averting its gaze.

It's nothing, Glaceon thought.

D looked down at the plate on the ice surface, the one he had brought over yesterday, but now it was empty.

He went forward and picked up the plate, asking, "Did you like yesterday's food?"

Glaceon hesitated a bit and then nodded lightly, "Vu..."

Then, what kind of help could it provide?

Just like what Alcremie said last night, D provided them with food, and they would help D in return.

D did not know what Glaceon was thinking. He collected the plate and then turned back to the kitchen, taking out new food from the fridge to give to Glaceon.

D: "Eat up! After eating, you can stroll outside. I still have work to do! It's safe here; no Pokemon will attack recklessly."

"If you have any problems, you can find Alcremie or Koraidon. You should remember Koraidon, right? The red Pokemon you saw yesterday."

Glaceon nodded, "Vu."

Seeing this, D smiled and bid farewell to Glaceon.

As soon as he stepped outside, he saw a very eager Pawmi.

"Wait a moment." D said to it.

He returned the plate to the kitchen and then took Pawmi to the utility room.

The generator sat quietly against the wall.

In the Pokemon World, Pokemon is a very important resource.

Different types of Pokemon can bring various changes to human life.

Fire Type Pokemon can start fires, Water Type Pokemon can water, Grass Type Pokemon can look after plants.

And Electric Type Pokemon can provide electricity for humans.

For example, an Electivire can provide all the electricity needed by a large city's buildings for a year.

Because of this, Pokemon like Magneton and Electivire are often kept in power plants, working alongside humans.

Some scientists have even developed various machines adapted to the power generation of different Electric Type Pokemon, like a hamster wheel generator for Pikachu.

Of course, those that consume the Pokemon's stamina are in the minority.

Most generators are of the energy storage type, where you have the Electric Type Pokemon input enough electricity into it beforehand. This way, when there's a power outage, you can start it to power homes.

The generator Keri sold was also like this; it was a machine with a silver casing, and a metal ring on top designed to absorb electricity.

According to the manual, you only need to place an Electric Type Pokemon in there and have it use its Electric Type move; the electricity it releases will be automatically absorbed.

Thinking of this, D picked up Pawmi and said to it, "I need a little bit of your electricity."

Electricity? As an Electric Type Pokemon, Pawmi naturally could release electricity, an ease to it.

"Bamo~" Pawmi nodded happily, indicating its willingness!

"Good child." D patted its head, then placed it on the metal ring, "Don't be nervous, just use your best Electric Type move now!"

"Bamo~"

Pawmi responded joyfully, then stood up and rubbed its developing electric pouches on its cheeks with its paw.

Gradually, small currents were generated through the friction.

"Ba—mo!"

Soon, accompanied by Pawmi's enthusiastic cry, a stream of electricity was released from its paw.

The generator detected the electricity and started operating automatically, absorbing Pawmi's move through the metal ring.

The move seemed to be the Electric Type "Thunder Shock", not very powerful but the best move Pawmi currently had.

D's mind recalled the game's level for Pawmi to learn Thunder Shock, lv3.

This indicated that Pawmi was indeed quite weak, even smaller than a Cyclizar.

It had been brought by a Bombirdier without fully mastering the skills for foraging, far from its parents, alone on the ranch.

Soon, all the Thunder Shock was absorbed.

D glanced at the generator's energy storage bar, noticing it hadn't even reached 1%.

"Looks like we need to put Pawmi's development plan on the agenda." he thought.

Looking at the energy storage bar on the machine, D remembered Keri mentioning that if the generator was full, it could supply the ranch's electricity for half a year.

Currently, the ranch's electricity comes from a nearby power plant.

Today was just a test of the generator's function, which appeared to work.

D now had a rough idea, and at that moment, little Pawmi sat in the machine with a sad face.

It felt sorry for only being able to provide such a weak amount of electricity.

D wasn't too concerned and comforted it, "It's alright, I'll make some new dishes to boost your strength later."

The best would be to make dishes like Grassy Seed, which could enhance Electric Type energy.

But ingredients related to lightning weren't easy to find.

Using a Thunder Stone might cause Pawmi's teeth to chip due to its hardness.

Hmm, it's indeed a challenging task.

---

---

---

---

# 119: Chapter 117: A Leisurely Day

Next, D called the second Pokemon on the ranch that knows Electric type moves, Dragonite.

Dragonite looked brightly at the generator in front of it.

Did D specially prepare this machine to help it train Electric type moves? Dragonite couldn't help but think.

Just like when it practiced Rain Dance in the fields and Hurricane in the sky.

If it kept using Electric type moves on this machine, then...

Beside it, D was explaining the generator's operation to Dragonite, and suddenly noticed the odd look in Dragonite's eyes.

"...What are you thinking?"

Dragonite had completely regarded the generator as a tool to practice Electric type moves by now.

It joyfully hugged D.

"Roar!"

D is so wonderful!

Previously, with watering and milk delivery to help it train Rain Dance and Hurricane, because it used these moves every day, its familiarity with them increased significantly.

Now, it's starting to help it practice Electric type moves. It'll definitely work hard!

The hug left D confused with a head full of questions.

He certainly felt like Dragonite had misunderstood something...

And Dragonite had already begun channeling electricity into the generator.

Because its body size is much larger than Pawmi, it couldn't stand on the generator, so it placed its pudgy paws on it.

Then, crackling electric currents surged from Dragonite's fists and were absorbed by the generator.

D turned to look, seeing the generator's energy bar rising steadily.

1%…3%…4%…12%…23%…37%…

Finally fixed at 48%.

Witnessing this, Pawmi showed an admiring look in its eyes.

"Pammo!" Awesome!

But Dragonite had reached its limit.

Its body's electric power was drained, and it couldn't use any more Electric type moves.

If we use game terminology, it ran out of PP value (move usage count).

"That's enough, you've done quite well!"

D took Pawmi and Dragonite back to the front house and fetched two bottles of iced honey lemon water from the fridge.

This is a beverage he made earlier, which can relieve fatigue and restore energy when consumed.

Dragonite joined Pawmi to sit on the grass outside the door, leisurely enjoying the breeze and drinking honey lemon water.

After settling the two Pokemon, D returned to the storeroom, extracted tools and wood, and used the workbench there to start crafting a support frame for hops.

Summer is approaching, and the seeds of those summer crops he bought earlier can soon be planted.

Crops like corn don't need a frame to grow, but hops do.

They would climb the frames erected by the Rancher, reaching for sunlight on every leaf, then grow hops used for brewing beer.

Of course, to brew a bottle of beer, hops alone aren't enough; pure water, malt, and yeast are also required.

There's pure water in the ranch, yeast can be bought in town.

The ranch also stores a lot of malt, which he grew himself two months ago.

The spring wheat wasn't sold after harvesting; instead, he kept it.

Finally, the hops and a series of ingredients were placed in the brew buckets, stored in the cellar, waiting about a month for the brew to complete.

D is curious about what kind of magical effects his homemade beer might have.

The only regret is that the brewing process has certain environmental and temperature requirements.

If the brew bucket didn't need to be stored in a cool, shaded cellar, he might have spread the brew buckets all over the place.

The ranch doesn't have a cellar yet, but D isn't worried. Hops and barley can be stored until there's a cellar to start brewing.

At the current profitability rate of the ranch, by mid-fall at the latest, he should have saved enough to expand the house, requiring 2 million.

Winter is unsuitable for growing crops, so he can focus on brewing during that time.

Time ticked away, and as the sunlight rose high, the frames were completed one by one.

D directed Koraidon and Cyclizar to move them to the front; by then, the sun had fully risen.

Under the eaves, Dragonite and Pawmi lay down after finishing the honey lemon water; their upper bodies resting in the shade, their lower bodies basking in sunlight.

The two Pokemon were snoring peacefully, looking quite relaxed.

D smiled at the scene, and at this moment, Cyclizar suddenly made a sound.

D followed its gaze and saw Glaceon running out of the Ice Cave unbeknownst to him.

It was with Alcremie, Nacli, Smoliv, and Butterfree in the sheep pen.

The three Gogoats seemed to have finished their disco, lazily lying in the shade, munching on Grassy Seeds while watching them.

Smoliv leaned against Gogoat, nodding off.

Seeing this scene, D felt a bit surprised.

Glaceon has integrated into the farm so quickly?

He saw Alcremie holding a frozen wildflower, while five Naclis looked pitifully at Glaceon, with several small flowers placed in front of them.

Glaceon: "..."

Just as D thought it wouldn't freeze flowers for Nacli, Glaceon, silent for a while, blew an Icy Wind towards those flowers.

The Naclis were thrilled, and Alcremie cheerfully handed the icy flower to Glaceon.

"Maa~"

D couldn't understand, but Koraidon did.

Alcremie was telling Glaceon: "Alright, if it's frozen, it won't spoil! Place it where you sleep, and you can see it first thing every time you open your eyes~"

This was learned from D; Alcremie saw him storing things in the fridge, saying that way food wouldn't spoil.

Hearing this, Glaceon was slightly taken aback.

It hadn't realized that Alcremie asking it to freeze the flowers was to give them to it.

The other five Naclis saw Alcremie's gesture and followed suit, handing flowers to Glaceon.

Smoliv and Butterfree also looked delighted at this scene, hoping Glaceon might accept them.

D, though he didn't know what happened, seeing their harmonious interaction, couldn't help but crack a smile.

"Alright, let's get to work!" D said with a grin.

"Arghs~"

"Roar~"

Cyclizar and Koraidon responded, then they began focusing on setting up the frames.

Today is March 15, though it's still half a month until summer, some summer crops can start planting.

So, D will be quite busy today.

Apart from constructing hop frames, he also needs to plow the land again, and when the spring horse race ends, he can start planting summer crops.

Like pumpkins, corn, and pineapple.

Pumpkins can be used to make pumpkin pie and pumpkin porridge.

Corn can be cooked directly, made into popcorn, or corn chowder, and used to make ribs soup.

Pineapple can be used to make sweet and sour pork, and pineapple rice.

Once these crops mature, he can whip up more delicious dishes!

---
