# 120: Chapter 118: Rainy Night

In the evening, Zack drove over with the truck to pick up the goods.

He looked at the many Pokemon running around the ranch and grinned:

"It's getting more lively around here!"

D stood by his side, watching the bustling Pokemon with Zack, and couldn't help but feel a kind of sweet trouble in his heart:

"I'm getting more and more exhausted, spending so much time every day making Pokemon food and taking care of them."

Zack laughed heartily: "Of course, it's a common trouble for every Pokemon Trainer. If you want to make it easier, I suggest you capture a caretaker-type Pokemon or hire a caretaker."

A caretaker-type Pokemon...

D had considered this before, but it's too difficult to encounter these Pokemon in the wild.

As for hiring a caretaker, it's not something he needs to consider right now.

Because he's still living with a group of Pokemon in a small cabin, there's simply no place for a caretaker to stay.

Zack also realized this and then mysteriously said, "Of course, there's a third way."

D asked, "What is it?"

Zack laughed aloud: "That's to find a girlfriend, get married, have a child, and run the ranch together as a family! How happy such a life would be! This way, when you grow older, your child can inherit your ranch and help care for the Pokemon here."

D: "..."

"Better not." He said, temporarily putting aside this thought, as he preferred to enjoy this leisurely life for now rather than starting a family.

Seeing this, Zack shrugged: "Then there's no other way!"

He weighed today's milk and then loaded it onto the truck, saying, "Altogether, it's fifty-eight thousand six hundred League Coins, here you go!"

D reached out and took it.

Zack jumped onto the truck and waved to D through the window: "I'm off, see you tomorrow!"

"Bye-bye!"

Watching Zack's large truck drive away into the distance, D turned around and started preparing dinner for the Pokemon.

Since he'd been busy all day, he was exhausted, so today's dinner was very simple.

He heated the pan with oil, took out a steak to fry, then sandwiched the fried steak between two slices of toast, added crispy and refreshing lettuce, and finally spread a layer of salad dressing, and thus a D's Secret Sandwich was complete.

D let Koraidon and the others eat first, while he took a few snacks to the cave at the back.

Compared to when he came here this morning, some changes had occurred in the cave.

Beside the Ice Stone were a few flowers that had been frozen, clearly brought back by Glaceon, gifts from Alcremie and Nacli.

D placed the food in front of Glaceon and asked, "Do you like flowers?"

"Bui."

Glaceon gently nodded.

"That's great!" D said with a smile, "Soon, I'll plant some other kinds of flowers in the ranch, they're all very beautiful, you'll see them soon."

"Bui..."

Glaceon's blue eyes revealed a hint of anticipation.

D smiled slightly, then turned to leave without saying much more.

On his way past the storage room, he suddenly noticed the generator's energy bar was full.

"Hmm?"

Looking at the 100% power, D was quite surprised.

But quickly, as if realizing something, he shouted loudly toward the front: "Dragonite!"

At the sound of a "roar," Dragonite appeared in the storage room.

D pointed at the generator and asked, "Was this your doing?"

"Roar~"

Dragonite looked pleased.

At that moment, D suddenly realized why this morning Dragonite was looking at the generator like that.

Because he had previously said watering could train Rain Dance, and delivering milk could foster determination, so naturally, Dragonite considered supplying power to the generator as special training!

"..."

D was at a loss for words.

He thought for a moment, then slowly explained to Dragonite the function of the generator, such as providing electricity to the ranch, keeping the refrigerator running, and offering lighting, and so on.

After saying this, D paused and asked, "Did it help you practice any moves?"

"Roar~"

Dragonite nodded its large head.

The feeling of energy attracting the Electric Type Energy inside it, like a flood bursting from a dam, was fascinating.

Dragonite couldn't help but wonder if using moves like this in the future with all its energy would make the moves' power immense?

It had tried before when D wasn't watching but didn't succeed.

Dragonite felt it still needed more practice.

D: "..."

Seeing Dragonite nod, D was even more speechless.

Could this really help practice moves?

He was pleased Dragonite could be helped and also glad for the electricity it supplied to the ranch.

But there's a crucial point: the generator can't be overloaded, or it could explode!

D warned Dragonite about such consequences and pointed to the energy bar, telling it that charging could only continue up to the full level, beyond which it would explode.

The consequences of an explosion would be serious, from burning the house down to posing life-threatening dangers.

After hearing this, Dragonite immediately widened its eyes, looking at the generator with some fear.

Fortunately, it had stopped just in time before...

"But don't worry too much, just pay attention and everything will be fine, or you can call me to charge here together," D said, seeing Dragonite's frightened look.

"Roar." Dragonite nodded, indicating it would remember.

"Alright, go back and enjoy yourself!" D said.

He then stayed to switch the ranch's power source from the power plant to this generator.

This way, they could save quite a bit on electricity each year!

After finishing this task, D returned to the house to have dinner.

As usual, he turned on the TV and switched the program to the weather channel.

The female host on TV was reporting the weather for the next day.

"... It's expected that early tomorrow, the southwestern Paldea Region will experience some rainfall, but nothing major to worry about... Paldea Meteorological TV Station reporting!"

"Huh? It's going to rain?"

D hurriedly got up to bring the clothes and bedding he had hung outside inside, and closed the Chicken House's door.

Late at night, D was sleeping on the bed, holding Pawmi, when he was suddenly stirred awake by the continuous sound of rain outside.

Raindrops fell on the eaves, making a crackling sound.

"So it really is raining..."

D looked out the window, seeing nothing but darkness outside and raindrops sliding down the window, leaving streaks of water.

D didn't mind rainy days; on the contrary, this kind of weather was great for sleeping, especially with his beloved Pokemon by his side.

He turned over, stopped looking outside, and went back to sleep holding Pawmi amidst the muffled and noisy rain sound.

Meanwhile, at the Salt House.

A Nacli suddenly emitted a dazzling light from its body...

---

---

---

---

# 121: Chapter 119: Koraidon Gets Jealous (Double-Length Chapter)

Early the next morning, as the sky just started to lighten, D got out of bed.

Outside, a light rain was falling. The sky was gray, and the entire farm was shrouded in a misty drizzle, with the grass glistening with a soft sheen of moisture.

"Still raining, huh?"

D muttered, opening the door to let the cool breeze in, then grabbed some sandwiches and eggs from the fridge, making a few simple sandwiches which Cyclizar delivered to Glaceon.

After finishing breakfast quickly, Dragonite was already standing under the eaves outside the door, with its small red satchel on its back, waiting to start work.

D handed it two bottled goat milk: "Thanks for your hard work!"

"Roar~"

Dragonite put away the milk and disappeared into the rain.

D then put on rain boots and carried an umbrella as he stepped out of the house, turning back to stop Cyclizar and Koraidon who wanted to follow:

"I'm going to feed the Gogoat; don't come along. Stay inside, I'll be back soon!"

Rainy days would bring mud pits to the ground, and though Koraidon and Cyclizar wouldn't roll around in the mud, their paws would still get somewhat muddy, tracking it all over the house.

Thus, the two Pokemon stood under the eaves, watching eagerly as D walked into the misty rain.

The rain did nothing to dampen the Gogoat's exuberance.

When D arrived, the whole goat pen had transformed into a stage with neon lights flashing everywhere.

The three Gogoat were dancing wildly inside.

Nearby, Smoliv seemed unfazed by this scene.

It basked in the abundance of Grass  Type energy, swaying its body comfortably.

D, unfazed, poured a bag of Grassy Seed into the feed trough and filled the water trough nearby with water.

The three Gogoat immediately gathered around, shoving their heads into the food trough to start their breakfast.

D stood by, watching this scene with a sense of awe.

The Gogoat were becoming more robust; after evolving, their height had increased significantly.

If they stood up fully, they would give off an imposing presence, as if confronting not Gogoat but the legendary Sacred Swordsmen, Cobalion, and Virizion.

D shook his head, dismissing those odd thoughts.

Then he went to the Chicken House, taking feed from the silo and placing it in the feed trough, shutting the doors and windows to prevent the hens from getting soaked and sick.

In the Pokemon House, three Miltank had already milked themselves.

D came over and filled barrels with milk, placed them in a corner, then took out a brush to start grooming them.

The Miltank looked utterly content.

For them, this life was the epitome of happiness.

Every day, someone helped with milking, feeding, grooming, and they had fresh, fertile grass to eat.

They loved it here.

As D brushed the Miltank, he observed their mood. Seeing their enjoyment, his mood became uplifted.

Speaking of these three Miltank, though they looked alike, their personalities were completely different.

One was gentle and caring, often sharing its fresh milk with the weaker Pokemon in the farm.

Another was calm and liked tranquility, enjoying the lush grass outside and sunbathing on sunny days.

The third was lively and active, always full of energy, enjoying communicating with other Pokemon on the farm.

"All done, you're brushed!"

D patted the Miltank's head, hung the brush on the wall, and said, "Go play! I still have to go next door to feed Nacli."

The Miltank agreed gladly.

Seeing this, D smiled, then turned and headed next door.

As Rock Type Pokemon, Nacli did not like rainy days, mostly choosing to stay indoors and not venture out in such weather.

At this moment, the door of the Salt House was closed. The image of those five small figures flashed through D's mind, bringing a smile to his lips as he pushed open the door of the Salt House.

The first sight that greeted him was a huge figure made of rock.

It was a Pokemon made entirely of rock, its body square, like something assembled from building blocks.

D's eyes widened as he realized what had happened.

Nacli had evolved! Overnight, from Nacli to Naclstack!

Before he came in, the other four Nacli had gathered before the Naclstack, looking at their evolved companion with envy.

Seeing D approach, they ran over, encircling him.

"Nacli!"

"Nacli..."

The evolved Naclstack also moved its limbs and stepped forward.

"Naclstack~"

Its growl had clearly changed since its time as Nacli, losing its previous naivety, and now sounded like a mix between a deep voice and a rumble.

D looked with curiosity at this Naclstack, asking, "When did you evolve?"

"Naclstack... stack!" Naclstack responded.

It was just last night; while it was sleeping, it suddenly felt a mysterious power surge from within.

When it awoke, it found itself in its current state.

"Congratulations!" D smiled, reaching out to pat its head.

But in the next moment, the Naclstack in front of him spat out a large amount of crystal-clear, spotless Rock Salt.

In the blink of an eye, the Rock Salt piled into a "salt mountain" on the floor beside them.

---

"Wow!"

D widened his eyes, quite shocked as he watched the scene unfold.

He only knew that Naclstack could produce more Rock Salt, but he hadn't expected it to be this exaggerated.

The amount produced in one go exceeded what used to be gathered in two weeks before!

Nearby, the ecstatic Naclstack looked at the pile of Rock Salt, then at D: "Stack! Stack~"

Life on the farm, carefree as it was, hadn't erased Naclstack's past memories.

It still remembered how D protected them, cared for them, rescued them from the dangers of the wild when they were powerless, and brought them back to the farm, even building a beautiful house for them.

Naclstack never forgot these things for even a moment.

At this moment, the simple and pure-hearted Naclstack was expressing its gratitude to the human in front of it in the only way it knew.

This was all it could do.

Affectionately, Naclstack nudged D with its blocky head.

Though the language barrier existed, D miraculously felt the emotions of this pure-hearted Pokémon.

It was expressing gratitude towards him!

The emotions of the Rock Pokemon were as simple and profound as a mountain.

D was touched and patted Naclstack, saying, "Thank you, Naclstack, I can feel your sincerity."

"Stack~"

The four unevolved Nacli nearby were incredibly envious.

They jumped around beside him, uttering cries of "Nacli, Nacli."

Seeing this, D laughed and picked them up one by one, saying, "You guys also have to work hard! But remember, Naclstack isn't the final stop, there's the mightier Garganacl ahead."

"Nacli!" They would try their best!

Suddenly, D remembered that Garganacl was over two meters tall.

If all five Nacli evolved into Garganacl, their current Salt House would become very low and cramped for them.

"Looks like I need to ask Keri if she can build a bigger Salt House," D murmured to himself.

Then he went back to the cabin and fetched a bag, preparing to collect that pile of Rock Salt while mentioning Nacli's evolution to Alcremie.

Sure enough, upon hearing the news, Alcremie immediately expressed a desire to see for itself.

So D took Alcremie to the Salt House.

Upon seeing Naclstack for the first time, Alcremie's eyes showed a shocked expression: "Alcrem..."

Was this really the tiny Naclstack it used to know?

In front of Nacli, Alcremie had always been the big sister leading them around to play.

Now upon seeing Naclstack, larger than itself, Alcremie suddenly felt a reversal in roles.

Its little followers became bigger than itself overnight...

Naclstack, on the other hand, didn't notice Alcremie's thoughts. It greeted Alcremie happily: "Stack~"

As D was busy collecting the Rock Salt, he said to Alcremie: "Once Naclstack evolves again, they'll grow even larger! Then they'll be bigger and taller than me!"

"Alcrem?!" Alcremie was even more shocked.

D continued, "Then you can stand on Garganacl and let them carry you around to play."

In his mind, he imagined five Garganacl lifting Alcremie high.

At that time, Alcremie would become a Garganacl knight.

Hearing this, both Nacli and Naclstack felt very happy, looking forward to that day.

"Alright then, I'll just put these salts back in the house. Do you want to come with me, or stay here and play with Nacli?" D stood up and asked.

Nacli and Naclstack really wanted Alcremie to stay and play.

Seeing the familiar look in Naclstack's eyes, Alcremie suddenly realized it didn't matter how they changed; they would still be the friends playing by its side.

So Alcremie no longer hesitated and nodded joyfully, indicating it wanted to stay and play with Nacli.

"Okay then." D smiled, "You guys have fun; I won't disturb you!"

He carried that bag of Rock Salt away from the Salt House.

The rain outside hadn't stopped, and once D had put the salts away, Dragonite, who had gone to deliver milk, had also returned.

It landed under the eaves, and D stepped forward to take off the bag hanging from its waist and then took out a towel to dry its rain-soaked body.

A lot of work cannot be done on a rainy day.

The fields don't need watering, and the hens can't graze, so D gave Pawmi and Dragonite a day off to rest at home.

Dragonite sat on the floor, with a basket of small breads that D had made by its side. These breads were small enough for Dragonite to devour in a single bite.

Pawmi watched this scene with admiration.

Ever since witnessing Dragonite release so much electricity yesterday, Pawmi had been in awe of Dragonite.

D noticed this and suddenly realized how interesting it was to observe the interactions between different Pokémon.

Just like Alcremie and Nacli, Butterfree and Combee, Gogoat and Smoliv, Dragonite and Pawmi, and all the Pokémon giving flowers to Glaceon.

Of course, the most amusing of all was Koraidon and Cyclizar.

It turns out, even Pokémon get jealous because of each other's relationship with their Trainers.

For example, now D sat on the carpet watching TV, leaning against Cyclizar, and suddenly noticed Koraidon's expression full of jealousy.

So D stood up, made a cup of the red tea Mrs. Dantes had once gifted him, then casually sat down next to Koraidon.

Koraidon's face immediately lit up with a happy expression.

Seeing this, D moved back beside Cyclizar.

Koraidon began to feel jealous again.

"Pfft!" Watching this scene, D couldn't help but laugh.

Koraidon, focusing intently on the distance between D and Cyclizar, finally noticed D's behavior. Its eyes widened in shock, seemingly unable to believe it.

"Ah gah hiss!"

Then, it turned away, sulking.

D hurriedly approached to console it, "Alright, I didn't mean to. Come watch TV together!"

Koraidon lay on the carpet with its eyes closed, pretending to be asleep, but its twitching eyelids betrayed it.

Beside them, Cyclizar was looking over with a clueless expression, gazing at Koraidon: "Gao ow?"

Koraidon pretended not to hear anything.

D said, "Cyclizar, Dragonite, and Pawmi are all watching you!"

Upon hearing this, Koraidon couldn't sit still anymore.

It always thought its past self was silly, but now its behavior seemed unchanged from before.

How could it let Cyclizar see it like this?!

Koraidon was mortified and desperately wanted to leave.

But at that moment, D patted the spot next to him, "Come watch TV with us!"

Koraidon: "..."

In its helplessness, Koraidon ran over and lay behind D, acting as his cushion.

Cyclizar didn't understand Koraidon's actions and tilted its head looking at Koraidon: "Gao ow?"

Koraidon closed its eyes.

Not listening, not hearing, turtle chanting scripture.

Seeing this, D wanted to laugh but managed to hold it in.

This Koraidon's personality was really too cute!

Externally flashy and dominant, yet reliably steady, occasionally revealing a hint of silliness just like the goofy Cyclizar.

D took its large claw in his hand.

Hmm, what should he say at this moment?

"You are all my wings?"

He fell into thought, but just then, a piece of news on the TV caught his attention.

"...Starting today, the newly discovered Terastalization by Professors Sada and Turo will officially be included in battles. Can this innovative battling method revolutionize the Trainer industry? Stay tuned!"

Then the screen showed a scene of two Trainers in battle, both using Terastalization.

Seeing this scene, D was somewhat surprised.

Terastalization is only now being used in Trainer battles?

He thought it had started long ago!

"No wonder the Trainers I met before didn't use Terastalization," D murmured.

But this also proved that now was still a very early time, Arven was just a few years old, and his parents were still around, meaning there's at least ten years before the Scarlet and Violet storyline begins.

Ten years later...

By then, he'd already be thirty!

---

---

---

---

# 122: Chapter 120: Spring Derby Begins!

League Calendar Year 198, March 25th, sunny, Wednesday.

Today is the day of the Spring Horse Race. After finishing his work on the ranch, D specially brought several Pokemon to join in the fun.

By the way, to get familiar with the competition rules in advance, preparing for his future participation.

The Spring Horse Race is held at a vast grassland outside the town.

By the time D arrived, a lot of people had already gathered here.

Passing Trainers, traveling couples, boys coming with their fathers to watch, elderly people rearing horses, and more.

The grassland was beautifully decorated, and the entrance was draped with a banner reading "Porto Marinada Town Spring Horse Festival". Not far away were stalls selling race tickets, behind which were various small prizes and tools that could be exchanged with race tickets.

At this moment, many participants had already arrived at the racecourse.

D stood not far away, looking over there with envy.

There, he saw a Rapidash with its entire body ablaze with red flames, well-cared for, looking majestic and striking among all the horses.

Besides the Normal form of Rapidash, there was also the Galarian form of Rapidash.

Its entire body was snow-white, and the mane on its head and tail resembled colorful clouds, ethereal and dreamy.

Besides Rapidash, there were also Zebstrika surrounded by sparks, the powerful and robust Mudsdale, and a slightly chubby, light-brown Hippowdon.

Hmm?

Seems like something strange has mixed in.

Isn't Hippowdon a Hippopotas Pokemon? Why can it participate in the horse race too!

At this time, D heard the voice of Allen, the mail carrier, behind him.

He turned around and saw Allen running towards him, "Hey! D, you came too?"

D nodded, "Yeah! Finished the work at the ranch, so I came to have a look."

Allen pulled D to the edge of the racecourse, smiling broadly, "Come on, pick the contestant you plan to support later! Who do you intend to support?"

Honestly, D hadn't decided yet.

But he had a question he wanted to ask.

Looking at the sand-spewing Hippowdon, D asked, "Can that kind of Pokemon also participate in the horse race?"

Allen chuckled and explained, "It's because there are too few horse-type Pokemon! So the town rules allow any Pokemon with 'horse' in its name to participate!"

D: "..."

Allen continued, "It's much better now. Last year, someone even brought a Horsea to participate!"

Sorry, he couldn't imagine that scene.

After all, Horsea is so small, it can't possibly carry a human many times its size.

"Who are you planning to support?" Allen asked.

D's eyes scanned the group of horse-type Pokemon, finally settling on a sleek Rapidash.

"Well, that Rapidash seems to have the potential to win the championship."

Compared to Rapidash, Zebstrika seemed a bit too bulky, and Mudsdale's body shape didn't appear to be built for speed.

As for the Galarian Rapidash…it was also very agile.

D's gaze shifted between the two horses, momentarily unsure.

Allen looked at the scene excitedly and said, "I'm planning to support that Mudsdale! Oh heavens, it looks so strong!"

"Shouldn't such a bulky one run slowly?" D was a bit puzzled.

Allen, seeing D's expression, seemed to understand his thoughts, and explained, "No, no, no, this is not quite like traditional horse racing!"

D slightly raised an eyebrow.

Allen pointed to the Mudsdale, his eyes sparkling as he continued,

"During the race, Pokemon moves are allowed. Take that Mudsdale for instance, if it uses Earthquake while running, then other contestants will—boom!—lose balance and fall behind!"

D: "???"

"Wait, do Trainers ride on the horses during the race?"

"Of course!" Allen explained, "But Trainers will wear appropriate protective gear to shield themselves from the Pokemon moves' effects. Even if they get hit, it won't cause much harm."

D: "..."

Indeed, in this world, everyone has the superhuman physique of a protagonist, huh?

If that's the case, he needs to reconsider his choice of whom to support.

Earthquake could help Mudsdale narrow the speed gap with other horse-type Pokemon.

Hippopotas could kick up sand to obstruct other contestants' vision.

Zebstrika also had a lot of disruptive moves.

Thinking it over, it seemed even harder to choose.

But as the race was about to begin, Allen dragged D to buy race tickets.

The stall owner was a bald man with glasses, looking very shrewd. A placard on the table in front of him stated that each person could purchase up to 200 race tickets, at a price of 50 league coins each.

In the cabinet behind him were various tools.

The most basic were the special Poke Balls, like the Dive Ball and Nest Ball, both costing 50 race tickets.

More expensive items were those for Pokemons to hold, such as the Light Ball to boost Pikachu's attack and special attack, the Hard Stone to increase the power of Rock-type moves, and the Black Glasses to enhance the power of Dark-type moves.

These required 250 race tickets.

In addition, there were the Evolution Stones that D desired most.

He glanced around; except for the Sun Stone and Moon Stone, there were Ice, Fire, Water, Leaf, and Thunder Stones available.

And their exchange price was 300 race tickets.

"A race ticket is 50, 200 is just 10,000, let's buy it!"

D grandly bought two hundred from the owner.

Allen also purchased two hundred tickets.

At this moment, a horn sounded, announcing the start of the first round.

The owner behind them reminded, "Would you two like to place a bet?"

As he spoke, he produced a chart that recorded the odds for each contestant.

The highest was Hippopotas, reaching an astonishing ratio of 1/57.

Rapidash, Galarian Rapidash, and Zebstrika were roughly equal, all at 1/1.5.

Mudsdale hovered around 1/3.

"Looks like most people, like me, chose to bet on Rapidash!" D thought.

Allen straightforwardly placed all his race tickets on Mudsdale.

D hesitated for a moment before ultimately trusting his judgment and bet on that splendid Rapidash.

After betting, D took Koraidon and followed Allen to the spectator's stand.

Allen's Pokemon was a petite Tatsugiri, which he placed on his head to watch the race together.

Spectators gradually gathered.

All the participants in the racecourse had donned protective gear and mounted their horses.

With the sound of a whistle, all the horse-type Pokemon on the track began to run simultaneously.

Watching them gallop below, D was immensely envious.

At that moment, he made a decision in his heart: One day, he would own a horse!

Just like Calyrex's Spectrier and Glastrier!

Speaking of Spectrier and Glastrier, D suddenly thought he might visit the Galar Region.

Because Calyrex wasn't in a good situation, not only forgotten by the world, but both its horses had run away.

The storyline of The Crown Tundra flashed in his mind; maybe he could trick Calyrex and its horses into coming back.

Then borrow a horse from Calyrex to participate in the race.

But…

Right now, the ranch needed him during its development stage; if he left, the Pokemon would go hungry.

Plus, even if he reached The Crown Tundra, he might not find Calyrex.

But then, D suddenly thought of something: according to the legend of Calyrex, it is said to see everything from the past, present, and future.

So here comes the question: If he did something beneficial for Calyrex in the Paldea Region, would it be able to see?

For instance, building a shrine might raise Calyrex's favor, and when he later goes to the Galar Region, Calyrex might actively come to meet him.

Thinking of this, D decided to build a shrine for Calyrex back on the ranch.

Regardless of whether Calyrex could truly sense it, believing didn't cost anything anyway.

---

# 123: Chapter 121: Target—Spectrier and Glastrier!

"Mudsdale, keep it up!"

Watching the figures racing on the track, Allen couldn't help but shout out loud from the sidelines, cheering for the competitor he supported.

The atmosphere at the scene was thoroughly ignited, with cheers and shouts echoing in D's ears.

The passion deep inside him was sparked too, and he shouted along with the people around him: "Come on!"

All the competitors on the track were already speeding ahead.

Currently, the first position was held by the Rapidash which D had high hopes for, closely followed by the rainbow horse from the Galar Region and Zebstrika.

Mudsdale was slightly behind, and as for that Hippowdon, it had fallen to the back.

The commentator excitedly watched this scene and explained with an animated voice:

"The Rapidash that used Flame Charge is in the lead! Following closely is the Galarian Rapidash! It's using Agility!"

At this moment, Zebstrika in third place suddenly let out a long neigh, and immediately afterward, a burst of spark emerged from its body, sweeping towards the two Rapidash ahead.

The trainer of the Rapidash sensed the attack from behind and immediately pulled the reins sharply, causing the Rapidash to veer off its original path, but it also managed to dodge the attack just barely.

However, the Galarian Rapidash wasn't so lucky and got hit, causing its speed to drop, and it was instantly overtaken by Zebstrika.

The first to attack always easily attracts hatred.

After steadying itself, the Galarian Rapidash immediately used its spiritual power to materialize Psycho Cut, slicing towards Zebstrika.

The leading Rapidash also used Fire Spin as a counterattack.

Facing the simultaneous front and back attacks, Zebstrika emitted a powerful burst of spark from its body.

Basking in the spark, its speed suddenly increased, and it then dashed through the Fire Spin blocking in front.

Seeing this, the commentator was greatly shocked: "Wow! It's Charge! That Zebstrika used Charge to trigger its own Motor Drive ability, surpassing the leading Rapidash in one fell swoop!"

"Darn it!"

The trainer of the Rapidash gritted his teeth, just about to let the Rapidash attack when the whole track suddenly underwent a violent tremor.

The ground in front cracked, and Zebstrika, being careless, fell to the ground, with its trainer also tumbling off.

The Rapidash, due to focusing on Zebstrika, was also caught off guard by this sudden change and fell alongside Zebstrika.

"It's Mudsdale!" the commentator shouted: "Mudsdale used Earthquake! Rapidash and Zebstrika are in trouble! What? The Galarian Rapidash is levitating?! It's actually running on air! It's almost at the finish line! Can Rapidash and Zebstrika catch up? Keep it up!"

The finish line was just ahead, and the Galarian Rapidash was just about to reach there when suddenly an unexpected event happened.

Rocks surged from underground, blocking its path.

The trainer of the Galarian Rapidash hurriedly pulled the reins, but still crashed into them.

The female trainer flew out, landing on the grass, but thanks to her protective gear, she appeared unhurt.

Instead, she stood up and commanded her Pokemon to attack as she watched Mudsdale overtaking Rapidash.

"The Galarian Rapidash used Psybeam! It's a direct hit! Oh my god, Mudsdale has fallen into a state of confusion and veered off course!" The commentator quickly shouted loudly.

But right after, everyone at the event site noticed an incredible scene.

The four leading contestants were fighting fiercely, both getting injured.

Before they could remount, a plump Hippowdon nonchalantly rushed past them and dashed straight to the finish line.

"Oh my god! What happened? The supposedly overlooked Hippowdon contestant reached the finish line first?!" The commentator's tone was filled with shock.

The audience collectively gasped in astonishment.

Immediately, a voice filled with elation rang out: "I've struck it rich! I bet on Hippowdon!"

"What's the deal?!" Allen clenched his fists, furious: "We lost!"

D also felt like he could cough up blood.

Seriously?! Did that Hippowdon actually win the championship?!

But that's how things turned out.

Mayor Thomas came forward to present the championship trophy to the Hippowdon and took a photo with them using a camera.

Meanwhile, the other four competitors, with dirt-stained faces, removed their helmets, looked at each other in utter dismay, and then shrugged helplessly.

When all was said and done, the time was approaching three in the afternoon.

D and Allen leaned against the racetrack railing with their respective Pokemon, watching the crowd in the distance.

Allen looked pained, took out his wallet, and counted the money inside: "My ten thousand League Coins! Just went down the drain, darn it!"

D: "..."

He looked at the sunset on the horizon, offering words of solace: "Failure is a constant companion throughout life; such is life!"

Allen wasn't comforted at all; remembering that D also lost ten thousand, he couldn't help but ask: "Aren't you heartbroken?"

D shrugged nonchalantly: "Not really, my Naclstack coughs up a bit of Rock Salt and it's all earned back."

"Darn it!" Allen was so frustrated that he banged his head against the railing.

Then, realizing that D was a rancher who was skilled in breeding Pokemon, he quickly stopped and inquired:

"Aren't you planning to raise a Horse-type Pokemon?"

"I'd like to, but as you know, there's no Horse-type Pokemon around Porto Marinada Town," D spread his hands helplessly.

Moreover, his ambition had shifted; he didn't want a regular Horse-type Pokemon anymore, he wanted Calyrex's two Sovereign and Steed horses!

Thinking of this, D glanced at the vast racecourse.

Just wait! One day he would ride upon a Spectrier and Glastrier to compete here, reclaiming his lost ten thousand League Coins with interest!

"It's over," Allen had a despondent look: "I was counting on you to breed a powerful Horse-type Pokemon to win back my ten thousand League Coins, like those three Gogoat you bred."

D: "…The success of those Gogoat is not something that can be replicated."

"I believe in you!" Allen patted his shoulder: "If you can breed the first one, you can breed a second one! Don't worry D, I'll inquire for you while delivering messages about where Horse-type Pokemon might roam!"

However, the Paldea Region harbored no Rapidash nor Zebstrika, only a single Mudsdale.

The Rapidash here today all belonged to trainers traveling from other regions.

After pondering for a moment, D said: "Rather than Horse-type Pokemon, I'd prefer if you helped me find a housekeeper-type Pokemon."

"Housekeeper Pokemon?" A few figures popped up in Allen's mind: "You mean like Mr. Mime, Audino, or Indeedee, those adept at housekeeping?"

D nodded: "Yes, there are too many Pokemon on the ranch, and I'm barely managing alone."

Hearing this, Allen patted his chest and promised: "No problem, leave it to me! I'll find out for you!"

"Thanks a lot!" D responded.

Allen stretched, glanced at the sky, and said: "It's evening, time to go home! See you around, D!"

"Mhm."

D nodded, watching as Allen and Tatsugiri left, then patted the Koraidon beside him, smiling as he said: "Let's head home too!"

Koraidon: "Argh~"

It's getting dark, time to go home!

---

---

---

---

# 124: Chapter 122: Calyrex's Shrine

D was always the type to act on his word immediately.

The moment he returned to the farm, he put his hands to work, crafting a simple shrine out of leftover wooden boards and nails from the utility room.

He molded a clay figure of a big rabbit with a crown on its head, resembling a cauliflower a bit.

Once the clay dried, D placed the "clay rabbit" in the shrine and set it at the back of the shipping crates, in the fenced-off clearing.

Surprisingly, it looked quite the part.

"All done!"

D looked at everything with satisfaction.

Lastly, he brought a few Pokémon and made a formal offering, "Calyrex! Please bless my farm with favorable weather and abundant harvests this coming summer!"

With the thought, "Why not believe just a little since it doesn't cost anything," D went to get two Berries to serve as offerings in front of the shrine.

After doing all this, D began to recall which Legendary Pokémon in the realm of legendary beasts were as approachable as Calyrex.

Mewtwo of Kanto.

Ho-oh and Lugia of Johto.

Groudon, Kyogre, and Rayquaza of Hoenn.

Dialga, Palkia, Giratina, Arceus, and Regigigas of Sinnoh.

Reshiram, Zekrom, and Kyurem of Unova.

Zygarde, Xerneas, and Yveltal of Kalos.

Lunala, Solgaleo, Necrozma, and the four Tapu of Alola.

The Sword and Shield duo, and Eternatus of the Galar Region.

If there were any, D wouldn't mind placing his faith in them too.

However, unfortunately, all these Legendary Pokémon don't rely on the Power of Faith, and their elevated status and background mean they don't need believers.

Compared to them, Calyrex appears closer and more approachable.

Who among Pokémon players hasn't dreamt about legendary beasts?

He hoped that Calyrex would see everything he did and become more fond of him because of it.

So that when he travels to the Galar Region someday, Calyrex might appear before this "devout" follower of his own accord.

Today is March 25th, with only six days left until the official start of the hot summer season.

The change of season is usually the busiest time, so D planned to take advantage of these few free days to rest and conserve energy for the tasks ahead.

That night, he went to bed early, holding his fluffy little Pawmi as he peacefully drifted to sleep.

————

Meanwhile, far away in the Galar Region, snowflakes fell across a vast snowy field.

Calyrex opened its eyes.

It felt a faint increase in the Power of Faith within its body again.

This familiar feeling reminded Calyrex of the sudden appearance of a human believer last time.

"Let's see what that believer has done this time,"

Calyrex focused its attention.

Peering through the vast distances of time and space, it once again saw that believer, the only human believer it had in this world.

In a space that seemed like an illusion, Calyrex saw that human crafting a shrine and an idol with their own hands, worshiping with several Pokémon in front of the shrine.

"... Oh King! Please bless my farm with favorable weather and abundant harvests this coming summer!"

Seeing the human in the image and the Berries placed before the shrine, Calyrex was deeply moved.

It's been a long time.

It had been a long time since it had seen such a human.

Even the most devout followers in the past had done no more than this, right?

"This human is truly devout to me!" Calyrex thought, feeling touched.

In the past, it would have waved both hands without hesitation to fulfill the wishes of this devout believer and his Pokémon.

Making their farm bloom with flowers, grass lush and green, fields fertile, and crops bountiful.

But now...

The Power of Faith within it was too weak, far from sufficient to fulfill that little follower's wishes.

"I'm sorry." Calyrex, powerless, was filled with apology in its heart.

Not only had it lost belief, its power weakened, even its two beloved horses had left it.

If only that believer was in the Galar Region, Calyrex wished.

It would ask for help in planting a special kind of carrot to attract those two steeds.

Because besides power from human faith, it had another way to restore power.

That was to reunite with Glastrier and Spectrier, then combine with them using the Reins of Unity to regain strength within its body.

However, unfortunately, Calyrex sensed that the believer was currently in a place outside the Galar Region.

————

The next evening, Zack came driving his large truck to pick up goods as usual.

Seeing him arrive while grooming his Gogoat, D quickly got up to move the goat milk he planned to sell today.

Zack was weighing the goat milk when he noticed the peculiarly shaped shrine behind the shipping crates, and asked:

"Hey? What's that?"

D explained, "That's a shrine I made by hand, dedicated to a very powerful Pokémon."

Hearing this, Zack was immediately intrigued, "Oh? What kind of Pokémon? Is it that crowned bunny over there?"

What the heck, a crowned bunny!

Zack was from Paldea and was understandably unfamiliar with the legends of the Galar Region.

D organized his thoughts and introduced, "That is a Pokémon named Calyrex."

"Calyrex?" Zack had never heard of this name before.

"Yes." D nodded and continued, "In the Galar Region, there's a story about Calyrex, said to have once ruled the Galar in ancient times and was known as the King of Bountiful Harvests."

"Though small and delicate in appearance, Calyrex possesses great wisdom, able to see all things past, present, and future."

"Moreover, it has the power to heal hearts and make plants sprout, and it is said that when it raises its right hand, flowers bloom, and grass grows, and if it waves its left hand, the fields become fertile and crops flourish."

"For this reason, Calyrex was also known as The King of Bountiful Harvests in the past, a Pokémon full of compassion."

Zack understood.

His eyes widened in surprise as he asked, "Such a Pokémon exists? Wouldn't it be perfect for farms?"

"Exactly." D responded, "Calyrex used its power in ancient times to help humanity, ensuring their crops were plentiful and sparing them from hunger."

Saying this, D suddenly felt as though he had turned into a missionary for Calyrex...

After some quick thought, he dismissed this strange idea and nodded to Zack, saying, "That's it."

---

# 125: Chapter 123: Teddiursa's Mysterious Gift

"I've lived so many years, and this is the first time I've heard of such a Pokemon." Zack murmured, "If the Paldea Region had a Pokemon like Calyrex, I believe the local farmers would be willing to worship it."

However, unfortunately, there are very few legends in the Paldea Region.

"Isn't that a good thing?" D chuckled, "Those Pokemon called legends are too far removed from humans, and their appearances always bring various crises to the human world."

"Just like the legends of the Earth God and the Sea God in the Hoenn Region, it is said that when they awaken, the entire Hoenn Region is affected by their clash."

"Continuous heavy rain falls from the sky, earthquakes and scorching sun spread everywhere; that is the real chaos!"

The Paldea Region is thousands of miles away from the Hoenn Region, quite distant.

The people living in Paldea know almost nothing about the legends circulating there.

But Zack could hear the magnitude of the disasters they brought from D's tone.

"Indeed!" Zack agreed, "Compared to that, our place is more livable."

Then, he curiously asked, "Did you learn all this while traveling outside?"

D hadn't traveled, although the original person had been out, he didn't deliberately learn about those legends.

Pausing for a moment, D nodded, "Yes, 'I' had been to many places before."

"Young people should travel more!" Zack grinned, "Seeing you now, you must be planning to settle here?"

D turned back to look at the Pokemon in the pasture.

He once dreamed of traveling around the Pokemon World, but now this stable, peaceful life wasn't bad.

Although lacking the adventure and thrills of travel, it was peaceful and warm.

So, he nodded, "Yes, I plan to live here for good."

D thought, although he's at the pasture now, it doesn't mean he can't travel.

Once the pasture is up and running, with a Pokemon steward, and finances aren't tight, he could leisurely visit other regions.

See Pallet Town, see the World Tree, see Mt. Coronet...

"That's good, that's good." Zack said with a hint of sentiment, "To be honest, when I first saw you, I thought you wouldn't last!"

"Life on the pasture is so hard, and being a farmer is very boring. Rarely do young people persist like you, remember Elif from the neighboring Cherry Garden?" Zack asked.

D nodded, "Of course."

In the spring, he attended the Sakura Blossom Wine Party hosted by Mr. Elif and received Cherry Wine from him, he was a very kind bearded uncle.

"His daughter, Sina, is clamoring to become a Trainer and travel with Pokemon on adventures!" Zack laughed, "Elif is too worried about her safety, doesn't want her to go, and they've been in a cold war for days."

"It's just human nature!" D said.

If he had a daughter, he would also worry, as in this world, kids can travel at ten.

A ten-year-old girl traveling alone would naturally cause concern.

D thought about it from another perspective; if it were his daughter, he might send three Gogoats, Dragonite, and Koraidon to guard her.

Zack, perhaps reminded of something, had a slight tear in his eye, "Yes, human nature, parents always worry about their children."

He looked up at the sky, faintly hearing the cicadas in the back mountain.

"Summer is coming!" Zack said.

D also heard the cicadas, he and Zack looked up at the sky together, took a deep breath, saying, "Yes!"

"Well, unknowingly talked so much." Zack laughed as he loaded the goat milk onto the cart, waved goodbye to D, "See you!"

"See you!" D also bid farewell.

Watching Zack leave, he turned around.

And at this moment, a Fearow suddenly flew across the evening sky.

The Fearow slowly landed in the pasture, looking at D with sharp eyes.

In its strong talons, it clutched a thick envelope.

"Huh?"

Seeing this wild Fearow from nowhere, D looked surprised.

He pointed to himself and asked, "For me?"

The Fearow let out a sharp cry and nodded to confirm.

D roughly guessed who the sender was.

He reached out and took the envelope from the Fearow's talons, then had Koraidon bring a box of Poffin he baked for the Fearow.

D took a piece of Poffin from the box and offered it.

The Fearow looked down at the Poffin emitting a strange aroma, then opened its long sharp beak and ate it.

As the Poffin entered, the Fearow's eyes brightened suddenly.

D smiled and said, "Thank you for delivering the letter for Teddiursa, this treat is for you!"

The Fearow looked visibly happy, an expression of joy evident to the naked eye.

It let out an excited cry, grabbed the box of Poffins with its talons, and flew toward the sunset.

"Aga-sss?"

Koraidon turned its gaze to the envelope.

It sensed an aura from the envelope that differed from normal Pokemon.

Seeing the different packaging from before, D looked surprised.

Did Teddiursa and Mew learn to send mail?

What would this gift be?

D opened the envelope, and the first thing he saw was a sharp yellow feather, with a faint trace of electric current swirling around it.

"A feather?"

D was even more surprised.

What kind of feather would a Teddiursa with the Pickup trait specifically pick up to send back to the pasture?

But more than that, D was curious about what Teddiursa had written on the postcard this time.

He opened the envelope, and inside wasn't a postcard, but a simple crayon drawing, seemingly done by a three-year-old.

The drawing showed a strangely shaped Pokemon.

From its wings and shape, it vaguely resembled a Bird-type Pokemon.

Its body was filled with yellow crayon, with thick yellow lines drawn beside it, seemingly representing currents released by this Bird-type Pokemon.

Additionally, the drawing still bore two cute paw prints.

These indicated the identity of the sender.

D placed the yellow feather and the Pokemon from the drawing together for comparison,

Then, as if realizing something, he was shocked.

This is a Zapdos feather!

At this moment, D's previous speculation became true.

With the Pickup trait and the honey candy effect, Teddiursa and Mew really encountered Zapdos and successfully got a feather from it!

---

---

---

---

# 126: Chapter 124: Legendary Ingredient!

In D's mind, an image appeared of Teddiursa mischievously pulling feathers from Zapdos.

Facing the Teddiursa that darted out from a corner, Zapdos angrily released its electric power.

But the Teddiursa, feather in hand, turned and ran, triggering its quick escape ability, dodging Zapdos's attack and successfully escaping from the Legendary Pokemon.

After the escape, Teddiursa and Mew arrived in the human city.

Perhaps unintentionally, they saw through the street window a child drawing in a room.

So, Mew and Teddiursa thought they could express themselves through drawing and depicted the encounter with Zapdos using crayons on paper.

Finally, they placed the letter and Zapdos's feather into an envelope and handed it to a Fearow friend they met on the road, asking it to help deliver it back to the ranch.

As for why it wasn't the previous Pelipper.

D guessed it might be because Mew and Teddiursa's journey was still ongoing, not staying too long in one place.

What a big discovery!

He was troubled by not having any Electric Type ingredients to cook with, but unexpectedly, Teddiursa delivered the ingredient today.

Of course, the premise is that Zapdos's feather can be used in cooking.

So, here comes the question, what cooking method should he use for this Zapdos feather?

Frying, stir-frying, deep-frying, steaming, braising, boiling...

None of them seem quite suitable.

And even if he manages to cook it, Dragonite and Pawmi probably won't want to swallow the feather whole, will they?

A decent dish must first whet the appetite of those who eat it.

Moreover, this yellow and sharp feather looks like it would scratch the throat.

Just the thought of it is discomforting.

Just then, Dragonite, who had been napping on the grass all afternoon, rubbed its eyes and sat up.

D approached with the feather, asking, "Do you want to eat this?"

Dragonite looked at the feather with a troubled expression.

"Roar..." This probably isn't edible, right?

Although it's a dopey dragon, it knows what can and cannot be eaten.

Meanwhile, small Pawmi, busy all day, seeing the sky darkening, herded the hens back to the Chicken House and eagerly headed towards D after work.

Then it heard D with a yellow feather asking, "Pawmi, would you eat this?"

"Pawmi?!"

Pawmi was startled, looking at the feather and sensing an extraordinary aura from it.

It's just that... this probably can't be eaten, can it?

Pawmi also found it very difficult.

Observing this, D thought to himself, as expected.

He pondered as he stood and headed towards the wooden house.

If direct consumption isn't possible, how about grinding it into powder?

After a moment's thought, D decided to give it a try.

However, he didn't currently have a grinder at home, so he gazed at the sky and, seeing it still had some time until dark, he called Koraidon and rode it to Porto Marinada Town.

D remembered that Keri had a grinder.

When he and Koraidon arrived at Keri's house, she was having dinner.

Seeing D visit at this hour, Keri was surprised, then cheerfully asked, "Are you planning to expand your facilities again?"

D: "…No."

He asked, "I'd like to borrow your grinder to grind this feather, is that okay?"

While saying this, D took out Zapdos's feather.

"Of course!" Keri stood up and led D to her workshop, casting her gaze on the feather in D's hand, asking, "Is that... a Pokemon feather?"

D nodded. He glanced around; Keri's workshop wasn't as tidy as he imagined, rather it was a bit messy.

Various tools and workbenches were piled together, wooden boards leaned against the wall, and shavings remained on the desk, not yet cleaned.

"I just made a batch of wooden boards this afternoon and haven't had time to clean up, so it's a bit of a mess," Keri explained, a little embarrassed.

"It's okay." D wasn't too concerned; he focused on the grinder.

Keri went up and plugged in the power, then pressed a button on the machine, "Alright, you can put it in now."

D nodded, then tossed the feather in.

Accompanied by the machine's buzzing sound, a small amount of yellow powder soon fell from the outlet.

D carefully collected all the powder into a bottle.

At the moment his gaze fell on the powder, information about them surfaced in his mind.

[Zapdos Feather Powder: A legendary ingredient, this powder contains a large amount of electric energy, possibly usable in cooking, effect unknown.]

Can it really?! D was delighted.

And its quality is legendary!

This was D's first time encountering an ingredient of this quality.

If he used it for cooking, what effect might the dish have?

D was eager to go home and try it out.

Seeing how precious he considered the feather powder, Keri was very curious: "Whose Pokemon feather is this anyway, making you show such expressions?"

D replied, "Zapdos."

"Zapdos?" Keri hadn't heard of this Pokemon before.

"In legend, the Thunder God, a Pokemon that can control thunder at will," D said.

There seems to be more than one Zapdos in the world, each with different strengths, but there's only one true Thunder God.

That would be the Zapdos residing on Lightning Island in the Lugia explosive birth movie.

If one could gather Zapdos, Moltres, and Articuno, one could summon the legendary Pokemon, Lugia, master of ocean currents and Hurricane.

I just wonder whose Zapdos feather this is.

"Though I've never heard of it, from what you've said, it must be a very powerful Pokemon, right?" Keri murmured.

"Yes! After all, it's called a legendary Pokemon," D nodded.

"But, I'm really curious, where did you get it from?" Keri asked.

"Of course, it was given to me by Teddiursa, you've seen it, at last month's Spring Livestock Contest."

Upon hearing this, Keri immediately thought of that Teddiursa following D at the contest, apologizing to other ranchers and farmers.

Keri: "Its ability is Pickup?"

D nodded: "That's right."

Keri was amazed: "Its luck is unbelievable! To find a feather that fell off a legendary Pokemon."

D grinned: "It's no ordinary Teddiursa! Alright, it's getting late, I won't bother you any longer, see you!"

Keri: "…Goodbye."

D, excited, rode on Koraidon and, not lingering in town, returned straight to the ranch.

He headed to the kitchen area, looking down at the prepared ingredients and utensils before him.

So, what should he make with the feather powder?

Staring at the bag of flour in the kitchen corner, D already had the answer in his heart.

Steamed buns!

He was going to add the legendary feather powder to the flour to make steamed buns!

---

---

---

---

# 127: Chapter 125: Super-Powered Electric Cuisine! (Double Chapter)

The process of making baozi is not complicated, and since there was still a packet of yeast left at home, D poured flour, sugar, and Zapdos feather powder into a bowl, and then added an appropriate amount of yeast powder.

There was still half a bottle of warm water in the kettle. D poured it into a bowl, took a sip to ensure the temperature was suitable, and then began adding water to the bowl.

He added it while stirring with his hand, and when it seemed just right, he began kneading the dough formally.

Gradually, the dough solidified and formed. The originally pure white flour was dyed the color of lightning by the Zapdos feathers, feeling a bit prickly to the touch.

"Hmm, that should be about right! We can start fermenting now!"

D observed the dough's state, then took out a sheet of plastic wrap, covered it, and placed it on warm water to start fermenting.

The suitable temperature and sugar both helped the dough ferment.

Next, all there was to do was wait!

D wondered what the baozi made with these ingredients would be like once steamed.

Speaking of baozi, the Pokemon World also has a special kind of baozi.

It's the specialty of Mahogany Town in the Johto Region, the Rage Candy Bar.

Its effect is to heal all abnormal statuses of a Pokemon.

"I should take the opportunity to visit Mahogany Town and observe,"

After pondering for a moment, D glanced at the time, saw it was already six o'clock, and started preparing dinner for the Pokemon.

When he turned around, he saw Cyclizar and Pawmi squatting on the floor, looking over at him.

"Just a moment, dinner will be ready shortly!" D stepped forward and picked up little Pawmi, telling it happily, "I've found a way to make you stronger!"

"Pawmi?!" Pawmi looked surprised and delighted upon hearing this.

D rubbed faces with it and said with a smile, "Just wait a little longer, it'll be ready soon."

"Pawmi~" Pawmi was extremely happy.

Cyclizar, sitting nearby, showed an envious expression: "Gao..."

It thought about how everyone in the pasture was getting stronger: Dragonite, Gogoat, Nacli, Pawmi.

Before it knew it, it had fallen behind them.

Thinking of this, Cyclizar couldn't help but show a hint of longing in its eyes.

"Gao!"

D looked at it and asked, "Do you also want to become stronger?"

Cyclizar nodded: "Gao!"

"Of course!" D said, "Let me think of a way."

Having said that, Cyclizar was actually his first Pokemon, and ever since he came to the farm, it had been helping with work diligently alongside him.

But now, he hadn't yet created a dish that could enhance a Dragon Type Pokemon...

After thinking for a moment, D decided to take the opportunity while the dough was fermenting to better understand the few Pokemon around him.

He took a pen and paper from the drawer and wrote down the name of each Pokemon on the farm, then listed a table next to their names.

This included preferred foods, temperament, hobbies, and mastered moves.

Take Dragonite, for instance, its table looked like this.

Name: Dragonite.

Temperament: Gentle and silly.

Hobbies: Enjoys training and diligently practicing its mastered moves.

Favorite foods: Aromatic Baofen, Pan-Fried Pork Chops, Grilled Fish, Steamed Sea Bass, Oran Berry.

Mastered Moves: Wing Attack, Leer, Twister, Dragon Rage, Dragon Tail, Agility, Extreme Speed, Dragon Dance, Rain Dance, Hurricane, Thunder Punch, Thunderbolt.

This way, as more and more Pokemon join the farm, he won't get them mixed up.

With this thought, D called the two Pokemon who wanted to become stronger, Cyclizar and Pawmi, outside to demonstrate their mastered moves.

The two Pokemon exchanged a glance, and then little Pawmi took the lead in the demonstration.

D watched its figure running on the grass, softly chanting the names of the moves.

"Scratch, Growl, Thunder Shock, Quick Attack, Nuzzle... huh? Is that Wish? It must be an inherited move handed down from Pawmi's parents."

Pawmi didn't know many moves, and most were very basic, so D noted them in his notebook and said, "That's good, Cyclizar, your turn!"

"Gao~" Cyclizar also began its demonstration.

This scene attracted many Pokemon on the farm, with Alcremie, the four Nacli and a Naclstack, Butterfree, Glaceon, and the Miltank grazing outside all standing nearby to watch.

D looked around and realized that quite a few of them were present, so he didn't need to call them over.

Then, he focused his attention on Cyclizar.

"Tackle, Growl, Rapid Spin, Dragon Tail, Breaking Swipe, Quick Attack... hmm, not too bad." After all, Cyclizar wasn't even a year old yet.

D nodded at it: "I understand, I'll think of a way to help you later."

Cyclizar was very happy.

Next up!

D turned his gaze towards the other watching Pokemon.

Understanding what D was doing, Alcremie immediately ran up front to start her performance.

The Nacli and Naclstack quickly followed.

Next were Butterfree, Combee, Glaceon, the three Miltank, Gogoat, and Smoliv, all watching from nearby.

Dragonite also wanted to join in, but D refused.

He told Dragonite, "I've been following your training closely; no one knows your moves better than I do."

After thinking about it for a moment, Dragonite realized this was certainly the case.

No one understood it better than D.

---

So it affectionately went forward and hugged D in its arms.

"Alright, alright, Koraidon is still waiting for me!" D laughed and patted its plump, soft paws.

Dragonite obediently stepped aside.

D had already written Koraidon's name in the notebook.

"It's your turn!"

"Argh!"

Koraidon nodded and then began its demonstration.

"Sunny Day, Collision Course, Flamethrower, Breaking Swipe, Rock Smash, Ancient Power, Drain Punch, Dragon Claw, Dragon Tail, Sword Dance, Outrage, Agility, Quick Attack, Body Press... huh? Is that Growl or Roar?"

Watching Koraidon roaring on the spot, D was initially unsure.

But when he saw Koraidon's fierce aura from its roar blowing the grass, he knew the answer.

Ah, it's a Roar!

D noted these moves in his notebook, then nodded to Koraidon: "Alright, that's enough!"

"Argh~" Koraidon walked back to his side.

D then looked up at the Pokemon who were still watching and waved at them: "Everyone go and play!"

The Pokemon dispersed one after another.

D sat under the eaves, flipping through his notebook while pondering how to help Cyclizar.

How about letting its ancient relative Koraidon teach it first?

Thinking of this, D called over Koraidon and explained the situation.

After listening, Koraidon had a complex expression.

It looked over to Cyclizar, momentarily at a loss for words.

D patted its shoulder: "I'll leave Cyclizar with you for the time being! You don't have to do much, just teach it the moves you know and that are easy to learn!"

"Grr..." Hearing D's plan, Cyclizar looked timidly at Koraidon.

Koraidon: "..."

"It's settled then!" D made the decision for them, then turned to Cyclizar and said, "Don't worry, Koraidon is a good Pokemon and won't harm you!"

Cyclizar looked at Koraidon, who maintained a serious face as it gazed back at Cyclizar with its golden vertical pupils.

Then it sighed resignedly—this was a request from its favorite D, what else could it do?

Watching Koraidon transform into a strict teacher, D smiled and said, "Alright, I need to start preparing dinner!"

With that, he turned back to the house to prepare food for everyone.

Tonight's dinner was once again pan-fried pork chops.

Because D found out that not only Dragonite, but Cyclizar and Koraidon also loved them.

Pan-fried pork chops, goat's milk, Berry Juice, sandwiches, and occasionally a little dessert like Poffin or honey pudding—this was the daily food for the Pokemon.

After dinner, with the dough not yet fully risen, D sat at his desk to check the recent financial situation.

In the past month or so, he hadn't built any additional facilities on the farm, so the money saved was all put into the bank after purchasing ingredients.

Gogoat's high-quality milk, Nacli's Purifying Salt, and Miltank's fresh milk.

One must admit, the resources of the Pokemon World are incredibly abundant, and thanks to these products, his savings had already reached over 900,000.

According to D's estimation, Miltank's production could have been higher.

However, in practice, he found that a single Miltank didn't actually produce 20 liters of milk per day.

Because they also drink their own milk, he couldn't very well stop them from drinking it.

Moreover, with a large number of Pokemon on the farm, the demand for milk was tremendous, so the income from MooMoo Fresh Milk wasn't as high as he had imagined.

But still, it was enough.

If he maintains this pace, he wouldn't have to wait until autumn; by the second month of summer, he could expand his house.

This truly is a dreamy world!

In his previous life, he wouldn't have dared to dream of buying a house within five months of work!

However, the goal of buying a villa was still quite distant.

By now, it was late outside.

All the Pokemon on the farm had returned to their nests.

And D turned on the TV, listening to the news while humming a tune as he checked the dough's fermentation progress in the kitchen.

Seeing it had almost risen enough, D took the dough out, placed it on the counter, and began the next step.

—He divided the dough into equal portions, then rolled them into round, smooth buns.

Soon, twelve round, plump yellow buns were rolled out.

Their color was very different from typical buns, not white, but yellow.

Looking at this strange color, D couldn't help but worry, wondering if they were safe to eat.

The main component of bird feathers is protein; he wondered whether Zapdos's feathers were the same.

Thinking this, D placed the twelve buns in a steamer to cook.

He set an alarm and then went outside for a wash.

As the weather was getting hotter, daily work made him sweat a lot, and he couldn't go a day without bathing.

But the farm still didn't have a proper bath, so he could only rinse off with a tap on the grass outside the door.

After his shower, D started to wash clothes.

Summer clothes were very thin and wouldn't take too long to wash; soon the clothes were done, and D hung them on the clothes rack beside the house to dry.

---

After finishing everything, the alarm he set went off precisely.

D immediately went to the kitchen and lifted the lid of the pot, a rich flour aroma wafting out to greet him.

The twelve steamed buns lay quietly in the pot, each looking so soft and fragrant.

D glanced down, and information about these buns surfaced in his mind.

[Lightning Steamed Bun (S+): Legendary Cuisine, made from the powdered feathers of a legendary Pokemon, said to allow the eater to harness lightning.

Cooking Effect: Thunder God, enables a Pokemon to gain the power of thunder and lightning, allowing it to control lightning at will, just like the owner of the feathers, with massive bolts of lightning dropping from the clouds with a snap of the fingers, limited to Electric Type Pokemon, and each Pokemon can only consume it once.

Review: A one-of-a-kind dish in the world, feed it to your Electric Type Pokemon! Warning, do not consume indoors!]

What?

D was slightly taken aback; his first thought about the bun was not its effect, but Dragonite.

Limited to Electric Type Pokemon, meaning that even if Dragonite, being a Dragon Type, ate it, it would be useless...

Looking at these steaming buns, at this moment D was eager to know what kind of exaggerated enhancement they could bring to a Pokemon.

The word "warning" even appeared in the review.

After a brief silence, D called Pawmi, taking it out to the grass outside.

The weather was great today, the sky was clear, and the stars and moon were visible.

Moonlight illuminated the ground, allowing one to see nocturnal things clearly even without lights.

"Pa-mo?" Pawmi, following D outside, looked puzzled.

D handed it a Lightning Steamed Bun.

"Eat it, and you'll become stronger!"

"Pa-mo?"

Hearing this, Pawmi's eyes immediately sparkled.

It had unconditional trust in D, using its little claws to take the bun from D's hand, eating it with great effort.

D watched Pawmi's state from the side.

At first, there was no reaction in Pawmi's body.

But when it swallowed the last bite of the bun, a crack of thunder suddenly boomed in the sky, shaking everything around.

D looked up to see a stunning and spectacular scene.

Out of nowhere, thick ominous thunderclouds gathered in the once clear sky.

They clustered above the meadow, forming a thunderous vortex as they swirled.

That sight made D think a deity might descend from it the next second.

But there was no god.

With a thunderous crash, an enormous blue lightning bolt descended from the vortex's base high in the sky, striking little Pawmi.

The powerful impact caused D to stagger backward repeatedly, while a strong gust billowed out from Pawmi.

Koraidon appeared from somewhere, catching the nearly fallen D and helping him up.

"Gah-szzz..."

Most of the Pokemon in the meadow were drawn by this sudden shift.

Together with D, they watched what was happening to Pawmi.

Clad in blue lightning, Pawmi's eyes were flooded with a light so blue it seemed white, as if transformed into a deity, gritting its teeth in apparent agony, like enduring a divine tribulation.

This scene reminded D of the description of Zapdos.

It was said that when Zapdos rubbed its wings, massive lightning would immediately descend from the clouds.

When the sky darkened with continuous lightning, this legendary Pokemon would appear, and if struck by lightning, a great power would surge within it.

Suddenly, a growl snapped D out of his thoughts.

"Pa-mo——!"

Hearing Pawmi's cry, D's heart skipped a beat.

"Pawmi?"

He immediately wanted to intervene in the ceremony and pull Pawmi out.

But in the next second, D saw a familiar intense glow emanate from Pawmi.

Amid the glow, Pawmi's form rapidly changed, its body enlarging, and its ears lengthening.

From its original quadruped stance, it slowly and steadily stood upright.

Evolution?!

As the airflow surged, Pawmi—no, Pawmo's fur swayed along with it.

The transformation had not stopped.

Pawmo slowly opened its eyes, its gaze now much more resolute than in its previous stage.

Incredibly, it lifted its feet off the ground, gently levitating in the electricity!

Flying… it flew?!

D stood there, dumbfounded, watching this scene.

Suddenly, a rather inappropriate thought popped into his mind—

If it donned a helmet, a red cloak, and wielded a hammer, Pawmo could surely cosplay Thor right now.

And if there was a Tinkaton around, maybe it could forge a hammer for Pawmo.

---

---

---

---

# 128: Chapter 126: For the Glory of the Northern God!

The thunderclouds in the sky slowly dissipated.

Everything around returned to silence, and the Pawmo floating in mid-air also slowly landed on the grass.

It opened its determined eyes and looked over with some excitement.

"How do you feel? Have you gotten stronger?" D hurriedly asked about Pawmo's condition.

"Bamo!" Pawmo nodded repeatedly.

After evolving, the electric sacs on its cheeks were now very developed, and it could release electricity even without friction.

To demonstrate for D, a current crackled from Pawmo's cheeks.

Then, its entire body was enveloped in a spark, and a powerful Thunder Shock shot out.

D nodded and murmured, "Looks like I'll have to add Thunderbolt to Pawmi's notes."

"Is there anything else?" D asked.

Pawmo nodded its head and then began to demonstrate the moves it learned after evolving.

Besides Thunderbolt, there was also Thunder Punch, Discharge, Electro Ball, Electric Terrain...

Looking at those moves, D felt very shocked.

The effect of that Lightning Steamed Bun was really exaggerated, wasn't it?

That heavenly thunder not only evolved Pawmi into Pawmo but also allowed it to learn so many moves, truly deserving of the name "Thunder God."

One can only say that Legendary Cuisine is terrifying indeed!

A single feather of Zapdos has such great power. Then what if it's a feather from Ho-oh or Lugia?

Or, if it's a feather from Moltres or Articuno, made into Flame Steamed Bun or Frozen Steamed Bun, would it have a "Fire God" or "Ice God" effect?

And if we think even more boldly, instead of using feathers, what if we use Zapdos directly to make the cuisine?

This thought flashed through D's mind, but he quickly dismissed it.

Knowing that Pokemon have emotions and thoughts no less than humans, it would be too cruel to harm them.

Gradually, D calmed down.

Because these Pokemon feathers are not that easy to obtain.

For Teddiursa to pick up a feather from Zapdos was already a huge stroke of luck, let alone the others, which he dared not even think about.

Then, D recalled the scene when Pawmo's feet left the ground earlier.

He continued to ask, "Did you just fly?"

"Bamo?" Pawmo seemed confused.

But soon, it seemed to have thought of something, and eagerly nodded.

"Ba—mo!"

Pawmo started gathering energy, and a yellow electricity spread from its body in all directions.

The next second, Pawmo's feet slowly left the ground and it actually floated up.

Did it really fly?!

D widened his eyes and tentatively said, "Try moving around?"

"Bamo!"

Pawmo nodded and then started to try.

At first, Pawmo was very unfamiliar, with a flight posture that looked a bit clumsy.

But soon, it seemed to grow accustomed to the feeling and flew more skillfully.

Pawmo experienced the joy of flying for the first time, and with that feeling, it excitedly flew back and forth over the pasture.

D watched this scene, mumbling, "I wonder what Groudon would feel seeing this."

No matter what Groudon would feel, D was genuinely happy for Pawmo at that moment.

Tomorrow, he would make a trip to town to buy a beautiful and bright red cloak for Pawmo to cosplay as Thor.

In this world, there's Hat Pikachu, Rock Pikachu, Surfing Pikachu, so Pawmo with a cloak seems reasonable, right?

After flying a circle, Pawmo rushed over.

D opened his arms to catch it, and Pawmo, as usual, charmed D.

"Bamo~"

Though it evolved, Pawmo was still fluffy, and the paw pads felt even softer. D couldn't help but take a big sniff.

"Alright, we can go back now!"

He held Pawmo as he went back to the room.

Next up, the remaining Lightning Steamed Buns needed to be dealt with.

Legendary Cuisine was very rare, and D instinctively wanted to store them in the fridge, saving them for when more Electric Type Pokemon joined the pasture so they could consume them.

However, even if they are put in the freezer, over time, the food might spoil.

What should be done?

Just then, Dragonite, who witnessed Pawmi's transformation, looked hopefully at the buns.

D said, "These buns have no effect on you; only Electric Type Pokemon like Pawmi can grow stronger after consuming them."

Hearing this, Dragonite felt very disappointed.

"Awoo..."

D: "..."

Fine, since they might spoil if kept too long, might as well share them with everyone to taste!

So he distributed the remaining buns to the Pokemon on the pasture, and out of curiosity, D tried one himself.

Well, the taste wasn't much different from a normal bun, just a slightly tingling sensation in the mouth, though not strong.

Maybe due to the residual Electric Type Energy in the feathers?

The buns weren't large.

D finished it in a few bites, then looked at the two on the pasture capable of using Electric Type moves: Dragonite and Butterfree, and asked, "How do you feel?"

Dragonite carefully sensed it, then raised a fist to reveal a stronger current than before.

"Awoo?!"

D also noticed this situation.

He couldn't help feeling puzzled, was there some hidden effect?

After pondering for a moment, D brought them outside and said to Dragonite, "Show me a Thunderbolt."

Dragonite obediently complied.

Its body was surrounded by sparks, releasing a stronger Thunderbolt.

"Indeed, the Electric Type moves' power has increased," D murmured.

He then looked at Butterfree: "Butterfree, how about you? How do you feel?"

"Muyi~"

Butterfree flapped its wings and used the Electroweb it learned during its Caterpie days.

Sparks flickered as an electrified web fell to the ground.

"The power seems greater than before as well." D confirmed the hidden effect of the Lightning Steamed Bun in his heart, which can enhance the power of Electric Type moves.

For non-Electric Type Pokemon, it was also a pretty good effect.

He said to the two Pokemon, "Congratulations, Dragonite, Butterfree."

"Awoo~"

"Muyi~"

"Alright now, time to sleep!" D grinned.

Everyone had gained something today, which was truly something to be happy about!

D and his Pokemon bid each other goodnight, then he took Pawmo to bed.

Perhaps because it finally achieved its dream, Pawmo slept especially soundly tonight.

I wonder what it dreamed of, D thought, as even in its sleep, Pawmo seemed to be charming someone, and like a child, showed an expression of attachment.

Seeing this scene, a smile involuntarily appeared on D's face, and he gently patted Pawmo's furry body.

He closed his eyes and soon drifted into dreamland.

---

---

---

---

# 129: Chapter 127: Tinkaton Anecdotes

Early the next morning, D was preparing breakfast when suddenly there was a knock at the door.

He went to open the door and saw Mayor Thomas standing outside with a worried expression.

D was a bit surprised: "Is the town hosting another event?"

"Not so soon!" Mayor Thomas said, "Last night many people in town saw lightning at the ranch, and I was worried something might have happened, so I came to check."

Lightning...

D looked down at Pawmo beside him and said, "That was my Pokemon practicing the Thunder move."

"Is that so?" Mayor Thomas sighed in relief, "Good thing nothing happened. You continue with your work then. I have things to tend to in town, so I won't disturb you."

Having said that, Mayor Thomas turned around and left.

"Pawmo..."

Pawmo was slow to realize what was happening.

Was that human talking about it just now?
