The disturbance caused by last night's lightning was indeed too big.

"It's okay, don't worry." D comforted him, turning back to the kitchen to bring the prepared breakfast to the table.

Today's breakfast was sandwiches paired with milk porridge. Although Koraidon, Dragonite, and Pawmo couldn't produce milk, milk porridge was still beneficial for their body growth and development.

Several Pokemon sat around the wooden table and began to eat.

D glanced at the old wall clock and started planning his day's schedule in his mind.

Aside from going to town to restock household supplies and groceries, he also needed to take care of the overgrown weeds at the ranch.

Because of the Grassy Seed's presence, Gogoat virtually stopped eating the wild grass on the ground.

Miltanks would only eat the patch of grass cultivated in front of the Pokemon House.

"Just right, the cut grass can be kept for Gogoat to make Grassy Seed."

D thought aloud while watching the Pokemon eating voraciously, his gaze accidentally meeting Pawmo's.

"Hurry up and finish eating! I've got to make a trip to town." He said.

Pawmo immediately sped up its eating.

Soon, breakfast time was over.

All the Pokemon at the ranch went about their activities, whether playing or working.

Meanwhile, D rode Cyclizar to town.

With so many Pokemon, the ranch consumed a large amount of meat daily.

The meat would lose its taste if kept too long, prompting him to regularly shop in town.

At the department store, D pushed a small shopping cart along the aisles, Cyclizar following him and curiously looking at the items on the shelves.

"Hmm, we're out of detergent, seems we've used up the vinegar too, and I should get two more boxes of soap."

D placed the household items needed at the ranch into the small cart, and finally purchased ten kilograms of meat.

The price of this meat was 980 League currency per pound, so for ten kilograms, or twenty pounds, he had to spend a total of 19,600 League currency.

Though it seemed like a lot, for meat-loving Koraidon, Cyclizar, and Dragonite, this was only a few days' supply.

Meaning, just the cost of meat each month was quite a hefty expense.

Fortunately, he hadn't gotten carried away and brought back all the Dragonites from Dragonite Island to the ranch.

Otherwise, he'd really be eaten into poverty.

After paying, D handed some items to Cyclizar to carry.

Instead of heading back straight to the ranch, they stopped by a children's clothing store on the street.

There, D saw the main purpose of this trip—a brightly colored red children's cloak.

The cloak was priced at 1500 League currency.

It's decided, it's you!

D bought it without hesitation, then rode Cyclizar back to the ranch to find Pawmo, who was tending to the chickens.

When he arrived at the Chicken House, Pawmi was happily floating in the air, watching the hens below pecking at food.

D waved at it through the fence, and Pawmi immediately flew over.

"Come, try it on, see if you like it?"

D tied the cloak around Pawmo's neck.

Pawmo looked curiously at the cloak behind it, and as the wind blew, the vibrant red cloak flapped in the breeze.

"Try flying up and see?" D suggested.

"Pawmi!"

Pawmi nodded its small head and then swooped into the sky.

The cloak behind it rustled in the wind, looking very stylish, like a superhero in comics fighting evil with a sense of justice.

Really cool!

Pawmo was already very cute, but now with a cloak, its charm skyrocketed.

Soon, Pawmo also seemed to find it fun, as it excitedly flew back and forth in the air.

After a while, it landed in front of D, its tone full of surprise, "Pawmi!"

It was clear that Pawmi liked this gift very much.

D smiled, looking at Pawmi's appearance, thinking, "Hmm, just missing a small hammer. Seems like it's time to catch a Tinkaton."

Tinkaton is a very special Pokemon, like Miltank, only females exist and no males.

It's highly intelligent, with a bold personality, a petite stature, yet it holds a hammer that's much larger than its own body.

Rumor has it that those hammers are made from metals collected from Bisharp.

To craft large and sturdy hammers, Tinkaton actively attacks groups of Bisharp to gather metals.

Tinkaton, whose appearance and strength cause a huge sense of disparity, also becomes a nightmare to numerous Pokemon as they breed and live.

Aside from Bisharp, Corviknight also suffers greatly.

It's said that Tinkaton especially loves to hit rocks with the hammer to attack Corviknight flying in the sky, then brings it back to its dwelling to take away their steel-like feathers.

This is precisely the reason why Corviknight cannot work as taxis in the Paldea region.

If they're attacked by Tinkaton during flight, it poses a serious danger to passengers.

"If I had Tinkaton, I could bring it to the mines in Zapapico Town to mine for ores." D thought.

The soil rich in minerals eaten by Nacli typically comes from Zapapico Town.

By then, he and Tinkaton could snack on some Lucky Pudding, and rare stones like diamonds, gold, and agate would be easy to get, even making Steven envious!

However, D had one more thing to tell Pawmo.

He remembered that for Pawmo to evolve to its final stage, Pawmot, it didn't require leveling up but a specific number of exercise steps.

In the game, Pawmo needed to run 1,000 steps to evolve into Pawmot.

Though he wasn't sure if this was the case in reality, D still informed Pawmo of the conditions for its evolution.

"Pawmi?" Pawmi's face was filled with determination.

Evolve into Pawmot?

It understood now!

---

---

---

---

# 130: Chapter 128: The Muscleman's Astonishment

As they spoke, Butterfree suddenly landed next to D, its gaze directed towards the entrance of the ranch.

"Um hmm..."

Following its line of sight, D saw a familiar figure standing there.

A strong man, full of muscles.

D raised his eyebrows slightly and stepped forward. Before he could speak, Everett waved his arms and greeted him.

"Long time no see, D! Remember me? We met once on Glaseado Mountain!"

D: "Of course!"

He had a deep impression of this Trainer who only captured muscle-type Pokémon.

D welcomed him into the ranch.

Everett looked around, seeing the neatly arranged patches of soil, which were visibly loose, seemingly just tilled and not yet planted with crops.

Everett looked surprised: "What the heck, you really are a farmer?!"

Everett hadn't believed it at all. To prove his suspicion, he came to Porto Marinada Town, and upon asking around, he found there really was a D Ranch nearby.

"So, I've never lied to you!" D said helplessly. "As you can see, I just tilled this land a few days ago, planning to plant summer crops soon."

He began strolling around the ranch with Everett, introducing this strong man to his ranch.

"This is the sheep pen. The magical goat milk I mentioned before comes from these three Gogoats."

"This is the Salt House. The Nacli and Naclstack here produce Rock Salt, which you can also find at Porto Marinada Market."

"Over there is where the Miltanks live. You've probably had the MooMoo Milk they produce, right?"

Everett nodded. He had indeed had it, and it was because of the MooMoo Milk that he grew so muscular.

"Here is the Chicken House. The hens are just ordinary hens, and their eggs are sold at the Porto Marinada Market, eventually turned into fried eggs for people to eat."

"As for that Pawmo in front of the door, its current job is as the chicken coop manager of the ranch."

Everett looked up, wide-eyed, at the Pawmo donned in a red cape and floating steadily in mid-air, asking in a daze:

"Is that a Pawmo?"

D nodded: "As you can see."

Everett: "…Although I'm not from Paldea, I know Pawmo are Electric Type and Fighting Type. How can it possibly fly?!"

D was already accustomed to such matters.

"That's because this Pawmo is a genius!" He began to speak earnestly with nonsense: "You know about Levitate, right?"

"…Yes."

"Pawmo has realized this ability through practice." His reasoning was convincing.

Is that so? Everett was utterly baffled.

At that moment, a dazzling green light suddenly erupted from the other side of the ranch.

Turning his head, Everett saw that the Gogoat, which had been lying on the ground eating, had somehow stood up.

Then, an unbelievable scene unfolded.

The three Gogoats were continuously enveloped in green light, expanding to cover the entire sheep pen, and even extending outward.

It seemed less like a sheep pen and more like a starry stage in Pokémon Hollywood.

He, a traveling Trainer, was like a member of the audience watching the performance.

"Wait, wait, wait!" Everett held his head, feeling like everything in front of him was beyond his comprehension: "Are those still the Gogoats I know?"

D: "…"

Everett rubbed his eyes, confirming that his eyes weren't deceiving him.

He thought of that Koraidon, then the flying Pawmo, and now these three strange Gogoats before him.

In that moment, Everett couldn't help but ask: "Did you also breed these Pokémon?"

A country farmer, living in a backward seaside town, how could he possibly breed so many powerful Pokémon?! He roared internally.

"Yes," D said earnestly. "But compared to the little girl who became an Elite at six, I've got a long way to go!"

Everett took a deep breath: "…Where is there a six-year-old Elite girl?"

Just then, D suddenly realized that Poppy, one of Paldea's Elite Four, hadn't been born yet.

He said mysteriously: "One day you'll hear her name."

Hearing this, Everett had no idea what he was thinking, but he quickly said: "Wait! You're not planning to pass on these Pokémon to your daughter after you get married and have children, so she can become an Elite, are you?"

"…"

D was momentarily speechless and switched topics, beginning to promote his goat milk.

Just then, Everett saw the young man before him mysteriously produce a bottle of goat milk.

"Would you like a bottle? It's fresh goat milk that can make Pokémon stronger—only 600 League Coins per bottle!"

Everett looked at the three Gogoats and asked: "Is it because of drinking the goat milk that they became so powerful?"

D thought for a moment and replied: "It's just one of the reasons."

Upon hearing this, Everett fell into thought.

He recalled hearing about D Ranch when he inquired at Porto Marinada Town and learning from the townsfolk about this miraculous goat milk sold there.

Rumor had it that after drinking this goat milk, one would feel completely energized, and Pokémon that lost their ability to battle would recover immediately after drinking it.

There were even stories of bedridden patients walking again after regularly drinking the goat milk.

But... could such an ordinary-looking bottle of goat milk truly have such miraculous effects?

After pondering for a moment, Everett pulled out a wallet and took out several League Coins: "I'll take one bottle!"

"Alright."

D waved his hand, signaling Koraidon to grab a bottle of packaged goat milk from the fridge.

Soon, the goat milk was in Everett's hands.

He didn't hesitate, opening the cap and drinking it all in one go.

Quickly, he felt a change within his body, exclaiming in surprise: "My body is heating up!"

"Do you feel less tired, like you have endless energy?" D asked.

Everett nodded repeatedly: "Exactly!"

"See, I didn't lie to you?" D smiled.

"So amazing!"

After personally experiencing the effects of the goat milk, Everett was amazed.

He truly believed that by regularly drinking this goat milk, both Pokémon and humans could become stronger!

Scenes of Everett and his Incineroar, with their muscular bodies, winning a world-class bodybuilding championship flashed through his mind.

He swiftly pulled out his wallet, counting the money before handing most of it out, leaving just enough for meals and travel expenses:

"I want ten bottles to take away!"

"Alright, please wait a moment."

A moment later.

D and Koraidon stood in the ranch, watching the contented Everett leave, and couldn't help but sigh.

"Looks like my selling skills aren't bad at all!"

"Aaah gah eee~"

D chuckled, turning around to walk back.

Then, out of nowhere, he heard the lively chirping of cicadas from the back mountain.

The river flowed with a gentle gurgle, and green lotus leaves grew on the river's surface.

A few wild Psyducks floated down from upstream.

As they floated past the ranch, they tilted their heads, staring blankly at the scene on the shore, then grabbed a lotus leaf and swam to the river's opposite bank, starting to hop around on that patch of grass.

The air temperature gradually rose, carrying a hint of warmth, as if declaring: Summer is here! Summer is here!

---

---

---

---

# 131: Chapter 129: Summer Returns

League Year 198, April 1st, Summer.

Early in the morning, the farm was bustling with activity.

Led by Alcremie, several Nacli were stacked on the evolved Naclstack, running around the grass.

Glaceon lay quietly on the grass, watching them.

Butterfree and Combee buzzed around the blooming flower fields, diligently collecting nectar.

The Miltank chewed on the dew-covered fertile grass, leisurely basking in the sun.

Pawmo, donned in a Red Cloak, floated above the Chicken House, watching the chickens below.

The Gogoat crowded around the feeding trough, greedily eating Grassy Seeds.

Not far behind them, Smoliv stretched out the green leaves on its head, seemingly wanting the intense sunlight to reach every corner.

Nearby the fields, D was directing several Pokemon to help with work.

"Koraidon, go get the seeds from the storage room for me, yes, the big box to the left of the entrance!"

"Dragonite, don't rush to do Rain Dance yet. Oh, by the way, did you deliver the milk today? You did? Great, hold on a moment, we'll plant the seeds with Cyclizar, and then you can do Rain Dance."

"Cyclizar, did you learn Dragon Claw from Koraidon before?"

"Gawoo!" Cyclizar nodded happily.

D nodded in satisfaction: "Great, let's practice it here then! Use Dragon Claw on the ground!"

"Gawoo!"

Cyclizar let out a growl, then extended its claws as a strong white light burst forth, forming two sharp white claws.

Aiming at the slightly soft ground below, Cyclizar slashed hard, instantly leaving marks like those of a plow.

"It seems you've pretty much mastered this move." D nodded approvingly, "Well done!"

"Gawoo~" Cyclizar looked a bit bashful.

"Alright then, let's continue!"

D picked up a hoe, looking at the vast land before him, starting a new round of planting with Cyclizar.

With the hoe and Dragon Claw continuously at work, in joint efforts, they quickly completed the plowing.

D received the seeds from Koraidon and summoned the Pokemon, giving each a bundle.

"You've all seen how I sow seeds, right?" D asked.

Koraidon, Dragonite, and Cyclizar stood in a row, each holding a packet of seeds, nodding at his words.

"Ah-chi!"

"Awoo~"

"Gawoo!"

"Very well." But fearing confusion, D demonstrated it again.

From the bag, he took out some seeds and placed them in the dug pit, saying:

"Just like this, take the seeds out and put them in, then cover the pit. However, be careful; don't sow too many seeds at once because they will compete for nutrients, and none will grow well."

The three Pokemon listened intently but confusedly.

Seeing this, D gave them an example: "Take the Gogoat, I only give them those Grassy Seeds daily."

"The current amount is just enough, but if ten more Gogoat come, there won't be enough. They won't be full and will get weaker and eventually sick from malnutrition."

This time, the Pokemon understood.

"Let's begin then!" Check the seed types, D instructed: "Cyclizar, you've got wheat seeds; plant them in that plot!"

"Koraidon, you're holding watermelon seeds, see that patch; go plant there."

"Dragonite, you've got melon seeds; plant them here."

With instructions given, the three Pokemon went off to work.

D, holding a packet of hop seeds, headed to the spot where he'd set up scaffolding and diligently planted the seeds.

Days earlier, with some free time, he had cultivated a large area on the farm, partitioned into four sections.

One was planted with corn, another with pumpkins, a third with pineapples, and the last with sunflowers.

D planned to create a Sunflora Field there; this crop is both beautiful and practical, producing sunflower seeds which can be pressed for oil and roasted into snacks.

In the cold winter, he could stay home watching TV, snacking on sunflower seeds.

Alcremie produces creamy caramel, and using this to roast seeds would yield a large batch of caramel-flavored snacks.

Unfortunately, Smoliv hasn't evolved yet, otherwise he wouldn't need to buy oil from town; the oil from Smoliv would suffice for farm use.

Time ticked by, and it wasn't until noon that all the fieldwork was done.

As the temperature rose, D felt the intense sunlight and dry air, thinking to himself, this is summer!

Smoliv loves this weather since it can turn the abundant sunlight into nutrients.

During a break, D glanced over and indeed saw Smoliv basking comfortably in the sun on the lawn.

With fieldwork done, the Pokemon were panting heavily from exhaustion.

D used a hose to wash the sweat off them.

"Good job!"

Dragonite, direct as always, performed Rain Dance, drawing a cloud over the grass it stood on.

In no time, the sun was covered, and a light drizzle began.

Dragonite shook its head with relish in the rain.

Outside this area, it was still a bright Sunny Day.

D looked at this incredulously.

At this moment, several wild Psyduck across the farm were drawn in, gazing curiously in this direction.

With hands propping their heads, they looked silly, hopping around on the grass, seemingly wanting to come play in the water, too.

D noticed them.

Occasionally, some wild Pokemon pass by and stop briefly on the farm.

Like Bird-type Pokemon flying overhead, or Bug-type Pokemon that wandered in.

Those Psyduck drifted down from upstream a few days ago, perhaps because the water flowed slower here, allowing them to reach the shore.

Since then, these wild Psyduck have been living on the opposite grass of the farm.

D shook his head, focusing on the task at hand.

Seeing Cyclizar and Koraidon were almost rinsed, he went inside to get a dry towel, drying the water off them.

At the same time, pondering what to have for lunch today.

Feeling this slightly warm weather, watching the Pokemon playing on the farm, D suddenly had a brilliant idea for a new dessert.

Got it!

---

---

---

---

# 132: Chapter 130: Creamy Smoothie

That's the cream milkshake!

Just in time, we have the full set of machines and ingredients at home.

MooMoo Fresh Milk, Gogoat's milk, Alcremie's cream, Berries...

The machine is a brand-new multifunctional juicer, which he bought from the town just yesterday, initially intending to make Berry Juice for the Pokemon to drink, but unexpectedly, it found its use here.

Thinking of this, D turned around and returned to his room.

He opened the refrigerator and took out a can of milk and a few Berries.

But there were no ice cubes in the fridge. After thinking for a moment, D found the Glaceon outside and asked it for some ice cubes.

"Vee?"

Glaceon looked a bit puzzled, but still used its Ice Type Energy to condense an ice chunk and handed it to him.

"You'll see later." D winked mysteriously at Glaceon.

Glaceon was even more confused: "Vee?"

D didn't explain and instead returned to his room the way he came.

He put the prepared MooMoo Fresh Milk, white sugar, ice cubes, and a Berry called Yache Berry into the juicer, turned it on, and began making the milkshake.

As the machine spun rapidly, the ice cubes were crushed, beginning to gradually merge with the Yache Berry and milk.

Yache Berry is a blue Berry, with a very unique taste. The resulting milkshake had a sky-blue color.

Next, D began preparing the cream.

He called over Alcremie.

Upon hearing that D needed help making a dessert, Alcremie rushed over to help make a big bowl of light cream.

"It's almost ready," D said.

With a ding, the machine stopped, and D unplugged it, pouring the milkshake into several prepared transparent glass cups.

Looking at Alcremie's cream, as white as a cloud, then at the sky-blue milkshake, an image suddenly appeared in his mind.

——Clear blue skies with fluffy white clouds floating, creating a leisurely and tranquil feeling.

Got it!

D didn't follow the original process of pouring all the milkshake into the cups in one go; instead, he poured a layer of milkshake first, then added a dollop of cream, repeating until the glasses were filled.

From the outside, it looked as if fluffy white clouds were floating in the blue sky.

D mentally named it "Cloud Milkshake" and finished it by swirling the cream into a spiral shape as a topping, then decorating it with a fresh red Cheri Berry.

Thus, a visually and flavorfully delightful cream milkshake was completed.

Alcremie, watching the cup of Cloud Milkshake from the side, showed admiration in its eyes: "Ma-ma!" Truly D, how amazing! Just like real clouds!

"It's done!"

D grinned while looking down, and the information about the cream milkshake emerged in his mind.

[Cloud Milkshake (B+): A delicious dessert made with top-quality fresh milk, ice cubes made by Pokemon, Alcremie's cream, and Berries.

Cooking Effect: Slightly increases an Ice Type Pokemon's chance of learning Ice Type Moves after consumption.

Additional Effect: Slightly boosts an Ice Type Pokemon's fondness.

Evaluation: A dessert that can make Pokemon feel happiness!]

Hiss!

Looking at the familiar description, a dish he once made suddenly came to D's mind.

That's the Grassy Seed!

Grassy Seed slightly increases a Grass Type Pokemon's chance of learning Grass Type Moves.

The originally ordinary Skiddos transformed into three Grass Saint Swordsmen after eating a large number of Grassy Seeds.

D had always been trying to see if he could create a dish with similar effects but had yet to succeed.

But now...

Finally, it happened!

Just like the saying goes: effort doesn't always lead to success, but sometimes goodness comes unexpectedly.

Coincidentally, there happens to be an Ice Type Pokemon at the ranch.

So, if Glaceon eats cream milkshakes every day, could it transform like the three Skiddos into an "Ice God"?

With bootleg Grass Saint Swordsmen Gogoat and bootleg Thunder God Pawmo, it seems a bootleg Ice God is about to be born.

His ranch is truly a place of great talents!

D felt excited and accelerated the speed of his hands.

Soon, a dozen cream milkshakes were all ready.

D called for Cyclizar and Koraidon to help distribute them to everyone.

He himself took a cup to find Glaceon on the grass.

"Want to try?" D placed the cup of cream milkshake in front of it.

"Vee..."

Glaceon stared blankly at the cream milkshake before it.

From a distance, it could already smell the sweet aroma emanating from it and sense that icy, cool scent.

Glaceon loved sweets, though no one knew about this preference.

It swallowed a bit secretly and then pretended to be calm and stood up, bending down to stick out its little pink tongue to gently lick the cool cream on top.

In an instant, Glaceon was captivated by the taste.

So delicious!

"Tasty, right?"

Seeing the usually cool Glaceon eating so happily, D couldn't help but smile a little.

Perhaps Glaceon has a liking for sweets? He quietly memorized this hobby of Glaceon's in his heart.

Glaceon licked the entire cup of cream milkshake clean in one go.

Next up, the main event.

D observed it and asked, "Do you feel any changes in your body?"

"Vee?" Glaceon was a bit confused; it carefully sensed and lightly shook its head.

"It seems this cup of cream milkshake didn't help Glaceon learn any Ice Type Moves," D thought.

However, he wasn't in a rush. As long as Glaceon continues to eat the milkshakes, it will eventually learn more powerful Ice Type Moves.

Thinking of this, D asked again, "Want some more?"

"Vee..."

Glaceon wanted to eat more, but it felt a bit embarrassed, as it feared it would appear as a glutton in front of this human.

Seeing Glaceon's hesitant expression, D chuckled.

"Vee!"

Realizing its thoughts had been exposed, Glaceon's face turned red, and it ran away in shame and indignation.

"This Glaceon's personality is quite interesting," thought D.

At first glance, it seemed very aloof, but the more he interacted with it, the more he realized it wasn't as cold as it appeared.

For instance, it liked flowers and often went to the edge of the flower fields to inhale the scent of the blossoms.

It also liked sweets, which is not at all a hobby one would expect from a seemingly cold and distant Pokemon.

Watching Glaceon's back, D realized this was a great opportunity to deepen his relationship with it.

Maybe if he made a few more cream milkshakes, he could freely cuddle with Glaceon!

---

---

---

---

# 133: Chapter 131: Summer and the Night of Fireflies (4k)

D turned back to the cabin, preparing to freeze some ice cubes in the refrigerator using molds so he wouldn't always have to trouble Glaceon for ice.

On the way back, D saw Cyclizar, who had just finished eating a creamy ice, practicing moves with Koraidon.

He stopped, standing beside Koraidon, watching Cyclizar practice diligently.

He had to admit, Koraidon was a good teacher, and Cyclizar was a dedicated student.

D had been observing the interaction between these two Pokemon.

Initially, Koraidon didn't seem very interested in teaching Cyclizar.

Cyclizar also had a sense of fear towards the fierce Koraidon.

Yet, despite this, Cyclizar persisted.

Seeing its effort, Koraidon's attitude towards Cyclizar gradually improved, and it started teaching earnestly.

The move they were practicing this time seemed to be Dragon Pulse.

This is a special attack move, where you mobilize the Dragon Type Energy in your body to your mouth and then unleash it towards the enemy in one go.

Koraidon's teaching method was straightforward and simple, first demonstrating itself, then letting Cyclizar follow its moves to learn.

But from Cyclizar's perspective, it could only see Koraidon unleashing the Dragon Pulse but didn't know how to do it itself.

Therefore, Cyclizar never quite grasped the essentials of this move.

D watched from the side for a while before speaking up, "Try concentrating the Dragon Type Energy in your body into your mouth?"

Hearing this, Koraidon let out a helpless growl, "Agh..."

This technique had already been explained to Cyclizar, but Cyclizar, or rather its past self, wasn't too smart.

"Gao?"

Cyclizar blinked, then tried again.

It gathered all the Dragon Type Energy in its body into its mouth, causing its cheeks to puff up with the large amount of energy.

D said, "Yes, just like that! Spit it out!"

Cyclizar tried hard to spit them out, but failed, swallowing all that energy down its stomach in one gulp.

Cyclizar opened its mouth wide, as if it burped, letting out a puff of air.

D: "..."

Koraidon: "..."

It couldn't even look directly.

Cyclizar's face turned bright red, and its eyes moistened, looking as if it might cry,

"Gao..."

"Alright, alright, take your time, no rush." D stepped forward to comfort it.

Cyclizar nodded and started practicing earnestly again.

D watched for a while longer, then left the time to Koraidon and Cyclizar, and returned to the house to continue his work.

On the grass, Koraidon continued guiding Cyclizar in learning the move.

Looking at the diligent Cyclizar, Koraidon gradually recalled its past self.

It thought of many things, maybe when it was still a Cyclizar, that Koraidon viewed it this way?

Back when it was about to fall into the Time Machine, the other tried to stop it with all its might, but failed.

At that time, perhaps that Koraidon's heart had accepted it?

Koraidon was a bit confused, sometimes it couldn't even tell if it was Koraidon or Cyclizar.

Though it didn't know what all this was about, for it, as long as it could return to D's side, that was enough.

Looking at its past self earnestly learning, Koraidon thought perhaps its past self wasn't that foolish either?

Wait a minute!

If it and Cyclizar were the same entity, it meant it had been disdaining itself all along?

Koraidon suddenly realized this.

It couldn't help but think, maybe it should treat itself better, and together with D, make itself as smart and sensible as it is now.

————

The summer night sky was always so beautiful.

Dragonite lay as usual on the grass, watching the stars in the sky.

The gentle evening breeze blew across its body, as if D were giving it a massage.

Dragonite felt very comfortable.

Suddenly, a rustling sound of footsteps came from the side.

Dragonite turned its head and saw Cyclizar approaching.

In its mind, it recalled Cyclizar's daily training appearances with Koraidon, and remembered Cyclizar once saying it was afraid of Koraidon.

Dragonite couldn't help but ask, "Oow?" (Are you not afraid of Koraidon anymore?)

Cyclizar shook its head, answering with some confusion, "Gao..." (I don't know why, it seems to be less scary now...)

Dragonite appeared very happy.

"Oow!" (That's right! Koraidon is actually a very good Pokemon! It even taught me Dragon Tail!)

Hearing this, Cyclizar showed a silly grin.

But soon, it looked down in disappointment: "Gao..."

Because it still couldn't master Dragon Pulse.

"Oow..." Dragonite shook its head, indicating that it couldn't either.

The two Pokemon sat in silence, looking worried, on the grass.

Just then, D, who had finished washing up, came out to check on the Chicken House, and saw Dragonite and Cyclizar as he passed by.

"I was wondering why I didn't see you in the house, turns out you guys came here to look at the stars?" D walked over with a smile and sat down next to the two Pokemon: "What's up? You both seem a bit down?"

---

"Gah-oh..."

Looking at Cyclizar, D guessed, "Is it because you can't use Dragon Pulse?"

Cyclizar nodded its head, looking disappointed.

Seeing this, D comforted it, "Don't worry too much, maybe you just haven't grasped the knack of it yet. If you keep trying, one day you'll figure it out and become as strong as Koraidon."

"Gah-oh?" Cyclizar tilted its head in confusion.

Speaking of Koraidon, D remembered a legend and smiled at Cyclizar:

"Speaking of which, you have a profound connection with Koraidon! Some urban legends say that Koraidon is what you used to look like."

Cyclizar looked completely puzzled, not understanding.

D thought for a moment and explained, "It's how you were a long, long time ago."

"Besides Koraidon, there's Miraidon, which is supposedly what you'll look like a long, long time in the future. One represents the past, one the future, and you, you belong to the present."

"Gah-oh?" Cyclizar was even more perplexed.

This made it think of the evolution of those Pokemon in the ranch.

So the question arose: Can it also evolve into Koraidon and Miraidon?

Thinking of this, Cyclizar's eyes lit up instantly.

If it evolves, will it become as powerful as Koraidon?

D sensed that Cyclizar might have misunderstood something, but he didn't think much of it. He just patted Cyclizar's head and said, "It's okay if you don't understand. The era they live in is too far from us."

At this point, the nearby Dragonite couldn't help but ask, "Awu?"

What about it? Does Dragonite also have past and future forms?

Although D couldn't understand Dragonite's words, he could roughly guess its meaning through its expression and gaze.

He was about to shake his head, but suddenly remembered the giant Dragonite once seen near Bill's Lighthouse in the anime, hovering above the sea and shrouded in mist.

Rumor has it that the giant Dragonite is an Ancient Dragonite, just like the Ancient Jigglypuff, Ancient Gengar, and Ancient Alakazam sealed in the mountains behind Pallet Town.

Thinking of this, D replied, "Maybe."

At this time, other Pokemon started gathering around.

Butterfree, Pawmo, Alcremie, Koraidon...

Butterfree flew over to stay with D upon seeing him sitting on the grass, stargazing.

While Pawmo, Koraidon, and Alcremie came out because D hadn't returned indoors to sleep.

D looked at them and continued answering Dragonite's question, "Maybe a long, long time ago, Dragonite had a gigantic body like a mountain."

A mountain-sized body... How terrifying would such power be?

Dragonite widened its eyes, imagining the scene.

D sat on the grass, smiling as he enjoyed the quiet night with a group of Pokemon.

Looking at the empty grass, he suddenly felt something was missing, so he returned inside to fetch a picnic cloth, spread it out on the grass, and arranged snacks on it.

Lucky Pudding, Berry, Strawberry Milk, Berry Juice, Honey Bun, Sandwich, Cream Smoothie, Grassy Seed...

D asked Alcremie to call everyone over.

Alcremie gladly agreed.

Soon enough, Glaceon, Gogoat, Nacli, Naclstack, Miltank, Smoliv, Combee, and other Pokemon gathered on the soft grass.

Glaceon's eyes fixed on the cream smoothie on the picnic cloth, showing a strong desire to eat.

Seeing this, D handed the cream smoothie to it, then took the opportunity to rub Glaceon's head.

Glaceon, never having been touched by a human before, instantly froze.

"Eat up!" D grinned widely.

"Eevee..."

Glaceon lowered its head and silently began licking the smoothie with its small tongue.

D took out a Grassy Seed from the picnic basket.

Smelling it, the three Gogoat nudged their way over.

While petting their fur, D fed them the Grassy Seed.

"Hmm, maybe I can make some other dishes using grass," D thought.

Like grass ice cream, grass pie, grass cake...

Luckily, he already had the kitchen tools at home to make cakes, so D planned to try making Green Grass Cake tomorrow to give Gogoat a taste change.

"Meeh!"

At this moment, Gogoat seemed to sense his thoughts and suddenly showed a happy expression, licking him with its tongue that had a faint grassy smell.

D was startled by their actions and quickly withdrew his hand placed on their horns.

Soon after, he realized that Gogoat had sensed his thoughts through his hand!

This was Gogoat's special ability.

With a touch of their horns, they can feel the emotions of the other party and can read the trainer's thoughts from the subtle differences in grip pressure.

D curiously asked, "You sensed my emotions?"

The three Gogoat squinted their eyes happily, "Meeh~"

"Good job!" D praised, petting the middle Gogoat.

This Gogoat was the one that battled Koraidon earlier and was also the leader of the other two Gogoat.

After evolving from Skiddo to Gogoat, it won in horn games, which made it the leader.

Having finished the Grassy Seed, the three Gogoat lay down nearby.

D then turned to look at the Miltank, not far away, rolling around on the grass.

The Miltank was lively and energetic, especially adept at the Move Rollout.

Watching its actions, D suddenly had a whimsical idea, thinking of a traditional snack - Fried Mung Bean Dumplings.

So the question arose: Could this snack enhance Miltank's Rollout Move?

"I'll try making it later," D planned.

He ate a piece of Lucky Pudding and started playing with Nacli and Naclstack.

First stacking Nacli together, then putting them all onto Naclstack.

Tilting his head and looking at Naclstack for a while, D suddenly felt that this Pokemon's style was very different from other Pokemon.

Square and neat, unlike creatures from the Pokemon World, looking more like they came from the game Minecraft.

At this time, Butterfree beside him suddenly noticed something, "Muh-ee!"

D turned around and followed its gaze to see some scattered yellow-green glows on the gently flowing river in the nighttime.

They floated above the river, gently dancing in the tranquil night.

Those are... fireflies?

D was somewhat surprised because, logically speaking, fireflies should appear around July and August.

But soon he thought that the climate of this world was very different from his past life. With distinct seasons here, plus Porto Marinada Town's coastal location, the nearby climate was warm and humid year-round, so fireflies appearing wasn't strange.

Gradually, more fireflies gathered by the river, creating a beautiful galaxy slowly flowing in the night.

"Mama..."

Alcremie was stunned by this scene, murmuring softly.

The other Pokemon all turned their heads to look.

D couldn't help but sigh, "Yes, it's truly beautiful!"

In his past life, always bustling through city life, he never had the chance to see such sights.

Maybe, this is the charm of nature?

Beautiful and yet making one feel close.

Not only them, but the group of dazed Psyduck across the river also noticed the scene.

One Psyduck tilted its head, attempting to reach out with its duck foot to catch a firefly.

But failing to catch it, it tumbled into the river, clumsily scrambling back up.

Ignoring the river water all over it, the Psyduck dazedly gazed up at the river.

Clearly, it too was stunned by this beautiful scene.

Watching them, D couldn't help but think that perhaps in this respect, they are alike.

Whether humans or Pokemon, they are all part of nature.

---

---

---

---

# 134: Chapter 132: Is Gogoat Lazy or Not?

Early the next morning, after finishing breakfast, D suddenly noticed that the grass in the pasture had grown significantly taller.

Since there was nothing else to do at the moment, he grabbed a sickle and started cutting the grass, collecting all of the clippings to later make Grassy Seed treats for the Gogoat.

However, the pasture was so large that after several hours of work, he still hadn't finished and was feeling quite sore.

"Phew!" D stood up, taking a deep breath, and thought to himself, "I guess it's time to buy a lawnmower."

With this in mind, he walked back, removing his leather gloves and putting the sickle back in the storeroom.

Upon returning to the front, D saw that Koraidon was teaching Cyclizar some moves, with Dragonite eagerly observing on the side.

Hmm, it seems I'll have to ride another Pokémon to town.

D decided against disturbing them. After a brief thought, he headed to the goat pen to find a Gogoat, casually choosing one to ride.

"Let's go, accompany me to town!"

"Baa~"

Gogoat agreed happily.

The evolved Gogoat stood at an impressive 1.8 meters tall and could easily carry a person of average weight.

D rode the Gogoat past Koraidon and Cyclizar, telling them:

"I'm heading to town, keep practicing."

"Gah-sis!"

"Gao-oh!"

"Awoo~"

The three Pokémon blinked their eyes.

Koraidon wanted to take D to town, but since it had tasks to attend to, it could only watch them leave longingly.

This was the first time D rode a Gogoat.

Compared to Koraidon and Cyclizar, riding the Gogoat didn't feel much different.

No, to be precise, the Gogoat was even furrier, and the sensation of sitting on its back was softer.

More interestingly, Gogoat was very intelligent.

By placing his hands on its pair of long horns, Gogoat could sense D's thoughts, adjusting direction and speed according to his wishes, offering a ride experience of complete freedom.

In a sense, Gogoat was even more like a motorcycle than Cyclizar.

"No wonder it has been assisting humans since 5000 years ago," D thought.

It truly is a reliable Pokémon.

The only downside is, if only it didn't light up.

Watching the Gogoat suddenly shine with a vivid green glow while running, D was momentarily at a loss for words.

This feeling, why does it resemble those flashy mounts in pay-to-win online games?

Recharge 998, get a deluxe flashy glowing mount!

Shaking his head to dispel these strange thoughts, D halted the Gogoat at the roadside, telling it:

"Release the energy in your body first!"

"Baa~"

And so, Gogoat began its Disco in the wild.

A powerful surge of Grass Type Energy erupted from its body, instantly enveloping everything around and forming a lush green terrain.

But that was just the beginning.

In this Grassy Terrain, Gogoat unleashed other moves.

Leaf Blade, Vine Whip, Razor Leaf, Solar Beam and other Grass Type Moves were flung about aggressively.

Watching this, D initially worried if doing this every day would burden Gogoat too much, causing discomfort or unease.

But he soon noticed Gogoat's face full of enjoyment.

D: "..."

The green glow spread further, attracting many wild Pokémon to come and watch.

Gradually, as time ticked away, more wild Pokémon gathered around.

D noticed that, compared to before, Gogoat seemed to extend its Disco time more and more each day…

Once the energy was fully dispersed, Gogoat still appeared unsatisfied.

It seemed that merely using moves was no longer enough to consume their abundant energy.

Watching this, D fell deep into thought.

Undoubtedly, this was all due to consuming large amounts of Grassy Seed daily.

To resolve this, he would need to stop feeding the Gogoat Grassy Seed.

However, Grassy Seed had become their favorite treat, and stopping it might make the three Gogoats unhappy.

So, besides Disco, what other method could help release this excess energy?

The first thing that came to D's mind was battling; it's clearly more effective to vent energy through battles than merely using moves.

Moreover…

D recalled how keen the Gogoat seemed to battle with Koraidon before; clearly, it's not a Pokémon that dislikes fighting.

Thinking of this, D looked at the Gogoat, which had now come before him, and asked:

"Gogoat, if I let you battle with other Pokémon, would you be willing?"

"Baa?!"

Hearing this, Gogoat's face lit up with excitement, eyes filled with fighting spirit.

D: "..."

Indeed, at heart, it's still a lively and combative Gogoat!

Life on the farm was nothing but eating and sleeping, leading Gogoat to lazily lie on the grass, giving D a false sense of a laid-back, lazy personality.

After a bit of thought, D asked: "What if the opponent is a very strong Pokémon?"

"Baa!"

With a "swish" sound, a very exaggerated Grass Blade manifested in Gogoat's mouth.

It wasn't going to back down!

Invaders of the pasture shall face the wrath of the sheep!

"…Alright, alright, we'll discuss this later; for now, put away your Divine Power!"

Gogoat obediently withdrew its power.

D mounted it again, continuing towards Porto Marinada Town.

Meanwhile, he contemplated how to effectively help Gogoat release its surplus energy and satisfy its vigorous battling needs.

Taking them to battle other Trainers?

No, that won't work, there is still plenty of work waiting for him at the farm, he probably won't have that much time.

How about letting Gogoat spar with Dragonite and Cyclizar, learning and improving together?

This was feasible, but one Dragonite and Cyclizar might not suffice for three Gogoats.

As Porto Marinada Town drew nearer, D's mind rapidly flashed through past events, seeking ways to help Gogoat release its energy.

Finally, the scene froze, lingering on a few youths clad in Orange Academy uniforms.

Got it!

If he claimed the farm can assist Trainers in training, could he then entice some young Trainers to practice at the farm?

By sending Gogoat to battle them, the Trainers would gain experience, Gogoat would expend its excess energy, and they might even get some exercise and shed some weight.

Plus, with the magical cooking on the farm to restore vitality, there'd be no worry of any Pokémon getting injured.

It's truly a win-win situation!

---

---

---

---

# 135: Chapter 133: Grass Trial

Just like playing a game, the player challenges the dungeon and gains tons of experience from it.

If there are Pokemon with high comprehension, they might be able to learn some move techniques from Gogoat.

D had already thought of a name for this "dungeon."

It's the Grass Trial!

If other Pokemon are interested, maybe they can participate too.

Like Dragonite, who is diligently training to become stronger.

It can test the effect of Rain Dance + Hurricane + Thunder on challengers.

D felt a slight stir in his heart, but the specifics needed to be planned in detail later.

As for now, he needed to buy a lawnmower first.

Upon entering Porto Marinada Town, Gogoat slowed its pace.

Its hooves made a clattering sound on the red brick path of Porto Marinada Town.

D's attention was drawn to it.

He looked at the beautiful red brick path below, and thoughts of the simple wooden path at his ranch came to mind.

It was laid using excess wood from the ranch, full of rustic charm, though it also had a very plain and tidy appearance. However, its lifespan was indeed very short.

After a few heavy rains, the wood on the ground becomes soft and rotten, and D even thought they wouldn't last through the summer.

"Hmm, it's time to formally plan the roads in the ranch." D thought to himself.

Otherwise, on rainy days, the roads would be full of muddy puddles, and just going out would make his shoes muddy.

At such times, having a red brick path could avoid such problems.

It was convenient; he was heading to Keri's shop, and could inquire along the way.

The town was still bustling as usual within days of his last visit.

As Porto Marinada Town is by the sea, many tourists from inland areas would especially come here to see the ocean.

Speaking of the seaside, D remembered Allen once mentioned that every summer, the town would hold a grand fireworks festival there.

At that time, people would gather by the sea, setting up a lively market that would sell sushi, takoyaki, and oden-like snacks.

But now, it's only the beginning of the month, so there's still quite some time until that day.

Gathering his thoughts, D rode Gogoat towards Keri's shop.

When he arrived, Keri was sitting on a wooden stump beside the house, surrounded by a group of Grass Type Pokemon.

There were summer form Deerling, fragrant Bounsweet, a cute Sprigatito, and a Mankey.

Keri was feeding Pokemon snacks to them, and upon hearing a sound, she turned her head, and upon seeing D, her face lit up with a smile:

"Changed mounts this time?"

D jumped down from Gogoat and said, "The Cyclizar are practicing moves at the ranch, so I let Gogoat bring me here."

He glanced at the group of Pokemon and asked, "Are they Pokemon from the forest?"

D remembered Keri once mentioned some injured wild Pokemon would come to her for help.

Keri nodded gently, "Yes! It's rare for these little guys to gather today. I had nothing much to do, so I came over to play with them for a while. Somehow, being with them always brings a rare sense of peace to my heart."

"Maybe that's the charm of Pokemon?" D said with a smile, looking at the Sprigatito snuggling at Keri's feet: "Sprigatito is quite a rare Pokemon!"

Sprigatito, the Grass-type Starter Pokemon of the Paldea Region, is a very popular Pokemon.

Keri looked down at Sprigatito tenderly and said, "Whether common or rare, Pokemon are no different to me. Maybe it's because I'm not a Trainer!"

D paused, then agreed, "True."

Whether it's the commonly seen Mankey by the roadside or the rare Sprigatito deep in the woods, aside from their appearance, they all have a pure heart.

Keri stood up with a smile and asked, "What are you here to buy today?"

D: "A lawnmower. The wild grass at the ranch is growing too fast, and Gogoat and Miltank don't eat much of it, so I plan to cut it all down."

Upon hearing this, Keri couldn't help but say, "Pokemon living at your ranch are really fortunate."

D raised an eyebrow: "Hmm?"

Keri explained, "Few Ranchers would go through the trouble of preparing individual meals for them every day like you do."

After all, whether Gogoat or Miltank, their food in the wild is usually wild grass and flowers. Occasionally, if they're lucky, they might come across Berry trees and pick a few Berries to eat.

Whereas those raised on the ranch mostly eat fodder and feed bought from the store.

By comparison, D treats his Pokemon very well indeed.

D understood what Keri meant and laughed, "That's why my gas bill each month is several times higher than other ranches!"

Keri chuckled and then led the way ahead: "Come with me!"

The two went inside, and Keri pushed out several lawnmowers from the warehouse, introducing them one by one:

"Look, these are all of them, each with different prices and performances. The cheapest here is 5000 League Coins. It's an older model that makes a lot of noise during use. The others are similar, but different brands have different prices."

D nodded thoughtfully, looking through them all, and finally picked a push-type lawnmower, spending 7000 League Coins.

Then, D asked, "By the way, do you have red bricks here? I want to lay a red brick path at the ranch."

"Yes, we do."

Keri nodded, recalling the size of D Ranch in her mind, and then thoughtfully said:

"However, I suggest you first properly plan the areas of the ranch. Otherwise, if you lay the red brick path now, it might obstruct future construction of other facilities, and you'll have to redo it, which would be quite troublesome."

"True." D nodded, thinking about it and deciding to go back to the ranch to plan it out first: "I'll go back and check, and come back when I'm ready."

"Of course, no problem, always welcome," Keri nodded with a smile.

After leaving the town, D directly returned to the ranch.

Gogoat went back to the pen, and he took paper and pen, summoned Dragonite, and flew into the air, looking down on the entire ranch from above, sketching out the rough terrain and the locations of various buildings.

However, D didn't immediately start planning the ranch, as he still had other tasks to attend to.

He handed the paper and pen to Cyclizar to place on the desk, then pushed the lawnmower to start trimming the grass in the ranch.

The ranch is huge; even though many facilities have been added, there's still a large piece of land lying idle.

According to the original owner's memory, it used to be a pasture.

The original owner's grandfather once raised dozens of cattle and sheep Pokemon there.

As D pushed the lawnmower, he calculated the size of the ranch in his mind.

Currently, about a quarter of the whole ranch is utilized.

He usually didn't feel it, but now that he's mowing the lawn, goodness! This place is enormous!

D thought of the vast Cherry Garden of the Elif family, wondering how their family of four managed to plant so many cherry trees.

It's overwhelming!

---

---

---

---

# 136: Chapter 134: Green Grass Cake

By the afternoon, this grass-cutting operation finally came to an official conclusion.

Looking at the pile of grass in the pasture, stacked like a small mountain, D said to the Gogoat:

"Now you guys will have a feast!"

The three Gogoat circled around the pile, sniffing at it occasionally but not taking a bite.

D didn't mind. He took the towel handed over by Koraidon and wiped the sweat off his face, then carried some of the grass back to his room, soaking it in a basin of water.

For this grass, he planned to use part of it to make Grassy Seed and develop new Grass Type dishes, while the rest would be dried and stored as hay.

That way, when the ground is covered in snow in the future and no grass grows, there will be snacks for the Gogoat and Miltank.

Just as humans eat dried salted fish and cured meats.

D opened the fridge, took out a bottle of juice, and gulped it down to quench his thirst.

Seeing that it was still early, he decided to try developing new green dishes.

He boiled water in a pot, and once it was boiling, he added grass, waiting a few minutes to complete a simple boiled grass dish.

Looking down, D saw the information on the boiled grass appear in his mind.

[Boiled Grass (F-): A very poor dish. The original flavor of the grass was lost after being boiled in large quantities of water, and overcooking ruined its texture. The terrible taste makes it hard to swallow.

Cooking Effect: Easily lowers the favorability of Grass Type Pokemon.

Evaluation: The taste is like chewing wax; maybe only a Grass Type Pokemon starving for a week would try it.]

D: "..."

Darn, there really is a dish with a debuff; I thought there wasn't, D couldn't help but mutter internally.

But this was just a small experiment, and such an outcome was within his expectations.

After dumping the boiled grass into the trash, D looked at the remaining grass and pondered, "If boiling doesn't work, how about juicing?"

Thinking of this, he opened the juicer, placed the cleaned grass inside, and added an appropriate amount of water.

As the juicer whirred, the clear water gradually turned green, and soon, several cups of grass juice were ready.

D poured them into bowls.

[Grass Juice (C): A drink made from the ripe summer grass, appreciated by many Grass Type Pokemon for its rich flavor and texture.

Cooking Effect: Level 1 restorative potion, slightly recovering the Grass Type energy in a Pokemon's body, restoring the PP value of Grass Type moves.

Evaluation: Maybe juicing fresh spring grass would achieve better results.]

Hmm...

D stared at the grass juice before him.

A restorative potion, likely similar to a small PP restorative item in a game.

The effect is decent, but the downside is it only restores PP values for Grass Type moves and isn't as useful for Pokemon of other types.

Then, D began experimenting with frying grass, spicy grass, Steamed Green Grass, and any cooking method he could think of.

However, the results were consistently unsatisfactory.

[Fried Grass (F): Effect is to reduce Grass Type Pokemon's favorability.]

[Steamed Green Grass (E): Effect is to slightly restore Grass Type Pokemon's stamina.]

[Spicy Grass (D-): Effect is to occasionally make a Grass Type Pokemon breathe fire, increasing the favorability of certain Pokemon.]

D couldn't help feeling a bit speechless.

No doubt, the certain Pokemon referred to here must be the Fire and Grass Dual Type Capsakid and its evolved form, Scovillain.

In his mind, he pictured that odd-looking Pokemon with a humanoid body but two large chili-like heads.

One head green, one red; when the green head is stimulated by spicy elements, it becomes fierce, converting the spiciness into Fire Energy, and spews explosive flames from the red head.

Honestly, it's a bit hard to appreciate.

D thought that Hydreigon with its three heads was more pleasing to the eye than Scovillain.

He called over Cyclizar to help deliver the edible grass dishes to the Gogoat, while the rest was thrown into the trash bin.

Next up was the highlight of this experiment — the Green Grass Cake.

This was a very special dish.

In a game he played in his previous life, if players fed the Green Grass Cake to the little characters they were raising, and then had them tend to the crops in their farm.

The crops taken care of by the little characters would produce slight mutations.

D recalled the cake-making process in his mind and began trying his hand at it.

First, he used the juicer to crush the grass, then got a suitable amount of cake flour, added vegetable oil, the crushed grass paste, and three fresh egg yolks.

After mixing them evenly, he added half a bottle of MooMoo Milk, continued mixing, and soon it became a deep green liquid batter.

He then whipped the egg whites with sugar into something resembling cream, before adding it to the prepared green batter.

Once the two mixtures were fully combined, he poured them into a cake mold and put it in the oven to bake for 55 minutes.

D hoped for the cooking effect as he sat in his chair, and after a long wait, with a 'ding,' the cake was ready.

---

D eagerly took it out and looked down, and the information about the cake appeared before him.

[Incomplete Green Grass Cake, effect unknown.]

Huh, incomplete?

D was slightly stunned, then remembered that in the game, the cake still needed a layer of cream on top.

With this thought, he went outside and called over Alcremie, borrowing a big bucket of light cream from it.

But the light cream was white and didn't have a green grass flavor.

After pondering for a moment, D took the freshly crushed grass and added it to the light cream, stirring continuously until the white cream turned the color of grass.

Next up was the final step.

D set the baked cake base and evenly spread the grass-scented green cream on it until everything was smooth.

Finally, he placed a tender green piece of grass on top, and the cake was officially complete.

From its appearance, it looked almost identical to Lazy Sheep's Green Grass Cake.

This could very well mean he succeeded on his first try!

D looked on eagerly and was thrilled to find that the cake had transformed from [Incomplete Green Grass Cake] to [Green Grass Cake].

[Green Grass Cake (A): A cake made with mature summer grass, flour, MooMoo Fresh Milk, and Alcremie's cream.

Cooking Effect: Flower Trick, when fed to Grass Type Pokemon, there's a slight chance the crops nurtured with Grass Type Energy will exhibit minor mutations.

Evaluation: Perfectly integrates the flavors of different ingredients, popular among Grass Type Pokemon, and Grass Type Pokemon who eat it might bring you a pleasant surprise out of gratitude.]

It's here! It's the Green Grass Cake, as expected!

D felt a surge of joy and pride inside: "It seems I really have a knack for making cakes!"

He carried the finished cake impatiently to the goat pen, where he found three Gogoat eating.

The Gogoat caught a whiff of a very special scent.

They looked down to see D holding a small green cake in his hands.

That enticing smell was emanating from the cake.

"Mbaaah?"

"Smo?"

Even the nearby Smoliv was drawn in.

D looked at them and introduced: "This is a Green Grass Cake made from grass and other ingredients, and should suit your taste."

He took out a fruit knife and swiftly cut the cake into several pieces, distributing them to the Pokemon in front of him.

The Gogoat licked it once, then sped up eating the cake.

They ate quickly, finishing the cake in no time and looking expectantly.

Seeing the Gogoat enjoyed it, D cheerfully said: "If you like it, I'll make it for you often in the future."

There was conveniently a large pile of grass nearby.

"Mbaaah～"

The Gogoat were delighted.

Right at that moment, a dazzling light suddenly burst from Smoliv nearby.

D and the Gogoat's attention were instantly captured.

In the light, Smoliv's appearance rapidly changed.

Its body grew larger, sprouted little arms and legs, and the olive on its head split in two, with the original green leaves wrapping its entire body.

From the outside, it looked like a small girl in a green dress with a bun hairstyle.

Moments later, when the light faded, D smiled and said: "Congratulations, Smoliv, oh no, I should call you Dolliv now."

"Dolliv～"

Dolliv let out a cheer, and in the next second, two streams of fragrant oil spurted from the olives on its head.

D and the Gogoat got splashed all over.

The Gogoat bent down and licked the delicious oil on them.

D dabbed a little oil with his hand, sniffed it under his nose, and indeed smelled an extremely fresh and savory fragrance.

This made his eyes light up; the quality of the oil produced by Dolliv was far superior to the ordinary oils sold in town!

"How aromatic!"

Dolliv, seeing that he liked it, couldn't help but cheer: "Dolliv!"

Then, two more streams of oil sprayed out.

D was a bit helpless: "Alright, alright, it's such a waste for this tasty oil to fall on the ground."

But he also knew that spraying oil was a way for Dolliv to express its emotions.

At this moment, Alcremie came bouncing from a distance, with Glaceon and the Nacli following behind it.

Seeing them, D quickly called out: "Alcremie, come check out who this is!"

"Maaa?"

Alcremie turned its head, and when it clearly saw Dolliv, it immediately widened its eyes in disbelief.

"Maaa!"

"Dolliv～"

Dolliv was gentle and soft, a bit like a shy little girl.

Alcremie circled around it, seemingly amazed at how Smoliv evolved into this form.

D got up and stood with the Gogoat, smiling at the scene.

Then, he remembered something important: "Oh right."

The Gogoat turned to look: "Mbaaah?"

D said to them: "From today forward, I have a new task for you, which is to use your Grass Type Energy to help me tend to the crops in the field, okay?"

"Mbaaah～"

Grass Type Energy? Of course, no problem! The Gogoat happily agreed.

"Then I'll leave it to you!" D smiled and patted their heads.

The crops tended by the Gogoat underwent magical mutations, and cooking made from these crops would be fed to the Pokemon on the farm, relying on their power to develop the farm, creating a perfect cycle.

D was eager to see the kinds of magical changes the crops would undergo when nurtured by them.

Unfortunately, this batch of crops had only just been planted and would take a long time to mature.

Of course, it's also possible they'll remain unchanged.

Because the probability that the Green Grass Cake causes a mutation is so low.

An extremely small chance, which suggests that maybe only one plant in the whole field might change.

But it doesn't matter; all he wanted was to enjoy life well, the rest is good if it comes, but not required.

The sun in the distance was setting, and as twilight approached, the weary birds in the back mountain began to return home.

D stretched, then looked at Glaceon and went to pick it up.

Glaceon obviously didn't expect D to suddenly make this move, and it froze for a moment, forgetting to struggle.

D looked into its eyes and smiled: "You're so cute!"

"Vee…!"

Glaceon finally reacted, hurriedly breaking free and jumping to the ground, quickly running towards its Ice House.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but laugh.

Meanwhile, a Nacli was looking up at him, jumping twice on the grass: "Nacl!"

"Sure, I'll give you a hug too!"

D bent down and picked up the Nacli. When he lifted it overhead, Nacli let out a surprised cry: "Nacl!"

The other three Nacli quickly gathered around too, and D hugged each in turn. When he finally finished hugging the last Nacli, Naclstack's expectant face appeared in front of him.

D: "..."

He went forward and tried hard to lift Naclstack, but ended up sitting breathless on the grass.

"No way, you're too heavy." D said, panting.

After evolving, Naclstack's weight had soared to over 200 pounds; it wasn't something an average person could lift.

Upon hearing this, Naclstack's face showed disappointment, but quickly recovered.

Because it came up with a great idea!

Naclstack crouched, lying on the ground, and turned its head, making a muffled sound: "Stack!"

"What?" looking at Naclstack's action, D guessed: "You want me to get on?"

"Stack!" Naclstack nodded its head.

Since D couldn't lift it, it would carry D!

"But honestly, I'm pretty heavy!" D reminded.

Naclstack had no problem with that.

Upon seeing this, D held its shoulder and sat on Naclstack. The evolved Naclstack was quite large, with a broad back, more than enough for one person.

Once he was seated, Naclstack stood back up, letting out a happy growl: "Stack～"

D laughed uncontrollably: "When you evolve into Garganacl, I'll be able to sit on your shoulder."

---

---

---

---

# 137: Chapter 135

Night fell, and the room was brightly lit.

D sat at his desk, listening to the harmonious bug buzz coming from outside, and began writing down the recipe and process for [Green Grass Cake] in his cookbook.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Alcremie, and Dragonite had all gone out to play.

After the onset of summer, if the weather was clear, one could see a large number of fireflies by the river every day.

Complemented by the bright moonlight, the beautiful starry sky, and the expansive meadows, it was like a natural pastoral scene akin to a painting.

And a group of Pokémon also became part of this painting, playing and living freely and carefree within it.

D, however, did not go out to play with them.

Because there was still something at home waiting for him to handle.

Looking at the roughly drawn map of the ranch from the day, D fell into contemplation.

"The facilities in the ranch are still incomplete. There's a Chicken House, Pokemon House, and Salt House, but some ranch facilities are yet to be built."

"A grain barn for storing grain, a watermill to grind wheat into flour, a greenhouse for year-round planting, and a stable could also be prepared in advance."

Looking at this map, D marked the facilities that had already been built and, according to his ideas, made a simple plan for those that hadn't been constructed yet.

"The watermill can be built in front of the Chicken House. There's a river there, just perfect for using the water's force to turn the mill's wheel."

"The barn... can be built next to the Pokemon House, and the stable could be beside the sheep pen."

As for the greenhouse, D plans to build it next to the farmland.

He quickly sketched the approximate locations of these facilities on the map.

This way, there would still be a large open area in the ranch left unused.

D's gaze turned to the west side of the ranch, where there was a vast meadow.

He drew a circle on it with his pen and thought, "This area can be set aside to plant fruit trees and turned into an orchard."

Just like the many cherry trees in the Elif Cherry Garden, if they can't finish all the fruits, they could share them with the wild Pokémon, like Teddiursa, living in the back mountains.

Besides that, the farming area needs to expand its planting scale again.

But first, he needs to capture one or more Pokémon who can use the "Dig" move.

With that Pokémon digging, he sowing and fertilizing, Dragonite watering, Gogoat assisting the crop growth, and then harvesting together.

This would make things much easier; otherwise, he really couldn't manage it alone.

So, the matter of paving the red brick paths isn't so urgent.

It's better to wait until all facilities are completed before planning specifically.

Next, D leaned against the back of his chair, thinking about the recent happenings in the ranch.

After Nacli evolved into Naclstack, their size and weight massively increased.

Right now, having only one is okay, but if the other four all evolve, the current Salt House would seem a bit cramped.

About this, D has two solutions in mind.

First, expand the Salt House.

Second, let them use Dig to improve the depth of the Salt House, creating a cave for Naclstack and Garganacl to live in.

However, Naclstack doesn't know how to Dig yet, so this plan has to be put off for now.

Next is the matter of Glaceon.

Cream Smoothie can help it grow stronger, but there hasn't been much effect yet because Glaceon doesn't eat Cream Smoothie very frequently, unlike Gogoat, who treats them as meals.

Apart from these, there is also his earlier conception regarding the Grass Trial.

In the first stage of the Grass Trial, one must battle a Gogoat.

The second stage, battle against two Gogoats simultaneously.

And the third stage requires facing the joint attack of three Gogoats.

Thinking of this, D turned his gaze back to the ranch map, looking at the unused vacant land under the Gogoat pen, and drew a simple battle area with his pen.

"Here, a battle arena can be specially constructed for fighting."

So the next morning, D got busy.

He used a mower to cut all the grass on that piece of land, leaving a rectangular blank space.

To test the size of the battle arena, D had Koraidon and Cyclizar stand at each end.

D observed for a moment and mused, "A bit too small."

Worried that Pokémon couldn't fully utilize their skills, he expanded the battle area by another round.

Once satisfied, he nodded in contentment, then had Koraidon use Flamethrower to dry the soil and finally grabbed a bucket of white paint from the storeroom to mark the lines and edges on the ground.

Soon, a battle arena much like those in anime was completed.

D dusted off his hands, carried the paint bucket back to the storeroom.

Next, it's about waiting for the challengers to come.

However, there was still one problem.

Simple practice might not be very appealing to some Trainers.

D fell into contemplation again.

What if prizes are set up for it?

As for the types of prizes, D hadn't thought it through yet.

After all, there are three stages in the Grass Trial. It's impossible for anyone to pass all stages in a short time. When someone manages to pass, they can deal with it then; no need to rush now.

---

---

---

---

# 138: Chapter 135

Thinking of this, D temporarily set the matter aside.

His gaze turned to the farmland.

At this moment, the farmland area was completely shrouded in a rich green glow.

Three Gogoats stood at the three corners of the farm, each using Grassy Terrain from different angles, to ensure every crop received adequate care.

Watching this scene, D was filled with anticipation: "I wonder what kind of magical crops the Gogoats will cultivate."

Will it be glowing tomatoes or icy watermelons like in the games? Or perhaps giant crops like those in Stardew Valley?

If it's the former, that's fine.

If it's the latter, then one watermelon might be enough to last them several weeks.

D stood and watched for a while, then got up and walked across the ranch to the pasture in the southwest corner of the ranch.

He planned to develop a Berry Garden here.

Although he had already planted a few in the farmland, their numbers were too few.

These Berries are not only for making delicious Poffins but can also be juiced and fed to wild Pokemon.

Like the group of Psyducks across the river.

Psyduck is also very cute, and building a good relationship with them might even lead to hiring them to help in the future.

Just like the Junimos in Stardew Valley, or the seven dwarfs in Harvest Moon, they could help harvest crops.

Thinking of this, D roughly inspected the environment of this area.

If he wanted to develop an orchard here, he must first remove the grass to prevent it from competing for nutrients with the seedlings.

Speaking of tree planting, D suddenly remembered something amusing about Aggron, a Pokemon with a Rock Type and Steel Type.

It's said that Aggron patrols the mountains continuously, and when they find the mountains becoming barren, they will tirelessly transport soil and plant seedlings, keeping their territory neat and tidy.

Such a strong and large beast-like Pokemon yet possessing a humble and unpretentious hobby like an old farmhand.

One must say, Aggron's kind of Pokemon is quite interesting, with a certain charm in its contrasting nature.

"It would be great to capture one and have it help manage my Berry Garden," D thought.

However, unfortunately, there doesn't seem to be any Aggron in the Paldea Region.

Though there are some in the neighboring Galar Region, he doesn't have time to go there now.

Just as D was pondering this, a Butterfree flew over to report:

"Fufu!"

"What's up?" D looked over and, seeing Butterfree's appearance, guessed: "Is there a guest visiting the ranch?"

"Fufu~" Butterfree nodded slightly.

Hmm? D was somewhat surprised.

Who would be visiting the ranch this early in the morning?

He lifted his foot and returned to the front with Butterfree.

Soon, D saw a timid figure in front of the ranch gate.

It was a girl around sixteen or seventeen years old, with short purple hair, squatting in front of the ranch with her ears covered and eyes closed, like a little mouse, muttering something to herself.

"Hello!" D slightly raised his eyebrows and greeted her.

But she didn't respond.

So he walked over, and only when he was close could he hear what the girl was saying.

She seemed frightened, muttering continuously, "You can't see me, you can't see me, you can't see me."

D: "..."

"Hello? Can you hear me?" D waved his hand.

Seeing no response from the girl, he asked the nearby Alcremie to tap her shoulder as a reminder.

Alcremie did so, happily stepping forward and patting the girl's shoulder with its small hand.

"Maa~"

But the next second, the girl shrieked as if startled, then fell back onto the grass.

Seeing she could hear, D asked again: "Hello? Do you need help?"

"Ah! I'm so sorry! I didn't mean to disturb you! Please forgive me!" The girl quickly stood up from the ground, apologizing repeatedly.

D was utterly baffled.

Just then, a man came running from not far away.

He was a slightly plump middle-aged man with brown hair, wearing a brown shirt.

D recognized his identity.

He was Elo, owner of the town's seed store.

Elo ran up, holding the girl and comforting her: "It's okay, it's okay now!"

Then he explained to D: "I'm very sorry, this is my daughter Bell. Because she experienced something as a child, she's been afraid to go out and has been staying indoors ever since."

"I just wanted her to try interacting with the outside world today, to come and inform the nearby ranchers that Elo Seed Store has received a new batch of Berry Seedlings, but as you can see, it failed..."

Elo sighed, looking at the girl beside him with a face full of worry.

He had been following closely out of concern.

D understood the situation, realizing what was going on.

He didn't mind much, only saying, "Being so afraid of the outside world must be due to psychological factors, right?"

He didn't know much about such matters.

Elo nodded, looking troubled: "Yes, we've invited psychologists for treatment before, but it hasn't been effective."

---

D nodded, glanced at the girl, and said nothing more, simply indicating that he would visit the shop in a while to buy some saplings.

Then, Elo left with the girl.

Alcremie watched their backs: "Maa... maa..."

D said to Alcremie, "This is what's called severe social anxiety."

Alcremie looked over, puzzled.

D explained, "It's when certain reasons make a person become very afraid of external things, afraid of talking to strangers, and only willing to stay in places they think are safe, like home, their own room."

Commonly known as being a shut-in.

"Maa... maa..." Alcremie tried hard to understand the meaning of this passage, yet couldn't comprehend it at all.

It thought of when it just patted the girl and her scared expression, and couldn't help but ask: "Maa... maa?"

D asked, "Are you asking if that condition can be cured? Is that right?"

"Maa... maa!" Alcremie nodded.

"I don't know either, because I'm not a psychologist." D shook his head, smiling as he patted Alcremie's little head, saying, "Alright, go play! I have to get to work!"

He went to the storage room, returned to the plot with a hoe and a sickle, and used the sickle to cut down all the weeds, then used the hoe to till the soil, making it soft and loose.

Only after digging twenty holes did D stop.

He leaned on the hoe, rested on the side, and looked at the plot, with visions of the not too distant future appearing in his mind.

By then, this place would have become a fruitful Berry Garden, with branches heavy with plump, ripe berries that would burst with sweet and delicious juice with just a bite.

Some cute little wild Pokemon like Greedent and Skwovet would be attracted to it.

He stood under the trees, feeding these Pokemon with berries, then gathered them in his arms to cuddle.

How wonderful!

A smile appeared on D's face, and he turned back, placing the farm tools back in the storage room. After washing his hands, he heard a vibrant voice.

"Mr. D!"

D turned around, seeing a familiar figure running from outside the ranch.

It was a young boy, with curly golden hair and fair skin, looking like he came from the city.

Seeing him, D appeared surprised.

The boy said happily, "Mr. D, do you remember me?"

"Of course I remember, Carter, right?" D asked.

Carter, who a few months ago passed by the ranch and mistook a Caterpie for a wild Pokemon, sent out Delibird to battle it, but unexpectedly was defeated by Caterpie's surprise Electroweb.

Seeing D still remembered him, Carter nodded happily, "Yes!"
