"How do you have time to come back? Aren't you busy with school?" D asked.

"The academy's courses are really easy, most of the time students are out on treasure hunts in the Paldea Region. I came today to challenge Caterpie!"

Carter's eyes were full of determination.

"Delibird and I have been through three months of grueling training, and we're not the same as before. This time we'll definitely win!" he said.

D: "..."

He looked at the Butterfree not far away and said, "You could, but Caterpie has now evolved into Butterfree, and I don't know if it wants to battle you. You know, Pokemon's personalities can change after evolution."

"Ah?" Carter was dumbfounded: "Then what should I do?"

Heh heh, took the bait.

D showed a "sincere" smile and said to Carter, "But there are other Pokemon on the ranch willing to battle, oh right, you came at the right time."

"My ranch is hosting an event called the [Grass Trial], which helps Pokemon train through battles. Do you want to give it a try?"

---

---

---

---

# 139: Chapter 136: Pain and Pleasure (Two Chapters Combined)

"Grass Trial?" Carter was indeed interested.

D nodded and introduced, "It's a trial I just set up. The content mainly involves battling against Grass Type to help challengers train and increase their Pokemon's battle experience against Grass Type Pokemon."

"If you clear all the trials, you can even get the mysterious reward I've prepared!"

Increased experience against Grass Type Pokemon... mysterious reward... Carter's eyes immediately widened, and he said excitedly, "I want to participate!"

As he spoke, he took out a Poke Ball, and with a flash of white light, a Delibird appeared in front of D.

"Delibird, there's a new battle! Are you ready?" Carter's eyes were filled with enthusiasm.

"Deli!" Delibird flapped its wings, responding with a determined gaze.

"Great, full of spirit," D praised. "But before we start, I need to introduce the rules of the trial to you."

He led Carter to the simple battle area and waved to the Gogoat, and three Gogoat came running over.

Seeing this, D continued, "The trial is divided into three stages. The first stage is a 1v1 battle, where you send out one Pokemon to battle against one Gogoat."

"The second stage is 2v2, where you can send out two Pokemon but need to battle against two Gogoat simultaneously."

"And the third stage..."

"3v3?" Carter chimed in, "I send out three Pokemon to do a team battle against these three Gogoat?"

D nodded, "That's right."

He started speaking nonsense seriously, "Team battles can train a trainer's command ability and cultivate the coordination between different Pokemon, achieving the effect of training."

Hearing this, Carter clenched his fist, "I understand! Can we start now?"

Delibird, being an Ice Type, naturally has an advantage over the Grass Type Gogoat. Plus, over the past few months, he and Delibird have undergone devilish special training, completely different from before, and have already collected three Gym Badges.

For them, isn't the Grass Trial going to be easy?

D nodded slightly, "Of course."

He stepped back and signaled to the Gogoat with his eyes.

The Gogoat leader understood his meaning and eagerly rushed to the battle area.

D stepped forward and whispered, "Take it slow, don't knock out your opponent immediately."

After all, the main purpose of the Grass Trial is to help the Gogoat release excess energy in their bodies. If they defeat the opponent immediately, it won't achieve the desired effect.

"Meh?" Gogoat was momentarily puzzled, but quickly understood D's meaning and nodded in agreement, "Meh!"

Seeing this, D stepped back with satisfaction and instructed Koraidon to fetch a few cups of Berry Milk from the fridge.

These Berry Milks were handmade by him and have very effective stamina recovery effects.

Watching his actions, Carter was a bit puzzled, "Huh? Mr. D, aren't you going to command the Gogoat in the battle?"

D shook his head, "The Gogoat know what to do."

Then he asked, "Are you ready?"

Carter and Delibird nodded at the same time.

D imitated the referee's demeanor from anime and announced, "Battle start!"

"Delibird, use Icy Wind!" Carter chose the first move.

Delibird flapped its wings, and Ice Type energy turned into icy cold air sweeping towards Gogoat.

Gogoat's eyes were filled with excitement. It lightly tapped its hind hooves, and a strong green light erupted around it.

Almost instantly, the entire grassland was enveloped, with countless illusory small grasses swaying gently as they manifested from the light.

Gogoat took the hit directly, but in the next second, a cluster of green healing light gathered from the ground and poured into its body.

"No effect?" Carter was taken aback, and then he saw Gogoat charging towards Delibird across the grass.

The pair of horns on its head kept growing larger and longer, and if they hit, the consequences would be unimaginable!

Carter quickly instructed Delibird to fly high into the air, but immediately, countless thick and long vines extended from Gogoat's body, tightly following Delibird into the sky, engaging in a tense and thrilling chase.

"Dodge! Dodge!" Carter's rhythm was completely disrupted by Gogoat, and he could only command in a fluster.

It's just unfortunate that the evasion divine technique didn't work this time.

In the chase, Gogoat's Vine Whip intertwined, transforming into an even bigger, thicker vine, and it surpassed Delibird's height in one swift move.

Then, like a colossal fallen pillar, it crashed down furiously, slamming Delibird to the ground and stirring up a cloud of dust.

Moments later, the dust settled, and Delibird laid in a big pit it had created, its eyes spinning in circles, clearly having lost its ability to battle.

Carter stared at the scene in astonishment.

What kind of move was that? So terrifying!

"Unfortunately, you failed the challenge," D stepped forward and fed the Berry Milk to Delibird.

Soon, Delibird opened its eyes, and as it drank the last drop of milk, its body restored to optimal condition.

Carter rushed over, initially wanting to ask if the move Gogoat just used was the Vine Whip he knew, but he was immediately drawn to the Berry Milk.

---

"What was that? The healing effect is just too exaggerated!"

D: "This is the power of culinary arts."

Carter: "??"

Wait! Does ordinary food have such effects?

Seeing Carter's puzzled expression, D chuckled and half-jokingly explained:

"It's not ordinary food. It's an ability I was born with that allows me to infuse my intentions and emotions into the food during the cooking process, thus creating dishes that help Pokemon grow."

In the future, the Pokemon on the farm will surely attract attention, so it's better to be prepared in advance. After all, this world already has some mysterious abilities.

Could there really be such a power? Carter was stunned for a moment and then thought of something: "Is it similar to Psychic, Aura, and the Power of Viridian?"

The academy library has books documenting these kinds of abilities.

Unlike Pokemon, these powers are bestowed upon fortunate humans, granting them these miraculous abilities.

Psychic power has effects similar to those of Psychic Type Pokemon.

Aura is said to enable sensing all things. In ancient times, those with such power were called Aura Guardians.

The Power of Viridian is the most special; it only appears in humans living near the Viridian Forest.

It's said they are blessed by nature, and humans with the Power of Viridian can use this power to heal Pokemon and connect with their intentions.

"That's right." D nodded.

"I see..." Having understood this, Carter was no longer confused and turned his attention to Gogoat: "Was that move just now Vine Whip?"

"Yes, when you train Vine Whip to its limits, situations like that can happen."

Saying this, D glanced at Gogoat, who was eager for more, and asked, "Do you want to continue the challenge?"

Upon hearing this, Carter looked at the seemingly ordinary Gogoat.

Images of Gogoat's impressive moves just flashed in his mind.

Grassy Terrain, Horn Leech, Vine Whip...

Gogoat defeated Delibird with just one Vine Whip, going against the type disadvantage.

Clearly, he and Delibird were no match for Gogoat as they were now.

Instinctively, Carter felt like retreating, as Gogoat's displayed strength far surpassed the Gym Leaders he had faced before.

But just then, seeing the formidable Gogoat, Delibird's fighting spirit was ignited, and it bravely faced Gogoat.

"Deli!"

Carter was momentarily taken aback, then understood Delibird's intention.

Alright! Let's battle again!

D was a bit surprised to see this scene.

He hadn't expected that this Delibird would be such a determined Pokemon.

However, he said nothing more, just, "You're welcome anytime."

After a pause, he reminded, "Don't approach this trial with a normal mindset; winning isn't the goal. The goal is to gain more experience from the battle and learn how to face strong opponents."

"Imagine yourself as the Champion of the Paldea Region, and think about how the Champion would act."

Hearing these words, Carter felt enlightened, and he gratefully thanked D: "Thank you so much for your teaching, Mr. D, I understand now!"

D let out a sigh of relief. Tricking wild Trainers to help Gogoat blow off steam was like harvesting wheat; before achieving the goal, the wheat shouldn't be neglected.

He announced, "Then let's start!"

This time, Carter changed his battle style.

He used a tactical retreat, dodging Gogoat's Vine Whip pursuits and looking for opportunities to attack.

Gradually, he and Delibird put all their energy into the battle.

Just when Carter thought he and Delibird were getting the hang of it, Gogoat unexpectedly changed its style.

It bent down slightly, its eyes full of determination locking onto the airborne Delibird, and the Grass Type Energy in its mouth surged wildly, forming a gigantic grassy blade.

The next second, Gogoat charged forward with incredible agility.

The huge blade slashed through, and Delibird's body was instantly knocked flying, crashing to the ground, unable to continue fighting.

Instant knockout! Another instant knockout!

Carter's face showed shock once again.

After D fed Delibird some Berry Milk, Carter hurriedly asked, "What... what... what move was that?! Can Gogoat use such a move?"

"Leaf Blade." D concisely replied, "Just used in a different way, but Gogoat not only used Leaf Blade but also the Flying Type Aerial Ace."

Aerial Ace + Leaf Blade, closing in with Aerial Ace and attacking with Leaf Blade, instantly knocking Delibird to the ground.

Carter: "..."

My goodness! What Pokemon uses Leaf Blade like that? Even the Grass Gym Leader's Grass Type Pokemon don't use Leaf Blade this way?!

This just defies logic!

No, to be precise, this Gogoat's strength is just terrifying!

Gym Leader? Elite? Or Champion?

Being instantly knocked out twice in a row, Carter felt a bit pained, but on second thought, having an opponent with Elite Level strength as a sparring partner, isn't that just amazing?

Seeing the expression on this kid's face, D chuckled and asked, "Are you going to give up?"

Carter took a deep breath, feeling both pain and joy, and resolutely shook his head: "No! We must pass this trial!"

---

Battle! Battle again! Battle the freaking battle!

"Very good." D showed an appreciative look.

The first stage of the Grass Trial, the third challenge, officially begins!

Time passed by the minute and second, the sun rose high and then slowly set.

D stood by watching the battle, and his expression changed from joy to shock.

Did he turn the kid into a masochist?

The sun set in the west, and a touch of sunset appeared on the horizon.

The energy within the three Gogoat was completely exhausted, although they were a bit tired, they felt genuinely happy.

Because using battle to exhaust energy is much more refreshing than the original way!

D patted their heads and then had Cyclizar bring over a basket of Grassy Seed and pour it into the feeder.

The three Gogoat leisurely returned to the pen and started eating dinner.

Carter and Delibird sat on the ground, panting heavily.

Although the magical cuisine helped Delibird regain physical strength, the mental energy consumed for the battle was irreplaceable.

Delibird was extremely exhausted, it lay on the ground and let out a weak "Bibi," then started snoring loudly.

Seeing this, Carter returned it to the Poke Ball and softly said, "You've worked hard, now get a good sleep!"

Looking at this boy, D felt genuine admiration.

"To be honest, I really admire you."

After a whole day of battling, Carter felt physically tired, but mentally he was refreshed.

He said, "No, Mr. D, it's us who should be thanking you."

D: "..." Crazy, truly crazy.

Carter looked at the Poke Ball in his hand and said, "Delibird and I learned a lot from today's battle... Although we kept getting knocked out in one move, I can feel that both of us are constantly improving."

D was slightly taken aback, seeing Carter like this, couldn't help but smile and said:

"I'm glad I could help you, and I hope you grow from this trial. Delibird is quite a potential Pokemon."

"Hmm?" Carter looked over spiritedly, "Mr. D, have you seen trainers who use Delibird?"

"I've heard about it." D thought of Pryce from the special edition: "That trainer's Delibird is very powerful, the Ice Type Energy on it can even freeze time and defeat legends."

"Freeze time, defeat legends..." Carter murmured these eight words in shock, "What kind of power must that be?"

"Who knows?" D shrugged.

Carter suddenly thought of something and asked, "Mr. D, you must be a very skilled trainer, right?"

D was slightly stunned: "Why do you say that?"

Carter: "Because you bred those three Gogoat! And you even made Caterpie learn Electroweb!"

D smiled and shook his head: "Not a trainer, at best half a breeder! But primarily a rancher! Do you remember the special power I told you I have?"

Incorporating love and emotion into the cuisine to create magical dishes that bring happiness to Pokemon.

Carter thought of this too, his eyes brightened and he nodded: "I remember, the Gogoat must feel your intent and emotion when you make the cuisine, that's why they are so powerful!"

"Our academy teachers say that emotions and intentions in the world are also sources of power, Pokemon grow, evolve, and learn moves this way! Love is infinite, and the power of love is limitless!"

"Our teachers also say that those who can achieve this are the greatest Pokemon Breeders in the world!"

D: "..."

Though, isn't that a bit exaggerated?

As night fell, Carter stood up to say goodbye to D: "Mr. D, I am leaving now, but we will be back tomorrow!"

D was worried no one would come tomorrow, and hearing this made him beam with delight:

"Always welcome."

---

---

---

---

# 140: Chapter 137: Berry Seedlings

In the evening, D sat at his desk, reflecting on the events of the day while pondering the rules of the Grass Trial.

So far, it seems that the Grass Trial not only helps Gogoat release excess energy but also aids trainers like Carter in their training.

If more trainers are drawn to this in the future, it means he'll need to prepare more energy-replenishing meals for them.

"Should I charge a small fee?" D murmured to himself.

He felt this was feasible, considering it as compensation for the healing services provided by the ranch.

Additionally, the effects of the Grass Trial are quite good; perhaps more trainers will be attracted to it in the future.

To prevent random and unqualified participants, D thought it might be necessary to restrict challengers' eligibility.

For example, only those who have collected a certain number of Gym Badges can participate.

But this all depends on how things develop later.

As for the rewards, he's already decided.

He was reluctant to give away items like the Water Stone and Big Root that Teddiursa found, so he decided to use cooked meals as substitutes.

Such as Grass Knots for Grass Type Pokemon, and creamy ice for Ice Type Pokemon—it all depends on the challenger's luck whether they can comprehend any moves.

Anyway, he can make these anytime, so giving them away doesn't bother him.

The next day, Carter indeed showed up.

D was overseeing Gogoat taking care of the crops, and Carter, seeing this scene, felt curious and asked, "What's going on?"

D explained, "The Grass Type Energy of Gogoat can greatly assist in the growth of crops."

Carter understood.

Seeing that it was about time, D called a halt, then turned to look at Carter's Delibird, asking, "How does it feel?"

"It's great!" Carter exclaimed excitedly.

D nodded and didn't say much else, leaving the three Gogoat to play with Delibird.

"I have to go attend to the ranch work, Gogoat knows what to do. You just battle with them, and Cyclizar will substitute for me in watching your battles," he said.

"Alright!" nodded Carter repeatedly.

Leaving the work to his deputy Cyclizar, D took a final glance back at the busy ranch.

The Chicken Coop Manager Pawmo, Dragonite watering the fields, Miltank grazing, Combee collecting honey, and the playful Alcremie, Dolliv, Glaceon, and Nacli.

Everything at the ranch was running smoothly and in order.

D withdrew his gaze and said to Koraidon beside him, "Let's go!"

"Aga-hiss!"

Today he planned to go to the seed store in Porto Marinada Town to buy a batch of berry seedlings.

Riding on Koraidon, D sped all the way to Porto Marinada Town.

When he pushed open the seed store door, he saw the store owner Elo and his wife Mrs. Abby cheering for their daughter, Bell.

And standing in front of them was a fuzzy-tailed Skwovet.

"Have courage, Bell! You can do it!" Elo held his breath.

"Mom believes in you! Try touching it!" said Abby.

The purple-haired girl Bell clenched her fists and looked nervously at the Skwovet in front of her, seemingly wanting to pluck up the courage to touch it.

But when her hand reached a few centimeters from the Skwovet, she slumped down like a deflated balloon.

"I can't do it! I still can't do it!" Bell, frustrated, held her head and squatted down on the spot.

At that moment, D and Koraidon, who walked in, caught their attention.

The family of three turned their heads, and Bell saw Koraidon, eyes widening, emitting a shrill scream, and fainted on the spot.

Koraidon: "..."

D: "..."

No wonder she was squatting outside the ranch yesterday, muttering 'You can't see me, you can't see me.'

Elo and his wife hurried to support their daughter.

Mrs. Abby helped her into a room.

Elo was full of helplessness: "I am very sorry."

D shook his head, looking at the room: "Is she alright?"

"She's fine, just fainted." Elo seemed already accustomed to this: "Bell has been very afraid of Pokemon since she accidentally wandered into a forest at four years old and was attacked by a rampaging Scyther. Since then, she's been terrified of Pokemon and doesn't dare leave the house."

As he spoke, Elo patted the little head of the Skwovet.

The Skwovet squeaked and ran away.

"Since Bell is very afraid of large and intimidating Pokemon, we thought maybe smaller Pokemon would be better received by her."

Elo helplessly shrugged: "But as you see, despite her efforts to overcome the psychological barrier for our sake, she just can't make the decision to conquer the fear within."

"Aga-hiss...," Koraidon drooped his head down.

Seeing this, Elo comforted, "Hey big guy, don't be sad. Bell isn't afraid of you specifically; she's scared of all large Pokemon."

D: "..."

This reminded him of Lillie from the anime, but not quite the same.

At least Lillie wasn't socially anxious, and wasn't afraid to step outside, nor would she faint at the sight of Pokemon.

"Alright, let's not dwell on this." Elo shook his head, asking, "You're here to buy berry seedlings this time, right?"

D nodded.

"Come with me!"

With Elo's guidance, he arrived at an area full of berry seedlings.

Elo introduced, "These are all newly arrived varieties of berry seedlings. Besides the common Oran Berry and Pecha Berry, we have Razz Berry, Mago Berry, Nanab Berry, Durin Berry, Nomel Berry, and Starf Berry. Each berry has a different flavor, which ones would you like to buy?"

D pondered for a moment, counted the types of seedlings, and seeing there were only twelve, he said, "I'll take one of each."

He picked and matched until he barely managed to get twenty seedlings.

"Alright, do you need me to help deliver them to your ranch?" After paying, Elo packed the berry seedlings and asked.

D shook his head, looking at Koraidon's back: "I'll take them back myself."

He asked Elo to tie the berry seedlings with rope, hanging them on both sides of Koraidon's body, then bid farewell to Elo, riding back to the ranch on Koraidon.

Carter and his Delibird were still battling with the Gogoat.

D took a glance, then with Koraidon, carried the berry seedlings to the orchard area.

He called over Pawmo to bring the shovel and Psyduck Spray Bottle.

Pawmo was quick, cloaked in a little red cape, darting off like lightning, soon returning with the shovel and watering can.

D transplanted each berry seedling into the soil, spacing each one two meters apart, arranged in four rows of five.

After planting, D used the Psyduck Spray Bottle to water them, making the soil moist and soft, ideal for the growth of the berry seedlings.

With the sun high in the sky, D looked at the vast expanse of berry seedlings in front of him, feeling a heart full of joy and anticipation.

"Alright!" he said to Pawmo, floating nearby, and Koraidon, "By autumn, these seedlings will turn into an orchard, bearing lots of delicious berries."

"Pawmot..." Pawmo's eyes sparkled, filled with the same anticipation.

Koraidon nodded his large head happily: "Aga-hiss!" It could affirm D was telling the truth!

---

---

---

---

# 141: Chapter 138: Courage Egg Fried Rice, Muster Your Courage!

After planting the Berry seedlings, D went to help Miltank brush its fur and took the opportunity to clean up the Chicken House, finding seven or eight fresh eggs inside.

Once everything was done, he began preparing lunch.

Just right, with fresh eggs in hand, D pondered whether to make fried rice with egg for lunch today?

Speaking of which, since coming to this world, it seems like he hasn't made egg fried rice once.

Since that's the case, let's try it today at noon and see what kind of magical effect a simple egg fried rice might have.

Nearing noon, D originally wanted Carter to stay for a meal, but Carter declined.

"Thank you very much for your hospitality, Mr. D!" Carter said. "But I have to leave now; this extra-curricular activity only lasts half a month, and I need to go back to prepare for the mid-term exams! The class bus later should get me back to school by nighttime."

"Alright then."

D stood at the front of the farm and bid farewell to the boy, watching him leave.

But soon, he remembered something.

Oops! Forgot to have Carter help promote back at school! It would attract more victims!

D felt regretful inside, but there's a certain fate to such things.

"Well, let's talk about it later!"

After all, Trainers pass by the farm from time to time.

Thinking this, D turned back to his room to continue preparing lunch.

The method of making egg fried rice is very simple.

D cracked the prepared eggs, separating the whites from the yolks, then used his hands to scatter the leftover rice from last night, continuously mixing with the yolks to ensure that each grain of rice was coated in golden egg liquid.

Next, he heated oil in the pan; the oil was produced by Dolliv.

Once the oil was heated, he cracked two eggs, scrambled and cooked them, then added diced ham and carrot, and finally poured in the rice, seasoning with the right amount of Purifying Salt, continuing to stir-fry.

Soon, the rice in the pan turned golden; each grain was coated fully with egg liquid, looking golden and transparent with distinct colors, and emitting a fragrant aroma.

Koraidon and other Pokemon were attracted over, eagerly looking at the egg fried rice in the pan.

Seeing them like this, D smiled and said, "Just wait for a bit, it will be ready soon!"

Saying this, seeing it was almost done, he turned off the heat, plated the egg fried rice, and let several Pokemon carry it to the table, while he stayed back to fry some steaks for Cyclizar, Dragonite, and Koraidon.

Just eating egg fried rice, Alcremie and Pawmo could be full, but not the big fellows, so some additional preparation was needed.

D took out purchased steaks from the fridge, putting them in the pan to fry, while checking the info about the egg fried rice.

[Courage Egg Fried Rice (B+): Egg fried rice made from normal eggs, normal rice, high-grade olive oil, and Purifying Salt.

Cooking Effect: Gather your courage! Humans and Pokemon will find the courage and determination to complete tasks they want to do but dare not undertake.

Evaluation: Extremely delicious egg fried rice! Pawmo's favorite!]

Huh?

Courage Egg Fried Rice?

D looked unexpectedly at the egg fried rice in front of him.

How does this courage thing work?

Just then, D suddenly heard a loud noise behind him.

He abruptly turned his head, seeing Koraidon running onto his bed, collapsing the bed he had just recently purchased.

Koraidon happily lay on the quilt, satisfied, closing its eyes for sleep.

Witnessing this, D felt a sudden wave of chest tightness and shortness of breath, shouting, "Koraidon!"

Koraidon instantly opened its eyes, waking up.

Upon realizing the surroundings, it immediately became flustered, hastily shaking its head in denial: "Ah gah si!"

D wanted to say more, but suddenly remembered something.

Koraidon wasn't the only one who ate the egg fried rice!

He quickly turned his gaze to the dining table nearby.

The egg fried rice in front of Cyclizar, Alcremie, Pawmo, and Dragonite was also half gone.

D's gaze moved upward, and upon seeing the expressions on these Pokemon's faces, he felt a pang in his heart.

Sure enough, the next second, he saw Alcremie sprinting to the kitchen area, starting to produce cream in various places, seemingly wanting to turn it into its own Cream Workshop.

Cyclizar rushed to him, affectionately licking his face with its big tongue, happily embracing him.

Pawmo and Dragonite were full of determination, and they dashed out of the house.

Seeing this, D seized Alcremie with one arm, Cyclizar with another, followed by Koraidon, quickly going outside to check the situation.

After leaving the room, Dragonite charged straight to the area where Gogoat and Dolliv lived, shouting at Gogoat: "Awwoo!"

That appearance seemed to challenge Gogoat.

Pawmo flew into the air, releasing all its electric energy, seemingly wanting to see how strong the energy within its body was.

Soon, a thundercloud rolling continuously was summoned by Pawmi, terrifying lightning bursting in the sky, Pawmi's red cloak rustling behind it.

Amidst the pervasive lightning, blue light erupted from its eyes, connecting it to the sky, appearing like a true Thunder God.

Before the sheep pen, Gogoats gladly accepted Dragonite's challenge.

They combined into one unit, battling Dragonite.

One released Grassy Terrain, the other two wielded Grass blades, attacking Dragonite from the left and right.

Dragonite couldn't take off in time and was slammed into losing its combat ability.

Gogoats calmly returned to the pen, continuing to eat, leaving Dragonite dizzy-eyed, lying on the ground, tongue languorously hanging to one side.

It's chaos! Everything's chaos!

D shouted loudly, "Stop everything!"

The numerous Pokémon immediately came to their senses, blinking as they realized they were doing what they'd always wanted to do but hadn't dared.

D sighed helplessly, struggling to drag Dragonite by its tail to his side and taking out Berry Milk to restore its strength.

Soon, Dragonite regained consciousness, showing confusion: "Awwoo?"

A moment later, D looked at the Pokemon lined up in a row, confessing humbly, as he paced back and forth in front of them.

Seeing their tension, D addressed Cyclizar first, sighing, "Wanted to mimic Koraidon and lick me too, huh?"

Cyclizar: "Gah aw..."

Koraidon looked innocent.

Next, D approached Alcremie.

"I know you love making desserts, but don't worry, once we have a big house in the future, I'll build a Cream Workshop just for you."

Alcremie, thinking D was going to scold him, had been worried, and upon hearing this, cheered: "Mama!" Hail D!

D walked up to Dragonite, amused by Dragonite's attempt to challenge three Gogoats, asking, "How does it feel?"

Dragonite looked crestfallen: "Awwoo..."

Then it was Pawmo's turn.

Considering Pawmi's display, D pondered aloud, "Seems the energy within you is even more profound than I expected, though fully releasing it on the farm could cause panic. Next time, Koraidon and I will take you to test it out over the sea."

"Pa mo!"

Pawmo nodded steadily.

The last one, he reached Koraidon's spot.

Picturing the recent scene, D felt exasperated yet amused, "Second time already."

Koraidon's demeanor dropped miserably: "Ah gah si..."

Seeing Koraidon like this, D's vexation evaporated.

What's to be done? It's his fault; he shouldn't have impulsively made this egg fried rice!

Oh well.

D sighed helplessly, allowing the numerous Pokémon to return to their meal, while he began cleaning up the kitchen coated in cream by Alcremie, planning to head to town in the afternoon to buy a new bed.

---

---

---

---

# 142: Chapter 139: Paranoia (Double Chapter)

By the time D finished cleaning the entire room, it was already one in the afternoon.

The egg fried rice he originally prepared for himself had already gone cold.

Putting the rag back in place, D looked at the egg fried rice, thinking about the performance of the Pokémon just now, and couldn't help feeling a bit hesitant.

He sat at the table, starting to recall things he wanted to do but didn't dare try.

However, after thinking for quite a while, he couldn't come up with anything meaningful.

Should I give it a taste? Try if this dish has the same effect on humans as it does on Pokémon?

Ultimately, D decided to give it a try.

He picked up the spoon and started eating.

Hmm, it still had a bit of warmth, the rice was soft, the egg was savory, and it tasted pretty good.

Just right since D was a bit hungry, he swiftly finished off the bowl of rice, and after eating it all, he even had the mind to grab a cup of iced juice from the fridge and take a few sips.

"Huh? It doesn't seem like there's any particularly strong feeling?" D wondered to himself.

Yet in the next moment, his mind suddenly conjured up ideas he had fantasized about before but never tried.

Snuggle with Glaceon! Press his face against its soft belly and snuggle like crazy!

Because he used to fear scaring Glaceon, worrying that Glaceon would consider him a creep, he never dared to do it.

But now...

D suddenly felt an unprovoked surge of courage in his heart.

Just go for it! Hug Glaceon and snuggle with it like he would with Pawmo!

But soon, D wanted to see if ordinary people could avoid the effects of the cooking effect.

So his lifted foot was retracted, and he sat back down, after a while, that feeling gradually dissipated.

D looked at the empty egg fried rice and began to reflect.

"It seems that eating this egg fried rice just injects a surge of courage into the eater, prompting them to impulsively do things they want to do but dare not."

"This feeling isn't terribly strong; a normal person can still resist it. As long as they maintain strong willpower, they can avoid being affected by the cooking effect."

"Without courage, the cooking's influence also dissipates."

However, if this dish is consumed when someone is indecisive, it could be very useful, helping them make decisions.

For example, for those with decision-making difficulty or those who are introverted and don't know how to express themselves or refuse.

Thinking of this, D suddenly thought of the girl from Elo's seed shop family.

Elo said that she also wanted to overcome her psychological hurdles but never could muster up the determination.

So the question is, could this dish help her?

Since he was going to town to buy a bed in the afternoon anyway, D planned to give it a try.

He then got up, took ingredients from the fridge, made an extra portion of egg fried rice, packed it in a lunch box, and rode his Cyclizar to town.

This lunch box was something he bought previously, intending to use it when exploring the wilderness, but he hadn't had a chance yet, so it hadn't been used once.

Cyclizar was fast, reaching Porto Marinada Town in about ten minutes.

He stood in front of the seed shop, looking at the sign on the door, and to avoid last time's incident, D specifically put Cyclizar back in the Poke Ball before entering.

It was afternoon, so there weren't many people in the store.

Elo was dusting the shelves with a feather duster, and upon hearing footsteps, he turned and saw D.

Elo's face showed a bit of surprise, then smiled and said, "Good afternoon! Is there something else you wanted to buy?"

"No." D shook his head and gestured with the lunch box containing the Courage Egg Fried Rice, briefly explaining the magical cuisine's effect to Elo.

After listening, Elo looked surprised, "Such a cuisine exists? How did you make this kind of food?"

D looked at his palm and recited the explanation he gave to Carter once more.

"I see..." Elo muttered and nodded.

Hearing that D had such a wonderful ability, he wasn't envious or jealous. Instead, he felt happy for him.

Elo originally wanted to say perhaps because you lost a relative in this world, the divine heart couldn't bear it, so it granted you this blessing, but he thought better of it, as that would bring up sad memories.

So he changed the subject, "Does it really bring courage to people?"

D smiled and nodded, "Bringing happiness to others is the meaning of cuisine's existence."

Elo nodded, glanced upstairs, and said, "Belle should be in her room drawing right now, I'll call her down!"

Saying so, he went to the foot of the stairs and called up, "Belle! Belle! Come down quickly!"

Soon, a girl's head appeared upstairs.

She only showed half of her face peeking downwards, and upon seeing a stranger at home, immediately retracted her head like a little mouse.

Elo was quite helpless, he turned and said, "Belle is very shy and afraid of strangers. Her room is the only place where she can feel completely at ease. Except for meal times, she stays in her room, focusing on what she likes."

D nodded in understanding, extended his hand, and handed the egg fried rice to Elo, "In that case, you should give it to her!"

Elo: "Please wait here just a moment!"

D had no objections.

Taking the egg fried rice from D's hand, Elo went upstairs.

---

He gently knocked on his daughter's door: "Bell?"

Soon, the door opened.

Elo eagerly handed over the egg fried rice, then explained to his daughter the courage-boosting effects it could bring.

Upon hearing it, Bell was full of surprise: "Huh? How can there be such a food?"

"I don't know either. D specifically sent it over. He said this dish could help you." Elo explained, "D is the rancher at the ranch you visited that day. He just moved to the town this spring."

As he spoke, Elo whistled out the window, and soon enough, a small Skwovet scurried in through the window.

"Wanna give it a try?" Holding onto the hope that playing dead would somehow work, he wished his daughter could overcome her psychological barriers, not stay cooped up at home all the time, and be able to live under the sun.

"Okay." Feeling the expectations her father placed on her, Bell took a deep breath and opened the lunchbox.

The enticing aroma instantly wafted into her face. This egg fried rice seemed freshly made, still steaming.

The golden egg liquid nearly enveloped every grain of rice, each grain distinct, with the scent of oil, the fragrance of rice, and the aroma of eggs blending together, revealing a golden sheen that looked incredibly tempting.

Even though she had just had lunch, Bell couldn't help swallowing a mouthful of saliva at this moment.

Elo felt the same. He hadn't expected D to be such a good cook and thought maybe this dish could really help Bell?

"Eat up!" Elo urged.

Bell picked up a spoon and cautiously took a bite.

As the egg fried rice entered her mouth, her eyes immediately lit up.

Elo eagerly asked, "How does it feel?"

Bell blinked, tasting carefully for a moment, and suddenly felt a surge of courage within her.

She bit her lip for a while, as if making some kind of decision, and said to her father in front of her:

"Dad... actually, I don't want to go out. I want to stay at home forever..."

Elo was slightly taken aback.

Bell, too, seemed surprised that she had spoken her thoughts out loud.

She gasped and instinctively said, "I'm sorry," but then continued:

"I know you and Mom want me to go out more, but the outside world is so dangerous. There are Pokemon everywhere, and if I'm not careful, I could lose my life..."

Listening to his daughter, Elo's heart grew heavier: "Is this your true thought?"

The courage-boosting effect of the egg fried rice faded, and Bell quickly covered her mouth, then apologized to her father: "I'm sorry!"

"Sigh..." Elo asked again, "Do you not like it when Dad and Mom always push you to go out?"

"No..." Bell lowered her head, as if giving up: "I just... just feel like it's very dangerous outside. Walking on the street, I could get hit by a car; in the forest, I could be attacked by scary Pokemon!"

"Every Pokemon is so terrifying! I could be electrocuted by them, burnt to death by flames, or sliced in two by Scyther. And if it's a small Pokemon, what if I get bitten and die of infection? Ah ah ah... I don't want to die on the vast plains!"

Elo: "..." This child, why is she talking about death!

He hadn't expected that his actions all this time had brought such a burden to his daughter. Elo sighed and said to Bell:

"Let's talk when your mom returns. D is still downstairs, go on and continue with your drawing!"

Elo turned and headed downstairs.

Seeing his worried expression, and having overheard the conversation just now, D asked, "Is everything okay?"

"Sigh..." Elo let out a sigh. "I just wanted Bell to go out more, see the world, so when her mother and I are gone, she could live well in this world alone."

"Is that her true thought?" D asked.

Elo nodded: "But we should really thank you, D. If it weren't for you, I might still be forcibly pushing Bell to do things she doesn't like."

Upon hearing this, D thought to himself that the daughter might have some sort of paranoia, but it wasn't his place to interfere with family affairs.

He could only offer comfort: "Don't worry too much. That day is still far off. Maybe Bell will change along the way?"

"Let's hope so!" Elo seemed to have an endless amount of sighs. He took a bag of fertilizer from the nearby shelf and said, "Anyway, we need to thank you for your help today. This is new fertilizer from the store..."

"No, I can't accept it." D waved his hands repeatedly, indicating he was just coming over to offer a little help within his ability.

But Elo insisted, handing over the fertilizer:

"We're neighbors. This bag of fertilizer is a Berry Fertilizer, specially used for cultivating Berry trees. It's said to help trees produce more fruit."

"Since it's just arrived, I don't know the effects yet, so I hope you'll accept it. If it works well, come by to buy a few more bags."

D understood and accepted the bag of fertilizer: "I get it. If it's very effective, I'll come back to buy a few more."

"That's the spirit!"

Elo detailed the fertilization stages and amounts for each tree.

D nodded, indicating he remembered everything.

Then, he left the seed store, released Cyclizar from the Poke Ball, and thinking about today's events, he couldn't help but shake his head.

Sigh, such a wonderful girl!

D glanced back just in time to notice a person standing at the second-floor window of the seed store, watching him.

Noticing his gaze, the girl immediately shrank back like a quail.

D: "..."

Forget it! He patted Cyclizar's shoulder and said to it, "Let's go!"

Koraidon broke the bed at home, so they needed to buy a new one.

To prevent future breakage, D thought they should get a different kind of bed.

A Japanese tatami-style bed seemed pretty decent, with no legs and directly on the wooden floor, meaning Koraidon wouldn't break it again.

Moreover, with an extra layer underneath the house, there was no worry about the bedding getting damp.

And even if it did get damp, it wouldn't matter. It was summer, and the sun was strong. A day under the sunshine would make the bedding dry, soft, and filled with sunshine's fragrance.

The streets of Porto Marinada Town were very clean, lined with neat and attractive shops and residences.

D walked with Cyclizar along the streets, hoping that when he reached Keri's store, time, too, would arrive at mid-May.

By then, the crops in the fields should all be mature, and he could see what changes Gogoat's care brought to these crops.

Alas, it was impossible.

By three o'clock in the afternoon, after purchasing a bed from Keri's store, D returned to the ranch.

He began fertilizing the newly planted Berries and transplanted the tall Berry trees from the farmland area to the Orchard.

After completing this, D couldn't ignore the dirt on his hands, went to the storeroom, and used some wood to make a simple fence, enclosing the Berry Garden, leaving a small gate for entry and exit.

He and Koraidon then placed a sign painted with Berries beside the gate, indicating this area was specifically for cultivating Berries.

In the evening, after a busy day, D went to bed early after finishing dinner.

With a new tatami bed, Koraidon no longer worried about breaking it. He even boldly placed his tail on the bed, almost entirely on it.

Every time D saw this, it always seemed Koraidon had deliberately collapsed the previous two beds.

But seeing Koraidon's simple and sincere expression, D would suspect it was his own misunderstanding.

With nothing much for entertainment at the ranch, they went to sleep early every day.

Perhaps because he had never slept on a tatami before, D found himself struggling with insomnia that night.

He lay in the bedding with Pawmo, gazing at the ceiling above, wishing the era could progress a bit faster.

He truly missed his smartphone capable of internet and games!

But now, nothing of the sort was available, and thinking of this, D sighed inwardly.

Turning his head, he saw Koraidon's golden eyes, shimmering brightly in the dark.

"... Still awake?"

Koraidon seemed particularly happy: "Argh-rus~!"

"Look at you, silly." D didn't know what made Koraidon so gleeful and helplessly placed his ankle on its tail.

Koraidon thought to itself it wasn't silly at all!

D shook his head in disbelief and thought maybe he was wrong after seeing Koraidon's innocent and straightforward expression.

With that, he pulled covers over himself and soon fell asleep after a long, eventful day.

"""

---

---

---

---

# 143: Chapter 140: Foraging Mountain Goods, Midterm Exam (Two-in-One)

The next morning, after finishing his work at the ranch, D decided to take a walk in the back mountain to see if he could find anything worth collecting.

Most of the Pokemon in the ranch had their own tasks to do.

Koraidon was teaching Cyclizar some moves, Gogoat was taking care of the crops, Combee were collecting honey, Dragonite had gone to town to deliver milk, and Pawmo was managing the chickens…

So for this trip, D specifically called upon Butterfree, who was familiar with the back mountain, to join him.

Before setting out, he advised Cyclizar, "I'm heading out for a bit. Remember to learn well from Koraidon. Have you learned Dragon Pulse from last time?"

"Gaow~"

Cyclizar nodded and then ran over to affectionately hug D.

Since what happened yesterday, Cyclizar realized D wouldn't get angry or scold him, and it gradually became more courageous.

Seeing this, D felt a bit helpless.

He patted Cyclizar's head and said, "Since you've learned Dragon Pulse, you can practice the next move."

Saying so, D turned his head to look at Koraidon, noticing its envious expression, and gave it a hug as well.

Only then was Koraidon satisfied.

D didn't forget to add, "One more thing, don't teach Cyclizar how to use Draco Meteor just yet."

"Ahh gaa?"

D glanced at it and then at Cyclizar, explaining, "That move has too much power and coverage area. A little accident and it might destroy the ranch."

Hearing this, the two Pokemon indicated that they understood.

Seeing this, D smiled and said, "Then I'll be off!"

He picked up his Vine Whip Backpack, which contained some food to lure Pokemon, and said to Butterfree beside him, "Let's go!"

"Mwee~"

In the midst of summer, the trees in the back mountain were growing taller and denser.

The dark green foliage blocked out the sun, casting mottled shadows on the ground, while the distant chirping of bugs was occasionally joined by the loud cries of cicadas.

Everything seemed to confirm that this was a vibrant and lively forest.

Upon entering, D encountered many familiar Pokemon.

The common Caterpie, Tarountula hanging upside down in trees, and Nymble hiding in the grass.

D wasn't very interested in them, and given the small size of these Bug Type Pokemon, they wouldn't actively attack humans either.

The summer forest also concealed many treasures.

After walking for a short distance, D discovered a cluster of wild ginger in a patch of grass.

He knew a bit about such goods but not much, only aware that it could be used to make soup.

"Just right, I can go back and buy a sand pot to make soup,"

he thought, pulling up the wild ginger by the roots and putting it into his backpack.

Butterfree curiously watched this scene: "Mwee?"

D explained the use of these mountain goods and then asked with a laugh, "Butterfree, do you know where else there are edible things growing nearby?"

Butterfree blinked, thought carefully, and then nodded repeatedly: "Mwee!"

Oh, it really does know?

Butterfree led the way, and D quickly followed.

After passing through a dense thicket, they arrived in front of a large tree.

Sure enough, the tree was laden with many green-skinned, very unripe fruits.

Butterfree gently plucked one and placed it in D's palm.

At first, D didn't recognize it, but soon he realized what it was.

Unripened mountain almonds!

"Mwee~" Butterfree said, some Pokemon come here to eat these when they can't find food.

D didn't quite understand, but he nodded, then shook his head: "No, you can't eat this now. It isn't ripe yet."

"Mwee?"

D thought for a moment: "It probably needs another month or two to mature! This is also delicious when ready, with a crunchy and refreshing taste, it's a very nutritious fruit."

"Mwee!"

Butterfree had already started drooling.

D laughed: "I'll bring you back here when they're ripe."

Butterfree nodded eagerly.

D asked again: "Besides these, what did you eat when you were living in the wild?"

"Mwee..."

Butterfree thought carefully.

When it was still a Caterpie, its food was mostly leaves from trees, and occasionally it was lucky enough to find a Berry.

Most of the time, they stayed hidden in dense foliage and grass to avoid being eaten by predatory Bird-type Pokemon.

Though the chances were slim, they had observed the eating habits of many other Pokemon.

Butterfree sniffed the air and then led D further into the forest.

Soon, they arrived under an ancient towering tree.

D peered past the trunk and spotted a hive of wild Combee ahead.

Combee were constantly flying in and out of the hive to collect nectar while their leader, Vespiquen, patrolled the area, guarding the honey painstakingly made by the Combee.

"Wild honey, huh…" D was a bit surprised and softly asked, "Have you ever come here to steal honey before?"

Butterfree shook its head and whispered back, "Mwee!"

It never dared to come!

Those Combee were fierce, and Vespiquen was terrifying, not friendly like the Combee on the ranch.

But other Pokemon would come, like Ursaring and Teddiursa, and Mothim would sneak over at night while the Combee were asleep to steal honey.

---

"Forget it, there's honey on the ranch, let's not disturb these Combee." D said.

Butterfree had no objections, and quietly they went, continuing their search for usable wild goods in this area.

Meanwhile, in Mesagoza City.

The renowned Paldea Academy is located in this biggest city of the Paldea Region.

Contrary to what D thought, Orange Academy is just a branch of Paldea Academy. Together with Naranja Academy, they form Paldea Academy.

The two academies have different names, different uniforms, and different teaching focuses.

Students coming here can choose their preferred academy based on their interests.

Today is the midterm exam day at Paldea Academy.

Apart from the basic written exam, the academy specifically organized a Pokemon battle competition between Orange Academy and Naranja Academy.

The ultimate winner, besides getting an A rating on the performance board, can also receive the academy's Treasure Hunt Scholarship.

It's a significant amount of money, attracting many students to enroll in the competition.

In the open-air battle venue at the center of Mesagoza City, a tense and exciting Pokemon battle is underway.

Students in Orange Academy and Naranja Academy uniforms have gathered around the battle venue.

They shouted excitedly while discussing the two Trainers in the match with their companions.

"Hey, Delibird versus Crocalor, quickly guess who will win this battle!"

"Of course, it's Crocalor! Fire Type is strong against Ice Type Delibird!"

"Plus, the Trainer of that Delibird is quite famous in our Orange Academy!"

"Oh? Tell me more, what's the story?!"

"His name is Carter, and he's said to be a young master from a big family in Cascarrafa City. He's been pampered since childhood and is terrible at training Pokemon; his Delibird is notoriously weak. Even a regular Sprigatito can defeat him with the wrong type matchup."

"I heard not long ago, the young master resolved to work hard; every day, he took his Delibird to the playground to do push-ups and run. But he ended up exhausted in less than a week, causing quite a fuss."

On the battle stage, Carter's face flushed as he listened to others' comments about himself.

But soon, images of the past few days emerged in his mind.

At that ranch, with the sparring of those Gogoat comparable to Elite Four level in strength, his Delibird gained considerable experience and has now undergone a complete transformation! It even learned more powerful moves.

So, they certainly won't lose!

As if feeling the Trainer's emotions, the Delibird on the battlefield also responded loudly to him.

"Bibi!"

At this moment, the referee waved the flag and announced, "Let the battle begin!"

"Delibird, use Rain Dance!"

Carter immediately issued the command.

Rain Dance can reduce the power of Fire Type moves, which is very effective when facing Crocalor.

The opposing Trainer didn't issue a command right away but instead looked up at the scene with Crocalor.

"Rain Dance, huh? I'll let you have one move then, lest others say I relied on type matchups to beat you."

"No need!" Feeling belittled, Carter was annoyed. At this moment, it began to rain in the sky.

Crocalor's Trainer finally got serious: "Use Ember!"

Command given, Crocalor took two steps forward, and a string of hot embers spewed from its mouth, aiming straight at the Delibird in mid-air.

"Dodge and use Double Team!" Carter reacted quickly.

Delibird flapped its wings, ducked down in the rain, and the embers brushed past it. Then, Delibird moved swiftly, and numerous clones appeared in the rain curtain.

"Double Team? When did it learn that?!" The crowd was astonished, knowing the move was quite hard to master!

Carter's ears were sharp, and he caught people's voices, a smile of excitement on his face, thinking: It's all thanks to Mr. D!

In the Grass Trial, the relentless attacks from Gogoat forced out the potential within Delibird, prompting it to learn Double Team to dodge attacks.

And that's not all!

Thinking of this, Carter shouted, "Use that move!"

At the same time, Crocalor's Trainer issued a command: "Don't think I can't counter Double Team, Crocalor, use Heat Wave!"

Both Pokemon moved simultaneously on the field.

Crocalor took a heavy step forward, fire light surging from its throat, transforming into a gust of hot wind, sweeping towards Delibird.

Delibird was unhurried; it first flew forward, charging straight into the hot wind.

"What is it doing?! Is it not afraid to die?!"

The onlookers were shocked.

But just as Delibird was about to be engulfed by the Heat Wave, its body suddenly changed direction exaggeratedly and then shot straight into the sky.

Delibird played with the Heat Wave, as white light emerged on its wings, forming into a blade, swiftly cutting forward, slicing at Crocalor.

Crocalor's body staggered backward.

The crowd held their breath, unable to believe their eyes.

Soon, someone recognized the move's name.

"It's Aerial Ace!"

Carter felt a surge of pleasure in his heart, pointing towards Crocalor, and declared loudly:

"Compared to Mr. D, you're still far behind! Delibird, use Fling!"

The opposing young Trainer panicked and hurriedly said, "Crocalor, dodge!"

But it was too late.

Delibird had already positioned itself behind Crocalor, tightly holding Crocalor with its wings, and then using all its strength to fling it into the sky.

"Whoa!"

The crowd gasped in shock, their gaze following Crocalor up into the air.

Delibird followed closely, moving even faster to place itself above Crocalor.

"Now, use Brick Break!"

"Bibi!"

Delibird raised its wings high, chopping down hard, smashing Crocalor directly to the ground.

Everything settled.

Crocalor lay in a big pit, its eyes turned into spirals, having lost the ability to fight.

The referee came forward to check and then announced:

"Crocalor is unable to battle! The winner of this match is Orange Academy first-year student Carter!"

"Wha... how is it possible?!"

"Carter and his Delibird actually won!"

"And they defeated Crocalor against the type matchup!"

"I must be seeing things, right?!"

"How did you do it?"

People gathered around Carter, shocked by his growth.

Carter proudly basked in their attention, thinking, let them call me a useless rich kid!

He didn't want to tell these annoying people how he and Delibird grew.

But then, thoughts of D Ranch's Grass Trial and the Gogoat that easily overpowered his Delibird flashed through Carter's mind.

His eyes flickered with a sly idea, unable to resist making them suffer alone, Carter cleared his throat, pretending to be mysterious:

"It all started with getting lost."

Then, he looked around at everyone and asked, "Have you heard of the Grass Trial?"

"Grass Trial?" People shook their heads: "What's that? Tell us!"

Carter's mouth curved into a mysterious smile as he said:

"It's a trial that helps Pokemon train, and the reason Delibird and I won is entirely because we participated in that trial."

He paused for a moment, then asked, "Do you know how long it took us?"

Their curiosity piqued, they asked him, "How long?"

Carter held up one finger: "A day and a half! In just a day and a half, Delibird and I underwent a transformation!"

"A day and a half?" They were stunned: "How could it be? To achieve such a drastic change in a Pokemon in so little time, even the best Trainers and Breeders might not be able to do it, right?"

"That's because the creator of the Grass Trial is no ordinary person." Carter teased: "If you want to know more, you'll have to check it out yourselves! It's located near Porto Marinada Town at D Ranch."

In his heart, Carter silently prayed:

Mr. D, Gogoat, make sure to teach those annoying people a good lesson; don't let me suffer alone!
