# 144: Chapter 141: Braised Chicken with Wild Mushrooms

In the end, D returned to the ranch with a basket full of wild ginger, apricots, and mountain mushrooms on his back.

The apricots were found by a Butterfree, and there were many growing on that tree. D only picked a few to taste, leaving the rest for the wild Pokemon in the forest.

As for the mountain mushrooms, they were almost everywhere on the back mountain and easy to find.

The load was too much, and D found it a bit strenuous.

Seeing this, Koraidon and a group of Pokemon playing in the ranch quickly came over, wanting to help D with the basket of wild goods.

D said with a smile, "I'll make something delicious for you all for lunch today!"

Koraidon, helping him take down the basket, couldn't help but be curious when hearing this: "Ah gah ss?"

"Stewed chicken with mushrooms, have you heard of it?" D asked.

"Ah gah ss!" Koraidon's eyes lit up, nodding repeatedly, looking very eager.

"Oh? Where did you hear about this?" D was somewhat surprised, although there are many Chinese restaurants in the Paldea Region, Koraidon was in the Paldea Giant Pit the whole time before.

Could it be that Professor Sada and Professor Turo made it?

Unable to figure it out, D assumed Koraidon was just talking nonsense and didn't think much of it.

He asked Koraidon to help take the basket to the kitchen, so he could wash the vegetables later.

Koraidon obediently went to do it.

Meanwhile, Pawmo, upon hearing D intended to use the chickens under its care for cooking, couldn't help but show a sad expression.

"Ba mo…"

Seeing Pawmo's expression, D was slightly taken aback, then understood its thoughts.

D asked, "Pawmo, are you worried that I will use the hens from the Chicken House for cooking?"

"Ba mo…" Pawmo nodded its head.

Cyclizar on the side was a bit puzzled: "Gah ow?"

In its understanding, hens were meant to be eaten.

D explained with a smile to the group of Pokemon: "Pawmo is reluctant to see them being eaten because it spends time with the hens every day, which is a very normal thing."

Cyclizar nodded adorably: "Gah ow…"

To avoid leaving psychological shadows on the kids, D thought for a while, then continued to ask:

"Rest assured, these hens are kept for laying eggs. You should know, Dragonite and others can eat up to a hundred eggs every day."

"Ow woo!" Dragonite hurried to defend itself, saying it didn't eat that many eggs! It still preferred fish the most!

D explained carefully: "Egg pudding is also made with eggs, you can eat two baskets every day, and sandwiches also have fried eggs. I usually put egg sauce in cooking, all of these require eggs."

Dragonite thought carefully and realized it might really eat a hundred eggs? It scratched its head embarrassedly.

Seeing Pawmo put its mind at ease, D asked again: "What if I buy other chickens?"

Pawmo seriously considered it and then nodded slightly: "Ba mo!"

"Good kid." D rubbed its head and then went inside to grab his wallet. Riding on Cyclizar, he said to the group of Pokemon, "I'm going to town to buy an old hen, and I'll be back soon."

Hearing this, the group of Pokemon obediently waited at home.

By now, it was almost noon, and most of the vendors in Porto Marinada Town Market had already packed up to go home for lunch.

D wandered around several times before finding a meat shop that hadn't closed yet and bought a freshly slaughtered three-yellow chicken.

This type of chicken has firm and rich meat, and after being simmered, the broth is fresh and the meat tender, harmonizing perfectly with the aroma of mountain mushrooms.

After paying, D realized there were too many Pokemon on the ranch, and one might not be enough, so he bought a few more and conveniently bought two clay pots.

After returning to the ranch, he sent Cyclizar out to play and began preparing lunch.

Although he planned to make stewed chicken with mountain mushrooms, this dish takes a long time to make. It needs to be simmered on low heat for four hours to be ready.

So for today's lunch, D prepared the usual: rice, tomatoes and eggs, fried meat patties, sandwiches, Berry Juice, and MooMoo Milk.

After finishing lunch, D started preparing the ingredients and put them in the clay pots for slow cooking.

Several Pokemon were eagerly watching the stove.

Seeing them like this, D found it somewhat amusing and waved his hand, saying: "You guys go play outside; it'll take a long time to be ready. You'll be able to eat it at dinner."

The group of Pokemon had no choice but to give up.

They only craved it because Koraidon had said how delicious it is; otherwise, they wouldn't be so eager now.

But since D said it would take a long while, they all left the house and went to play on the grass outside.

D, reading in his room, glanced up briefly and saw Koraidon leading Cyclizar and Dragonite to the riverside.

The soil there, softened by the river's flow, plus the jumping Psyduck, resulted in several mud pits.

D, afraid they would dirty themselves, shouted from afar: "Don't roll in the mud pits! Koraidon, bring Cyclizar and the others back to play on the grass!"

Koraidon, hearing D's voice from afar, looked disdainfully at the mud pits not far away.

It responded loudly: "Ah gah ss!"

It wouldn't roll in the mud pits! It hadn't even touched the mud!

However, just after saying this, Koraidon saw Cyclizar stepping into a mud pit, running to the river's edge, and lowering its head to drink some water.

Koraidon: "..."

It couldn't help but feel exasperated and pulled Cyclizar to a clean patch of grass.

Using a Berry it fumbled from somewhere, it made a trade with the Psyduck across the way.

Soon, a Psyduck approached, using Water Gun on Cyclizar to wash off the mud.

Koraidon was satisfied, thinking that as long as D didn't see, it hadn't rolled in the mud pit.

"Ow woo..."

Dragonite watched this scene, thinking Koraidon was quite nice to Cyclizar! It even used Berry to get Psyduck to wash it.

Koraidon gave the Berry to the Psyduck.

The Psyduck made a happy sound and ran away holding the Berry.

Koraidon, watching the Psyduck across the way, suddenly recalled the event in the not-so-distant future.

The crops in the field matured, attracting the Psyduck to come and steal the produce to eat, only to be caught by D and brought into the ranch.

Since then, these Psyduck took on the role of harvesters in the ranch.

They would pick all the fruits when the crops matured and put them into large baskets prepared by D. Some would be sold, and some would be kept to make delicious treats for them.

Since they're companions, it didn't care anymore!

Koraidon brought Cyclizar and Dragonite back to the grass in front and, like an adult, started guiding Cyclizar and Dragonite.

D, who was reading, took a moment to glance over, seeing they were getting along quite harmoniously, and continued reading.

This book was given to him by Mayor Thomas back then, containing a wealth of knowledge about ranch life, and D would occasionally take it out to read a bit.

---

---

---

---

# 145: Chapter 142: Another Customer Orders Milk

At four-thirty in the afternoon, the chicken soup was ready.

D lifted the lid of the clay pot, and a rich aroma of chicken mixed with the fragrant scent of mountain mushrooms wafted up to his nose.

Inside the pot, the broth was bubbling away with a layer of shiny oil floating on top. The chicken and mushrooms were plump and translucent, soaked with flavor.

[Braised Chicken with Mushrooms (A): A dish made by simmering freshly picked mountain mushrooms with Sanhuang chicken. The soup is fresh and delicious, favored by certain Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Oily and Slippery lv3, after consumption, the Pokemon's fur becomes slick and shiny, not prone to shedding, and its health improves to the maximum.

Comment: The perfect dish for autumn!]

Oily and Slippery?

"It seems to only be effective on furry Pokemon," D thought, "The effect is not bad, just right for the Pawmot and Glaceon on the farm."

Other breeders go through great lengths to feed numerous Pokeblocks to achieve this effect, but now, it seems a single dish of braised chicken with mushrooms would suffice.

D picked up a piece of chicken for a closer taste.

The chicken was tender and flavorful, the meat firm yet juicy, with the rich aroma of mountain mushrooms, a perfect combination.

Lying on the grass outside basking in the sun, Koraidon and other Pokemon twitched their noses, suddenly catching the supremely enticing aroma.

"Ah-gash!"

Koraidon immediately straightened up, while the Dragonite and Cyclizar next to it also caught the scent and couldn't resist floating towards the house.

Hearing the commotion behind him, D turned off the stove and used tools to place the hot clay pot on the small dining table.

"Be careful, it's hot!" he reminded without forgetting.

The clay pot was indeed hot, and several Pokemon weren't very adept at using utensils, so they could only sit at the table, staring blankly.

D thought for a moment, then took a few small bowls from the kitchen and began serving the meat into them.

The Alcremie, Dolliv, and Nacli weren't interested in this aroma, so they glanced over but continued to play outside.

Glaceon and Pawmot seemed to like it, as D noticed them salivating.

For instance, now, Glaceon looked at the steaming piece of chicken in front of it and tried to paw at it, but got scalded instead.

After a brief thought, Glaceon blew a cool breath at the meat, then easily swallowed it into its belly.

Seeing this, the gaze of Cyclizar and the other Pokemon turned unanimously towards it.

"Brii-ee?!" Realizing it was being watched by so many, Glaceon's heart suddenly skipped a beat.

Cyclizar looked down at the meat in front of it, then at Glaceon's empty plate.

"Garr-ow..."

"Brii-ee."

With that, Glaceon turned its head and blew a cold breath on the piece of meat.

Only then did Cyclizar happily start eating.

The remaining Pokemon followed suit, so Glaceon could only help blow on the meat for others while sneaking bites for itself.

Watching this group of Pokemon getting along so harmoniously, D couldn't help but smile.

He looked towards the joyfully eating Pawmot and asked, "How does it taste?"

"Bam-oh~" Pawmot nodded repeatedly.

D deliberately teased, "What if I want to use those chickens for cooking in the future?"

"Bam-oh?!"

Pawmot froze, blinking its eyes, seriously contemplating his words, looking quite conflicted.

All these days, it had been spending time with that group of hens. Although they couldn't talk and couldn't understand its intentions, watching the fluffy little chicks grow into big chickens gave it a real sense of accomplishment.

But the chicken tasted so good.

Delicious chicken on one side, the hens it had raised on the other, Pawmot didn't know how to choose.

It even felt that if it saw those chickens again, it would think of the taste of today's dish.

"Bam-oh..."

Pawmot looked troubled.

Just then, Cyclizar said, "Garr-ow." (Hens are food by nature.)

It knew this even before it came to the farm.

At these words, the scales in Pawmot's heart tipped.

"Bam-oh..."

Seeing it so conflicted, D couldn't help but laugh, "Alright, don't worry, I won't use them for cooking."

After all, they're just a few ordinary chickens, if Pawmot likes raising them, he would let it, he wouldn't mind the expense.

D sat down, eating while placing meat into the bowls in front of the Pokemon.

He cooked several chickens, and before long, they were all gone.

After the meal, Koraidon took Cyclizar and the other Pokemon outside to lie contently on the grass.

Looking at this scene, D felt that he wouldn't need to make dinner tonight.

Just as well, he could take a break.

After washing the dishes, it was already evening.

Zack arrived in his large truck to pick up the goods. D chatted with him while helping to load the buckets of cow and goat milk, along with a large bag of rock salt, onto the truck.

"Hey, did you hear? There was a mine collapse over near Zapapico Town!"

D was a bit surprised, "Mine collapse? What happened?"

Zack: "Apparently, a construction crew accidentally disturbed a wild Tyranitar residing in the mountains when they were digging a tunnel. That Tyranitar got really angry and collapsed many of the mine shafts! Fortunately, it happened at night, so besides a dozen miners getting injured, there weren't any deaths."

Tyranitar, also known as the Sandstorm Monarch, is powerful, a quasi-god in the Johto Region.

D's collector instincts were subtly stirred, "Is it still there?"

"I don't know." Zack shook his head, "Maybe it left! But after the cave-in, many ancient ruins appeared underground. Quite a few adventurers headed there upon hearing the news, hoping to find the legendary ancient treasures."

Ancient ruins… ancient treasures…

D thought it over and realized that there weren't any famous ruins in the Paldea Region.

As for ancient treasures, they likely referred to the Auspicious Armor and Malicious Armor, special items that allow Charcadet to evolve into Armarouge and Ceruledge.

"Alright, I have to go pick up goods at other farms, see you later!"

With a wave, Zack returned to his truck, departing with a load of goods.

With everything for the day done, D took a deep breath, feeling completely relaxed, planning to take a bath later and then watch some TV before going to sleep.

At this moment, another guest arrived at the farm.

Upon hearing a sound, D turned to see the Seed Shop Owner.

He couldn't help but feel a bit surprised, "Elo? What brings you here?"

Elo explained, "Well, I heard that the goat milk from your place in town is very popular and hard to get, so I was wondering if I could order directly from you..."

"Of course!" D called over Dragonite and told it about the matter.

After listening, Dragonite thumped its chest, making a promise, "Woo-oh-woo-oh!"

Leave it to me!

"I'll leave it to you then." D patted its head.

Seeing this, Elo was very grateful, "Thanks a lot."

D waved his hand casually, "It's nothing, we're all neighbors! Plus, you gave me a bag of fertilizer."

Seeing D so generous, Elo broke into a smile. He thought of that incident, and once again sincerely thanked D:

"Thanks to you, if it weren't for you, Abby and I would still be pushing Bell to do something she doesn't like."

---

---

---

---

# 146: Chapter 143: Dragonite Delivery Service

Speaking of Bell, D thought of the girl suffering from paranoia.

He casually asked, "How is Bell doing now?"

Elo sighed, "She's still the same, staying in her room all day drawing, barely coming out except at meal times."

D nodded and suddenly had a good idea.

"I have a method we could try, but I'm not sure if it will work."

"Oh?" Elo quickly asked, "What method? Tell me about it!"

D: "It's to let your daughter start raising a Pokemon from an egg."

"I think she's not just afraid of Pokemon; she's afraid of the outside world. Like you said, her childhood experiences left her with a psychological shadow, making her mistakenly believe that everything outside is full of dangers."

"In this case, achieving it all at once is impossible. We have to take it slow, first by giving her a Pokemon egg to take care of."

"To her, a Pokemon egg wouldn't seem so frightening; it's just a harmless egg."

"Once she's gradually familiarized with the egg, if the Pokemon hatches then, she might not feel so scared and might even accept the Pokemon she hatched with her own hands."

Elo nodded repeatedly, his eyes lighting up: "Why didn't I think of this brilliant idea?!"

D laughed: "It's just a suggestion, maybe it can help you."

Elo thought carefully, then asked again, "What kind of Pokemon egg do you think I should give Bell?"

Hearing this, D pondered while saying, "Firstly, it can't be a Scyther egg, nor any Pokemon that appears large and frightening."

Elo: "Mm-hmm, then what?"

D continued: "Secondly, it should be a Pokemon that's easy to get close to people and doesn't randomly emit fire or electricity, like Pikachu, because when stimulated, it discharges."

Elo agreed with a nod.

Suitable Pokemon...

A few types of Pokemon popped into D's mind, and then he suggested:

"Ralts, a small and humanoid Pokemon that can use psychic powers to sense the Trainer's feelings and thoughts."

"And also Vulpix, Togepi, Sunkern, Slowpoke, Eevee..."

Saying this, D suddenly realized that Pokemon like Ralts and Vulpix are rare, and it's hard for ordinary people to obtain their eggs.

After some thinking, he suggested, "Then how about Eevee!"

Eevee looks harmless, and many people raise this Pokemon as pets in ordinary families. Their eggs are relatively easy to obtain.

Elo responded with excitement, "I got it, thank you so much for your advice, D!"

D smiled, seeing that Elo was about to leave, he asked Dragonite to follow and familiarize itself with the route, so they could start delivering milk to them early in the morning.

Dragonite had no objections and nodded before following Elo to Porto Marinada Town.

Watching their backs, D couldn't help but think about what to do when the number of people ordering milk from the ranch increases and Dragonite can't keep up...

He thought of the group of Dragonite on Dragonite Island.

Could he possibly hire them to come over and help with work?

D remembered last time he went to Dragonite Island, several Dragonite wanted to follow him, and he thought this method was feasible.

But this matter still needed to be discussed with Dragonite.

Taking advantage of this time, D went and washed up, and when he returned to the house drying his hair, Dragonite landed in the ranch.

D was a bit surprised to see what Dragonite was carrying: "What's that?"

"Roar-woo~"

Dragonite happily walked forward, placing the bundle on the table. D opened it to find a pile of berries inside.

He raised an eyebrow: "From Mr. Elo?"

"Roar-woo!" Dragonite nodded.

"Alright." D understood it should be thanks for the suggestion he provided.

Refusing would be inappropriate, so he accepted it, placing it aside as snacks for the Pokemon.

As it was about to get dark, after delivering the items to D, Dragonite planned to go outside to watch fireflies and stars, but D stopped it.

Dragonite looked puzzled: "Roar-woo?"

D asked, "Dragonite, what do you think about the group of Dragonite on Dragonite Island?"

Upon hearing this, Dragonite became alert.

It wondered why? Did D take a liking to the other Dragonite?

No, that won't do! D is its Trainer! One Dragonite is enough!

With a serious expression, Dragonite shook its big head and started badmouthing the group of Dragonite: "Roar-woo roar-woo!"

D: "..."

Though he couldn't understand, D roughly guessed Dragonite's meaning.

His Pokemon are indeed quite jealous.

After some thought, D said, "It's not to catch them; it's to hire them to help work on the ranch."

Dragonite tilted its head in confusion: "Roar-woo?"

"I'm thinking that when more people start ordering milk here and you're overwhelmed, it'll exhaust you! That's when we can call them over to help with deliveries."

"Moreover, they are surely unfamiliar with the outside world of the island, at that time, you can be their leader, introducing them to the outside world and leading them to the town for deliveries."

Dragonite thought it over carefully, envisioning itself as a Dragonite King commanding a bunch of Dragonite to deliver milk across the sky.

The once weakest Dragonite becoming the leader of Dragonite, how come the more it thought about it, the more comfortable it felt?

Seeing Dragonite's expression soften, D quickly asked, "How is it? Want to give it a try? If they don't do well, we'll fire them."

"Roar-woo?" What does fire mean?

"It means not letting them deliver anymore, sending them back to Dragonite Island."

"Roar-woo!" That's acceptable!

"So it's settled then?" D asked.

Dragonite nodded, then joyfully hugged D.

D was so kind! Concerned about its fatigue, he called other Dragonite to help with the work! How is there such a kind Trainer in the world?!

Dragonite was very touched.

D patted its back, smiling: "Okay, there's no rush for this, go play outside!"

This was just his plan for the future, currently only two people ordered from the ranch, Dragonite could still manage.

Moreover, the ranch is in the development stage; at this time, if suddenly dozens of Dragonite showed up, it'd completely bankrupt him.

At times like this, it would be great to develop a hunger-satisfying dish.

Speaking of which, D remembered in the anime, May made "May's Food No. 2" based on a formula given by Professor Oak.

It's a very special Pokeblock, where one piece could fill even a Munchlax known for its large appetite.

---

---

---

---

# 147: Chapter 144: Housekeeper Pokemon

Early the next morning, D was standing by the farmland watching Dragonite water the crops when Allen suddenly came rushing in from outside on a Cyclizar.

From a distance, he excitedly shouted to D, "D, I've got news about the thing you asked me to do last time!"

Hearing this, D was slightly startled, wondering what task he had assigned to Allen.

But then he remembered the time during the spring competition when he had asked Allen to help him find a Housekeeper Pokemon.

Housekeeper Pokemon...

D was intrigued and quickly said, "Tell me more!"

Allen jumped off the Cyclizar and explained in detail:

"Here's the thing, a few days ago I received a letter, and the person asked me to deliver it to someone in the neighboring Cascarrafa City."

"That person lives in a very large mansion, and when I delivered the letter, I heard that the owner of the mansion was looking for someone to take care of two Indeedee!"

"Didn't I remember that you needed a Housekeeper Pokemon? So, I specifically asked around, and an elderly man, who seemed quite frail, received me. He asked me some questions about you and then suggested we come together to take a look when we have time."

Indeedee? And two of them?

D looked surprised, "Two?"

Allen nodded, "Yes, two Experieces Even Indeedee!"

D was a bit puzzled, "Why would that person give away such Pokemon?"

Allen shook his head, "I don't know, his only requirement is that someone can care for the two Indeedee well. Think about when you've got time, we can go together."

"I'm available right now." Saying that, D headed back to the house to change his clothes, "Which city is it again?"

Allen: "Cascarrafa City, it's very close to us. You can get there in less than two hours on a Cyclizar."

"Okay." D nodded.

He turned back into the house to change his clothes, grabbed some change and a backpack, and told Koraidon to watch over the ranch, saying he'd be back in the afternoon. There's food in the fridge, and it should be given to everyone for lunch.

Koraidon nodded back obediently and wasn't jealous at all.

"Good, I'll make you something tasty later."

D patted its head, then got on his Cyclizar just like Allen.

The two left the ranch and headed towards the target city.

On the way, D curiously asked, "Aren't you working today?"

Allen looked puzzled, "Today's the weekend! The post office is off for two days, no work!"

D: "..."

Oh dear, he completely forgot about that.

Allen laughed, "Have you been living alone so long that you've forgotten the days?"

"No." D said truthfully, "I just didn't expect you guys to have two days off without working overtime. I'm really envious."

Allen said speechlessly, "Is the work at the ranch that tiring?"

"Not really." D said.

With so many Pokemon to help, his daily work was very light, except when plowing and planting crops, which was a bit busier, and preparing large amounts of food daily.

Although tiring, seeing so many Pokemon living on the ranch was a joy for him.

He just felt this world is really easygoing, without overtimes and with two days off a week, which was unthinkable for a working-class person from his previous life, unless in specific jobs.

But he couldn't explain the specific reasons to Allen, so he switched the topic, "I haven't been to Cascarrafa City yet!"

Not thinking much of it, Allen heard this and laughed, "Cascarrafa City is much bigger than our Porto Marinada Town! It's a famous Water City, also known as the aquatic paradise."

D recalled the map of the Paldea Region in his mind.

Porto Marinada Town is located in the northwest part of the map, while Cascarrafa City is in the central area of the Paldea Region, situated next to the Paldea Giant Pit, with a vast desert area between it and Porto Marinada Town.

Thinking about this, D asked, "Do we need to go through the Asado Desert first?"

Allen shook his head, "No need, the desert is too dangerous, the Pokemon there are very aggressive, and the weather is extremely hot. Today we're taking the main road, which was officially built by the League. There's no danger from Pokemon on the road, so we can walk safely."

D nodded and didn't say much more.

The two continued along the main road.

Because of Allen's words, D made a point of observing the Pokemon by the roadside and indeed found, just as Allen had said, that they were all small in size, mild-mannered, and non-aggressive Pokemon.

Along the way, besides themselves, they occasionally saw a few passing trucks, backpackers camping in the fields, or Trainers engaged in Pokemon battles.

When running at full speed, Cyclizar's speed can rival that of a real motorcycle, and it's said that it can reach speeds of up to 120km/h.

As time ticked by, the sun rose high, the air became hot, and at last, a lively bustling city appeared ahead.

"We're here!" Allen said happily.

D looked up to see the entire Cascarrafa City situated on a cliff, due to the terrain, the whole city was divided into three tiers.

Through Cascarrafa City, he could see further into the distance, a stretch of endless rugged cliffs, unreachable by sight, obscured by white misty clouds.

---

"Could that be the Paldea Giant Pit?" D thought.

Just by looking from the outside, one could feel the grandeur and majesty of the Paldea Giant Pit. Who knows what it's like inside?

D wanted to go in for a visit, but feared it wouldn't be permitted.

The two entered Cascarrafa City. D dismounted from the Cyclizar, which transformed into an upright form and walked alongside him through the streets.

The streets were paved with a layer of blue and white tiles, and at intervals, beautiful fountain parks could be seen, with huge, magnificent water curtains flowing from the top layer, connecting the entire city.

People in hot bikinis and shorts strolled leisurely nearby, with a lively atmosphere everywhere.

Allen watched the scene and laughed, "Look, isn't it lively?"

"It sure is!"

D nodded, and Cascarrafa City gave him the feeling of being in the Water City Venice, where water-based elements could be seen everywhere.

However, they didn't have much time to enjoy the sights. Taking an elevator to the third floor, Allen turned a corner, passed through a bustling district, and reached a luxurious manor.

Two guards stood in front of the manor. As the two approached, Allen stated their purpose.

Upon hearing this, the guard on the left glanced at D for a moment, then stepped aside, opened the door, and respectfully said:

"The master has been expecting you for a while, please come in!"

The term 'master'... How rare that sounds, D couldn't help but muse internally.

Following Allen into the manor, D looked at the vast expanse of land before him and his first thought was, "Isn't this place too big?"

Allen whispered, "I was startled the first time I came too! But don't worry, the owner here is very amiable, nothing like the pompous rich people you see on TV."

D nodded and continued forward. Yet soon, they found themselves lost in the vast manor.

Allen: "…Strange, did I remember wrong? How is this not the right path?"

Looking at the huge garden maze around them, D finally noticed something was amiss.

He asked, "Did you not have someone leading the way last time you came?"

All along, they hadn't seen a single person.

Allen: "I did! There were quite a few people in the manor last time! Gardeners trimming the gardens, housekeepers in black tailcoats, maids watering plants..."

As he spoke, he suddenly realized something was wrong: "Huh? Why isn't there a single person today?"

D thought of scenes from movies and asked, "Could this be a ghost manor? Like in the movies, where everyone is a ghost trying to lure people in to kill them."

Just then, a chilly breeze blew past, as if something was watching them from the dark.

Allen was very scared, "Don't scare me!"

The two Cyclizars following them trembled in fear, "Gaaa…"

D: "…I was just joking."

Allen then heaved a sigh of relief.

D added, "But there are indeed ghosts in this world."

Allen and the two Cyclizars widened their eyes. They traversed the garden maze, frightened yet curious.

"Have you seen one?" Allen asked.

D nodded, "I encountered one when I traveled through the Galar Region before."

He then vividly described how he had met a ghostly little girl and helped deliver her letter to an old friend, only to find out that her old friend was already an elderly man in his sixties or seventies.

The old man said the little girl was his childhood sweetheart, whom he hadn't seen since they grew up.

D: "This means the girl had already died when she was a child, wandering the streets as a ghost."

After hearing this story, Allen held his Cyclizar tightly in fear.

D chuckled, "Is it really that scary?"

Allen and his Cyclizar nodded in unison.

D wanted to say more, but when he turned around, he saw his Cyclizar was also very scared.

"Gaaa…"

"Don't be afraid!"

D patted Cyclizar's shoulder and then took its paw, comforting it in this way.

Seeing that Cyclizar was not so afraid, he continued to move forward while saying:

"Actually, that ghost girl meant no harm. Someone once said, the ghosts you fear are the individuals whom others long to see but never can again."

"And after I delivered her letter, she gave me a gift, a Reaper Cloth imbued with amazing power."

"Look, thinking of it that way, it's not so scary, right?" D spread his hands. Although these were just encounters he had in a game.

Allen felt a little better, contemplating D's words carefully, and nodded, "Indeed."

He wanted to say more, but at that moment, they finally passed through the garden maze and saw a beautiful fountain on the lawn ahead.

A circle of Pokemon was gathered around the fountain.

There was a Sprigatito, Fuecoco, Quaxly, Pawmi, Tandemaus, Fidough…

Suddenly seeing these rare Pokemon, D couldn't help but show surprise.

But soon, he noticed the small creature surrounded by the Pokemon.

Allen saw it too; it was a Meowth.

To be precise, an injured Meowth.

"It's injured!" Allen exclaimed.

---

The group of Pokemon also noticed them and turned their heads one after another.

They didn't seem to be afraid of strangers.

Seeing this, D stepped forward to check on the Meowth's condition. Seeing that it was very weak, he took out a bottle of Berry Milk from his backpack to restore its strength.

This was originally prepared for Cyclizar, fearing that the journey was too long and Cyclizar would get thirsty, so D brought several bottles.

Unexpectedly, they came in handy here.

The Meowth drank the entire bottle of Berry Milk in one go, and its wounds began to heal at a speed visible to the naked eye.

The surrounding Pokemon looked on in wonder, casting curious glances at this human.

Meowth stood up: "Meow~"

Seeing its energetic appearance, D smiled and said, "Alright, be more careful next time."

"Meow~"

A group of Pokemon then hopped and skipped away, running into the garden maze to start a game of hide and seek.

Watching this scene, Allen once again felt the grandeur of the mansion's owner:

"Could it be that the purpose of building this garden maze was to let the Pokemon play hide and seek?"

D: "It seems that way at the moment."

He pondered whether to build a small maze for hide and seek at the ranch later.

Allen muttered, "Truly extravagant!"

D chuckled and looked up at the building ahead: "Let's go! That should be the destination."

Passing the garden fountain, they finally saw the third person besides themselves.

It was a maid wearing an apron, holding a broom and cleaning.

D walked up and politely introduced himself and his purpose for coming.

After listening, the maid smiled and nodded, turned around with her broom: "Please follow me! The master has been waiting for you for a long time."

Hearing this, D felt something was not quite right.

He looked at Allen, who seemed to have noticed nothing.

The two followed the maid into the large villa.

Pushing open the door, D saw an elderly man dressed in a blue kimono, with white hair, a face full of wrinkles, and a slightly tired expression, sitting on the sofa in the villa's hall.

And on his left and right sides were two similar-looking Pokemon.

They both had horns on their heads, except one had upward horns and the other had downward horns.

The one with upward horns had half-open, half-closed eyes and a blank expression, while the one with downward horns wore a gentle expression, with upturned mouth corners, displaying a pleasant smile.

Indeedee...

Information about the two Pokemon surfaced in D's mind; one was male, and the other was female.

They stood respectfully beside the old man, watching the few people who had pushed open the door and entered.

"Is it them?" D thought to himself.

They followed the maid to the old man.

At this point, the old man spoke, "You've finally arrived, please sit down."

The two nodded, then sat on the sofa opposite the old man.

"Hello," D greeted politely, "I'm D, currently running a ranch in Porto Marinada Town, and this is my partner and assistant, Cyclizar."

The old man nodded slightly, murmuring softly, "A rancher..."

After a moment, he continued: "You can just call me Sato, what do you think? Is that okay?"

Saying this, the old man turned his head and inquired of the two Indeeedees beside him.

The male Indeedee on the left nodded calmly.

The female Indeedee on the right looked at D, giving him a gentle smile, "Indeedee~"

Seeing their reactions, the old man laughed, "That's good, I see he's a great choice to take care of those two kids."

"Hmm?" Hearing this, D was somewhat taken aback, "Excuse me, Mr. Sato, what does that mean?"

Seeing his expression, the old man guessed what he was thinking—probably thought it was the two Indeeedees.

He laughed heartily and shook his head, "They've been with me for over twenty years, I can't bear to let anyone else take care of them. I'm looking to have their children adopted."

"I'll be frank, D, when you came over earlier, I witnessed your actions; you passed a small test I set for you."

D was slightly taken aback, with the image of the injured Meowth by the fountain flashing through his mind.

The old man smiled and said, "Being able to rescue an unfamiliar Pokemon shows you're a very compassionate person."

"Moreover, the ghost story you told in the garden was marvelous... the ghosts you fear are people others long to see but can never meet again, indeed!"

At this point, it seemed as if the old man thought of something, but he quickly recovered, continuing:

"Of course, that's not important, what's important is that when Cyclizar got scared, you comforted it in time and held its hand, showing you're very patient with Pokemon."

Upon hearing all this, D felt a bit embarrassed.

Seeing his expression, the old man couldn't help but laugh, "Please forgive me, it wasn't my intention to eavesdrop on your conversation. I heard from the servant that you got lost in that garden maze."

"Because a lot of Pokemon usually play there, for their safety, I have many surveillance cameras installed in that area."

---

D shook his head: "It's okay."

"In that case, please follow me!" The old man stood up and led the way.

D and Allen exchanged a glance, then lifted their feet to follow, eventually arriving at a warmly decorated nursery room.

There, D saw two Indeedees playing with toys.

They were very small, seeming to have just been born not long ago.

The old man introduced, "These are their children, just two weeks old, a brother and sister. Do you want to go up and get to know them?"

D nodded, then walked forward and squatted in front of the two Indeedees.

The siblings sensed a strange presence and turned to look at this stranger.

The sister Indeedy widened her eyes and then revealed an innocent smile: "Eee~ya!"

The brother, though young, had a mature expression. Seeing his sister being so enthusiastic to the stranger, he showed a disapproving look.

D took out two bottles of Berry Milk and two Lucky Puddings from his backpack.

He didn't feed them immediately but turned around and asked, "Mr. Sato, can they have some snacks?"

The old man and the two adult Indeedees stood aside, watching his interactions with the two little Indeedees, and nodded: "Of course."

With permission granted, D fed them the Berry Milk and Lucky Pudding.

He inserted the straw, and the sister Indeedy, smelling the scent, grabbed the bottle and instinctively started sipping the milk inside.

"Eya~!" She took a big gulp before stopping.

D opened the Lucky Pudding's package and fed it to her.

The sister Indeedy happily accepted it, and after she ate the entire pudding, the way she looked at him changed entirely.

"Eya! Eya!"

The sister Indeedy waved her arms, wanting to be hugged.

D reached out and picked her up, feeling the soft, furry texture in his hands, making his movements very gentle.

"Aren't you going to eat?" D looked at the brother Indeedy nearby.

The brother Indeedy, with eyes half-open, half-closed, still looked at his sister disapprovingly.

D used his other hand to nudge the bottle towards him: "Want to give it a try?"

Looking at the milk bottle so close, the brother Indeedy was a bit surprised. He smelled the milk's aroma wafting from it.

"Gulp!"

Secretly swallowing, the brother Indeedy finally couldn't resist stepping forward and taking a sip.

Seeing this, D chuckled with delight.

Quite adorable.

However, hearing his laughter, the brother Indeedy immediately reverted to his mature look, blushing and turning his head shyly, pretending nothing had happened.

D didn't say much more, just played with the sister Indeedy for a while.

The two adult Indeedees watched this scene tenderly, beginning to convers between themselves softly.

"Ai ee."

"Ai ee~"

Hearing their conversation, the old man's face softened into a gentle smile: "It looks like the siblings really like him."

The old man turned to look at the two Indeedees again, asking, "Are you sure? Is it him?"

The two Indeedees nodded earnestly.

"Ai ee."

————

After a dozen minutes, the sister Indeedy got tired of playing and joined her brother for a nap.

They are still young and need a lot of time to rest every day.

The group returned to the living room downstairs.

The old man said, "If possible, I'd like to wait until they're capable of doing tasks before handing them over to you. First, we'll teach them to do house chores during this time, and second, I hope the Indeedees can watch their kids grow up, so they feel assured."

"Of course, no problem." D agreed without hesitation.

He also really liked the two little Indeedees, but leaving them to grow up with their parents is very important for them.

Seeing him agree, the old man was pleased internally: "Please give me your address. When they grow up, I'll send them to your ranch, at most two months."

Pokemon grow quickly, and like Indeedy, they can grow from a small size to a normal size within three months.

"Alright." D had no objections. He told the other party the detailed address of his ranch, then asked, "Do I need to pay a deposit or anything?"

"That's not necessary." The old man gently shook his head: "We only have one request, and that's to take good care of the two little Indeedees."

---

---

---

---

# 148: Chapter 145: Missionary D

Having resolved the butler issue, D was in high spirits.

He specifically invited Allen out for a meal in Cascarrafa City.

They chose the Fresh Fish Restaurant, a place specializing in fish dishes.

The two of them sat under the sunshade at the entrance, with Cyclizar, as waiters brought various fish dishes and four drinks.

While placing a fresh, delicious piece of salmon sashimi in front of Cyclizar, D said:

"I really owe it to you today; otherwise, I might take ages to find the Housekeeper Pokémon."

Allen replied straightforwardly: "Just a minor thing! Is there anything else you need my help with?"

There really is. D thought to himself, as he asked: "Do you know where I can learn to make Pokeblock?"

"Pokeblock?" Allen took a bite of fish, thought carefully, and said: "I really don't know about that. Usually, people proficient at making Pokeblock are Pokémon Breeders."

"So where did they learn it from?" D asked.

"Probably the Breeder Association?" Allen guessed, "I'm not really sure, since I'm just a town mailman, but don't worry, I'll inquire for you when I deliver mail next time."

D: "Thanks a lot."

Allen reminded him: "I'm helping you out, so don't let me down in next year's horse race!"

Upon hearing this, D secretly complained in his heart: This guy's still thinking about the ten thousand League coins lost on betting horses!

But he didn't dare to make any promises, as he currently had no way to leave the ranch for too long, let alone leave the Paldea region.

Seeing D's expression, Allen began to insist: "No way! We had a deal! I help you find the Housekeeper Pokémon, and you help me win back those ten thousand League coins!"

"Fine, fine!" D nodded helplessly: "How about this, when winter comes and there's nothing to do in the fields, I'll take the opportunity to go out and see if I can encounter a Horse-type Pokémon."

By then, Indeedee should be able to help him share the workload at the ranch, allowing him some time to visit the Galar region.

This finally satisfied Allen, though he was still a bit confused: "But don't Horse-type Pokémon tend to be absent in winter?"

"Depends on the species!" D's mind recalled Calyrex's Glastrier: "Some Horse-type Pokémon are Ice Type, they like to appear in the icy snow."

Allen: "…I've never heard of such a Horse-type Pokémon."

D teased: "You'll find out when the time comes."

Allen: "Okay, then I'll await your good news!"

After finishing their meal, the two began to ride Cyclizar back.

By the time D returned to the ranch, it was already two o'clock in the afternoon.

A group of Pokémon were leisurely basking in the sun on the grass, and upon seeing D return, they all came up to greet him.

D patted each of their little heads, smiling as he asked: "Did you all have a good day?"

"Awoo!"

"Ah ga sss～"

"Muuu～"

"Yawn yawn!"

"..."

Seeing that everyone was safe and sound, D felt relieved.

He let Alcremie and the others continue to play, while he went back indoors to start tidying up the house that had been messed up by the Pokémon.

During midday, he wasn't around, so Glaceon's lunch was likely prepared with Koraidon's help.

This was evidenced by the notable reduction in sandwiches, Berries, and Berry Milk in the refrigerator.

Empty milk bottles were scattered loosely on the table, and one had even rolled onto the floor.

D stepped forward to pick them up, washed them clean, and placed them in the fridge for reuse later.

After cleaning indoors, D went outside, broom in hand, to sweep away the fallen leaves and dust near the shrine of Calyrex.

As he gazed at the mud-crafted cabbage rabbit in the shrine, D placed a few Berries in front of the shrine.

Suddenly, D realized something wasn't quite right.

He squatted down, carefully inspecting it. He eventually spotted several cracks on the side of the "Calyrex statue".

The mud statue was inherently fragile, and being exposed to the scorching sun all day led to cracking.

D: "..."

After pondering for a moment, he thought he might as well remake the Calyrex with a new statue.

So that afternoon, he gathered his tools and a piece of wood, sitting under the eaves and recalling the appearance of Calyrex while he carved the wooden sculpture.

On the expansive lawn before him, a group of Pokémon ran around boisterously.

The afternoon sunlight lay scattered across the ground.

D quietly carved the wood, occasionally looking up at Dragonite and Koraidon.

By 4:20 PM, a simple wooden sculpture of Calyrex was officially completed.

To make it long-lasting, D specifically approached Keri to have her polish it, just like making furniture.

"What's this? A huge hammer?"

Taking the oddly-shaped wooden sculpture with an oversized head from D, Keri's face was full of surprise.

D: "...Is it very ugly?"

Keri: "Not that ugly."

She noticed this "huge hammer" had a face and legs: "Could this be a Pokémon?"

"Yes." D said truthfully: "It's a Pokémon named Calyrex."

He shared the legend of Calyrex with Keri.

And told her Calyrex can help crops grow, suggesting she make a few statues to keep or sell to nearby farmers.

It was all for the cause of capturing Glastrier and Spectrier, D thought.

One person's faith is too weak, and perhaps Calyrex cannot sense it, but if more humans know Calyrex's name, maybe He will sense it all.

"Alright, alright, I'll polish and wax it for you, but it will take some time, you can pick it up tomorrow." Keri didn't take D's words to heart.

"Alright."

D acknowledged, turning away on Koraidon and leaving.

Meanwhile, in the distant Galar region, Calyrex once again sensed what His devoted follower had done.

What had He seen?!

That follower not only worshiped Him alone but was actively spreading His name to others in distant, unfamiliar lands!

"Truly a loyal follower of mine!"

At this moment, Calyrex's favor towards the little follower continually increased.

He sensed the Power of Faith accumulated within His body over this time, then gently waved His left hand, activating His power, and His eyes lit up with a glow.

Even though His power is still weak now, far from being able to make large fields fertile and bountiful, doing so for one or two is still possible.

Calyrex desired to use this method to give the little follower a hint, urging him to come to The Crown Tundra quickly in search of Him.

At midnight, when all creation had entered dreamland.

A beam representing harvest crossed distant space and fell onto an inconspicuous crop in the field.

In the next instant, the crop began changing at a speed visible to the naked eye.

It rooted and sprouted from the soil, gradually growing into a seedling, and turning into a sturdy vine.

Soon, the vine bloomed into a tender yellow flower, which slowly matured into a plump watermelon.

The transformation didn't stop there.

The watermelon grew larger and larger, occupying the land of surrounding plants, eventually becoming a colossal super watermelon, about over two meters tall.

---

---

---

---

# 149: Chapter 146: Oh My God, How Can There Be Such a Huge Watermelon?!

Early the next morning, the sky was bright as sunlight streamed through the window.

The Pawmo on the bed rubbed its eyes. Seeing it was getting late, it immediately woke up.

It turned its head to see D still sleeping, then climbed onto his chest: "Pawmo~"

"Hmm..." D groggily opened his eyes, saw Pawmo on his chest, and hugged it with a quick motion.

"Pawmo!" Pawmo exclaimed in surprise.

At that moment, the old-fashioned wall clock on the wall suddenly chimed.

D was startled awake and realized he needed to prepare breakfast for the Pokemon.

D got dressed to start the day. Pawmo also jumped down, chasing Alcremie around the room, waiting for breakfast.

D bypassed them and took ingredients from the fridge to start cooking.

This morning's breakfast was very simple, just sandwiches and MooMoo Milk as usual.

After eating, D, as usual, prepared to milk the Gogoat.

He walked out of the room and stepped outside, glancing around the ranch.

Hmm, the ranch looked thriving, and the weather was great... Hmm? What was that?!

Out of the corner of his eye, D suddenly noticed something enormous.

He turned his head and saw something unusual in the otherwise mundane field: a watermelon.

To be precise, a giant watermelon.

A group of Pokemon like Dolliv, Nacli, Gogoat, and Miltank were all attracted to it, curiously observing the gigantic watermelon.

Even the normally hardworking Combee had stopped collecting honey and was observing the giant watermelon.

What was going on?!

D stared wide-eyed and speechless at the scene.

His first reaction was that last night a fairy had blessed the ranch.

A fairy, a chance event from Stardew Valley.

Each nightfall, there was a certain chance a fairy would appear, helping players hasten the ripening of their crops.

But swiftly, D realized this wasn't the world of Stardew Valley. There were no fairies or elves here.

He stepped forward and approached to closely observe the giant watermelon.

He reached out and tapped on it, hearing a crisp sound, indicating it was fully ripe.

Looking at the watermelon, which was taller than he was, D couldn't help but fall into contemplation.

The creation of this giant crop was very likely due to Gogoat.

After eating Green Grass Cake, the crops grown using Gogoat's Grass Type Energy had a certain probability of mutating.

At present, it seemed that growing larger was indeed a mutation.

But there was still one question: How did it grow so large overnight?

D quickly brainstormed and incidentally checked the other crops nearby, discovering that only this watermelon had changed in the entire field.

First of all, it couldn't have been due to Gogoat, otherwise, why hadn't the other crops ripened, leaving just this watermelon mature?

Secondly, it couldn't possibly be because the Time God Dialga accelerated the time for this watermelon.

After all, he didn't know any Time God and had no dealings with Dialga.

The answer to the question was already incredibly clear.

Thinking of this, D turned to look at the empty shrine.

It's Calyrex!

It noticed his actions, and the early ripening of the watermelon was its reward!

Calyrex's power + Gogoat's energy = giant watermelon.

"Whoa, Calyrex really sensed it!"

D was internally greatly shocked.

After all, the Paldea Region and the Galar Region are separated by a sea.

But... why did Calyrex only help ripen one watermelon?

D was puzzled, and just then, he suddenly heard Keri's voice coming from not far away.

She was standing in front of the ranch, calling out, "Is D here? I brought you the wooden sculpture!"

Hearing the voice, D and a group of Pokemon turned to look.

Keri also noticed them and looked over in this direction.

In the next second, a look of shock appeared on her face.

D heard her say, "Oh my gosh! How can there be such a big watermelon!"

D: "..."

Keri was extremely amazed.

She came forward, staring at the watermelon in disbelief, circled around it, and gently tapped it with her hand.

"Truly a giant watermelon!"

"In fact, I'm also very shocked," D said from the side.

Immediately, Keri seemed to think of something, her eyes widening as she asked:

"Is this the power of Calyrex you mentioned yesterday?!"

D: "...Uh, probably?"

Calyrex ripened this watermelon, but its growth was due to Gogoat's power.

"I can't believe that legend turned out to be true!" Keri murmured, then her eyes gleamed: "No wonder it's the King of Bountiful Harvests; this is amazing!"

Seeing Keri's expression as if she was thinking, "Calyrex is incredible, I must also believe in it,"

D instantly didn't know whether it was him taking advantage of Calyrex or Calyrex taking advantage of him.

Oh no, he really has become a missionary for Calyrex.

After pondering for a moment, D thought: Let's consider it a reward for borrowing its horse someday! He would definitely ride its great horses fiercely, 135 Glastrier and 246 Spectrier, taking turns!

Keri handed over the polished and waxed statue.

D reached for it and "respectfully" placed the finely polished statue into the shrine, and then laid two Berries as offerings in front of the shrine.

Keri witnessed this scene from the side.

Only then did D have the chance to ask, "Why did you come over?"

Keri gestured towards the large truck outside: "I received a commission today to build a mill; I have to leave. If you can't find me at the shop, that's why I brought it over myself on the way."

So that's how it was.

D nodded.

The two turned to look at the giant watermelon in the field, and Keri asked, "What are you planning to do?"

D pondered for a moment, and suddenly, a good idea popped into his head.

Since Calyrex needed followers, why not take the opportunity to actively promote Calyrex's legend in the Paldea Region, helping it restore its power and increase its affinity?

As D thought about it, he organized his words and asked, "What do you think about me hosting a [Summer Watermelon Festival]?"

Inviting nearby farmers and some acquaintances from town to come to the ranch and gather, enjoying the giant watermelon—because he couldn't finish it by himself anyway.

By then, they would surely be shocked by how such a big watermelon could exist.

Then he could take the opportunity to tell everyone the legend of Calyrex.

Hearing this, Keri's eyes lit up, agreeing, "That sounds wonderful! I'll definitely come to join then."

"Then it's settled," D nodded with a smile, "Tomorrow! Tomorrow at 9 am, at D Ranch, see you there."

"No problem." Keri nodded with a smile, "But for now, I have to go; I'm really looking forward to tomorrow's Watermelon Festival."

After saying this, she bid farewell to D and drove off.

D glanced at the sky, internally apologizing to the people of the Galar Region.

From today, your Calyrex belongs to us in the Paldea Region!

For D, there was no psychological burden at all about taking a mythical beast from another region.

When feelings are mutual, how can it be considered cheating?

He laughed and patted the head of Koraidon beside him, then began to get busy with today's work.

Meanwhile, in the Galar Region, at The Crown Tundra.

Calyrex saw its little follower's joyous expression, with a gentle, elegant smile forming on its face.

Yet... How did it not know its powers could make a watermelon grow so large?

Looking at the watermelon that was even bigger than its two steeds, Calyrex was filled with confusion and puzzlement.

---

---

---

---

# 150: Chapter 147: He's Really... I'm Crying to Death (5k)

By the time D finished the work at the ranch, it was already nearing noon.

The giant watermelon in the ranch was truly hard to miss.

Before making lunch, D came to the field again, circling the big watermelon, pausing to observe and occasionally giving it a pat.

[Giant Watermelon: A kind of giant crop influenced by the Grass Energy of Gogoat, combined with some mysterious force.]

Reading the description of this watermelon and recalling yesterday's events, the owner of the "mysterious force" was now quite apparent.

After a moment of contemplation, D went back indoors to start making lunch.

Today's lunch was once again pan-fried meat patties and sandwiches, with Lucky Pudding, honey lemon water, small bread, and Berries to complete a meal that wasn't particularly lavish.

Though simple, it was a favorite among the Pokemon.

The thick-cut, oil-sizzling meat patties were tender and juicy, and the sandwiches with lettuce, tomatoes, and meat patties were nutritionally balanced. Even ordinary Berries could provide abundant nutrition for the Pokemon.

After the meal, drinking a cup of honey lemon water, sitting in the shadow under the eaves, enjoying the breeze while eating small bread and Berries, even the proud Glaceon showed a contented expression.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Dragonite, Pawmo, Glaceon, Alcremie, and the other Pokemon each took a cup of lemon water, sitting in a row under the eaves from large to small.

Seeing them like this, D smiled and shook his head, then sat at his desk to think about whom to invite the next day.

First, Mayor Thomas, who had always been very supportive, definitely needed an invitation.

This elderly man had been helping him since he first arrived, even gifting him a Cyclizar as an assistant.

Next were the people he knew in town.

Keri, living by the forest, the postman Allen, the Seed Shop Owner Elo and his family, the old man from Happy Ranch and his granddaughter Mei, and Zack, who came to the ranch to collect goods daily.

Lastly, the owners of nearby farms.

For example, Elif from Elif Cherry Garden, Ray from Thunder Ranch, Green from Light Green Ranch, and Mrs. Bobbi from Bobbi Farm...

D wrote a letter to each of them using the town's way of notifying about festivals, then called Dragonite and put some of the letters in its red little satchel.

"Huh?" Dragonite looked puzzled as D put the satchel around its neck.

D: "Could you please deliver these letters to Mr. Elif at Elif Cherry Garden, Mrs. Dantes in town, and Mr. Elo?"

Delivering letters, huh?

Dragonite nodded, happily accepted the task, and then skillfully flew towards the town.

Meanwhile, D contacted Allen by phone.

Since he hadn't visited the other farms except Elif Cherry Garden and didn't know their locations, he decided to leave it all to Allen for delivery.

Allen, who was working, immediately hurried over after receiving the call.

D handed the remaining letters to him and asked, "Do you have time tomorrow morning?"

"Tomorrow morning?" Allen was a bit surprised, "Why? Do you need my help with something?"

"No," D shook his head, "I just wanted to invite you to the ranch for an event. There's going to be a watermelon-eating event here at nine tomorrow morning."

"Watermelon? Where's the watermelon?"

Allen thought to himself that in this season, the watermelons should have just been planted, right?

Though they could be grown in a greenhouse, it seemed that D Ranch didn't have one yet.

Allen turned his head to look around, and the next second, he saw an enormously huge watermelon in the field.

Allen: "!!!"

He exclaimed in shock, "Is that the watermelon I know?"

D laughed, "Why don't you go and have a look?"

Sure enough, Allen went over.

He ran to the field, circled the big watermelon twice, reached out to touch it, and saw that the watermelon was still connected to the vine, not picked yet.

What does this mean?

This meant that the big watermelon was not a model; it was real!

Allen was utterly shocked, turning to D and exclaiming, "How did you grow this?"

"Do you really want to know?" D asked.

"Of course, tell me quickly!" Allen paused, looked around, and whispered, "I promise I won't tell anyone!"

"Then come with me," D led him to the statue of Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests, "It's all this Pokemon's blessing."

Looking at the big-headed Pokemon in the shrine, Allen couldn't help but ask, "What Pokemon is that?"

"Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests," explained D concisely, introducing the legend of Calyrex to Allen.

Although half of the credit belonged to Gogoat, as long as he continued to feed Calyrex the Green Grass Cake, Calyrex could do it himself.

After all, it was a Grass Type Pokemon too.

Under Allen's astonished gaze, D added:

"If you're growing anything at home, you can also trust in Calyrex or, like me, set up a statue to worship Him. He will bless your crops with a bountiful harvest."

Allen swallowed hard, nodding, "I got it." This world indeed had such incredible Pokemon!

"Where did you buy your statue?" Allen quickly asked.

He decided! He would buy one to take home!

His grandmother particularly loved to plant things, and their yard was already full of Berry trees and a garden.

---

"Uh... I made this myself." D thought for a moment, then added, "Perhaps after a while, you can buy it at Keri's shop."

He plans to collaborate with Keri later to see if they can mass-produce the statues of Calyrex. He will provide the design, and Keri will handle the wood carving, then polish and sell them to nearby farmers.

Allen nodded: "I'll go check it out later!"

Since he still had work to do, he didn't stay long at the ranch. After taking the letter from D, Allen turned and left.

He had to deliver these letters before dark.

After Allen left, D used a video phone to contact Mayor Thomas, inviting him to come over tomorrow for an event.

Mayor Thomas gladly accepted and said he would surely attend.

Seeing this, D felt relieved.

After finishing all the tasks, it was already four forty in the afternoon.

Looking at his ranch and the grassland around it.

D thought, "So next, there's just the matter of setting up the venue."

The tables and chairs at home weren't enough. He couldn't have everyone sitting on the grass munching on watermelons; that would be too impolite.

D was worried, as he couldn't possibly gather so many tables and chairs in such a short time.

Just then, the sound of a truck engine came from outside the ranch.

D turned his head and saw Keri driving a truck to the ranch.

Right behind her was Allen riding Cyclizar.

Keri parked the truck in the ranch and directed her Machamp partner to unload the items from the vehicle.

D walked up and was surprised to find that they were all items for setting up the event venue.

Tables, chairs, benches, celebration balloons...

D was taken aback, a bit puzzled: "What's all this?"

Allen came up beside him, patted him on the shoulder, and watched Keri direct Machamp to move things. He smiled and explained:

"Isn't this your first time hosting an event? Keri and I thought that since you're new here, you wouldn't have experience or enough tables, so we came to help you set up the venue. How about that?"

"This is a huge help!" D was grateful.

Keri walked over with a smile: "I'm charging for these, you know!"

D: "...How much?"

"Five watermelons!" Keri held out her hand, counting on her fingers: "Two for each Machamp, one for each Conkeldurr, and one for me."

D immediately understood her point and smiled: "Alright, plenty guaranteed!"

Keri: "Then let's get busy!"

So D, along with Koraidon and Cyclizar, began helping move the tables and chairs.

Allen and his Cyclizar joined in to help too.

Glaceon, Alcremie, Pawmo, and Butterfree were blowing up balloons on the side.

Keri coordinated everything.

Everyone and the Pokemon worked together to set up the venue, arranging tables and chairs and decorating the ranch with ribbons, hanging the inflated balloons on the D Ranch sign.

At five in the evening, Zack came to pick up the goods.

He looked at the scene, amazed: "Wow, it's really lively here!"

Then, he noticed the gigantic watermelon.

Zack's reaction was different from Keri and Allen's. After a brief shock, he muttered:

"Such a big watermelon, I wonder how much it would sell for at the market?"

Allen chuckled: "Uncle Zack, a family of three can't finish such a big watermelon!"

Zack was taken aback, then agreed: "That's true."

He looked at D and laughed heartily: "In that case, save a spot for me tomorrow!"

"Don't worry!" D nodded.

In the evening, D invited Keri and Allen to have dinner at the ranch.

The three of them, along with a group of Pokemon, enjoyed a barbecue under the starry sky, filled with the atmosphere of fireworks.

After eating and drinking, the sky was completely dark.

Allen watched the fireflies gathering by the river and couldn't help but exclaim: "Nature is truly beautiful!"

D helped Dragonite massage its full belly and said: "You could come to the countryside to run a ranch."

Allen shook his head: "Better not. I don't have as many Pokemon to help me work, I'd probably end up exhausted."

D: "It's not that exaggerated, is it?"

Hearing this, Allen, who was lying on the grass from eating too much, immediately sat up, as if remembering something.

"Honestly, this is the first time I've seen a ranch develop so quickly. It's only been a few months, and it's completely transformed."

Beside him, Keri nodded in agreement: "Do you remember Ray from Thunder Ranch?"

"I know him." D had met him.

Keri: "His ranch is now about the same as yours, but it took him almost two years to get it to this point, while you've only taken less than four months!"

D thought for a moment and speculated: "Maybe it's because I can catch Pokemon myself?"

He had caught the Gogoat and brought Nacli back from the wild. These not only saved him a lot of money but also provided a significant source of income.

"That's what I mean!" Allen lay back down: "Normal people don't have powerful Pokemon like Koraidon to help them, so running a ranch does take a lot of time."

D understood. He looked at Koraidon and reached out to pat its head.

After a while, Keri said: "It's getting late, we should head back."

---

D stood up to see them off; as the truck departed, the ranch returned to its peaceful state.

After a busy day, D felt a bit tired too, so he went to bed early, preparing to welcome tomorrow's event in his best state.

The next day.

D got up early. After he and his Pokémon finished breakfast, the people who received invitations yesterday also began arriving at the ranch.

The first to arrive was Mayor Thomas.

He wore a formal suit today, showing how much he valued today's event.

D welcomed him in, and when Mayor Thomas saw the giant watermelon, he rubbed his eyes in disbelief.

"My God! I swear, this is the first time in my life I've seen such a big watermelon!"

D smiled beside him and said, "You'll get used to it over time."

Then, he told Mayor Thomas about today's preparations and invited him to cut the giant watermelon.

Mayor Thomas happily agreed.

As the start time of the event approached, D planned to let Mayor Thomas sit and rest for a while.

Apart from the watermelon, he also prepared many snacks and treats.

Lucky Pudding, Berry Milk, Honey Bun, Creamy Smoothie...

But to his surprise, Mayor Thomas refused.

He acted like an elder from his own family, helping D welcome guests from the town.

With Mayor Thomas's arrival, it was like a switch was turned on, and people arrived at the ranch in groups of two or three.

The elder from Happy Ranch and his granddaughter Mei, Seed Shop Owner Elo and his wife Abby.

D didn't see their daughter Bell; she must be lounging in her room now.

Then came Ray from Thunder Ranch, Green from Light Green Ranch, the nearby farm owners.

The Elif family from Elif Cherry Garden, Allen riding a Cyclizar, Keri bringing Machamp and Conkeldurr, muscular Zack with a crew cut.

The last to arrive was the kindly looking, gray-haired Mrs. Dantes and her husband.

D saw this amiable elderly lady walk up to him carrying a cloth bag.

Mrs. Dantes beamed and handed over a bag of cookies: "These are little cookies I made this morning. Want to try?"

"Thank you very much." D smiled as he accepted the bag, appreciating Mrs. Dantes's gesture.

This lovable elderly lady often liked making small cookies and snacks to give to Dragonite, who would bring them back to the ranch.

Seeing that almost everyone had arrived, D had Dragonite and Koraidon work together to pick that giant watermelon and carry it over to the lawn.

"Agaaah~"

"Roar~"

The two Pokémon happily obliged. They flew to the farmland and brought back the giant watermelon.

Everyone's attention was drawn to the giant watermelon; they gathered around it, astonished expressions on their faces.

Mrs. Dantes covered her mouth in shock: "Oh my! This is simply unbelievable!"

Ray from Thunder Ranch was equally astonished: "Wha-what a huge watermelon! How did you grow it?"

The elder from Happy Ranch held his granddaughter Mei's hand, speechless at the sight of the giant watermelon.

Mei's eyes were bright, full of admiration: "Mr. D is amazing!"

Elo couldn't help but ask with a trembling voice, "Are those really the watermelon seeds sold from our store?"

"Absolutely, Mr. Elo." D's voice came from the side.

Everyone turned to look at the young man who grew the incredible giant watermelon.

Ray: "How did you do it?"

Looking at everyone's eyes filled with curiosity and amazement, D felt as if he had become a missionary, dressed in robes, holding a Bible, saying to the people "Believe in my Lord for eternal life."

Though awkward, for the sake of the two fine horses, he had to continue.

D cleared his throat and, under the gaze of the crowd, slowly began:

"It was all a blessing from the King of Bountiful Harvests."

"The King of Bountiful Harvests?" As soon as he finished speaking, someone immediately responded.

D looked over and found it was Mrs. Green from Light Green Ranch.

He nodded: "The legendary King of Bountiful Harvests, Calyrex, is the embodiment of abundance."

Next, D narrated the legend of Calyrex, focusing on its abilities to make flowers bloom, enrich the soil, and ensure bountiful crops.

"Calyrex is a Pokémon full of love and kindness, able to see everything from past, present, to future, bringing good fortune to those who believe in it."

It's no good, the more he spoke, the more he felt like a fraud... D internally complained.

He lightly coughed, then changed the topic: "Mayor Thomas, let's start cutting the melon!"

Before the crowd could react, Mayor Thomas had already, with a smile, walked onto the high platform.

Holding a watermelon knife, he cut the watermelon while saying, "Let's thank Calyrex for allowing us to enjoy delicious watermelon in this season."

————

The Galar Region, The Crown Tundra.

Suddenly, Calyrex felt a warm power surging within its body.

This feeling was very familiar; long, long ago, it had felt this way before.

It was the power transformed by human faith in it.

Feeling the warmth spreading inside, Calyrex flickered, and then appeared atop an endless, white snowfield.

Its toes gently touched the snow, eyes glowing with blue Psychic light.

Through the distant time and space, it saw a group of humans gathered, seemingly celebrating something, creating a lively atmosphere around them.

And its ever-dedicated little believer stood in front of the crowd, doing his utmost to proclaim its existence.

"...The great King of Bountiful Harvests."

"The embodiment of abundance... Pokémon full of love and kindness..."

"Let's thank Calyrex..."

Hearing these words, seeing that scene, Calyrex was deeply touched.

In its long, long life, this was the first time a human had done such a thing for it.

It couldn't say it wasn't moved.

Calyrex: "Truly unexpected, that little believer trusts me so much."

He was indeed very devout.

---

---
